### [1] [Inflation from Covariant Signature Change: A Geometric Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01274)
*Raghvendra Singh,Sergey Bondarenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协变机制，通过光滑改变度规符号（从欧几里得到洛伦兹），驱动有限时段的加速膨胀。该转变由标量插值器沿类时弦实现，在余维一超曲面上度规退化但曲率不变量有限（曲率正则）。爱因斯坦张量被重写为局域化有效源，在洛伦兹分支中导出了与模型无关的加速判据：加速度持续当插值器斜率超过由外曲率和空间里奇曲率确定的临界值，并在首次达到该值时结束。标准光滑剖面（tanh、广义逻辑斯蒂、幂律/反正切）给出了加速时段原时持续时间的闭式表达式。该构造提供了一条从正则欧几里得起源到早期洛伦兹加速膨胀的无奇点、无暴涨子路径，与无边界型边界条件兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暴涨模型依赖标量场（暴涨子）驱动早期宇宙加速膨胀，但其初始条件（如奇点问题、精细调节）及与量子引力边界条件（如无边界提议）的兼容性仍存疑。本文旨在发展一种纯几何机制，通过度规符号的平滑转变（从欧几里得到洛伦兹）产生有限时段的加速膨胀，无需引入暴涨子，为宇宙起源提供另一种无奇点描述。

Method: 1. 构建协变框架：引入标量插值器沿类时弦参数化度规符号转变，度规在转变超曲面退化但保持曲率正则；2. 将爱因斯坦张量分解为局域化有效源项，归因于插值器动力学；3. 导出洛伦兹分支中加速膨胀的判据（斜率-曲率不等式）；4. 选取四种标准光滑剖面（tanh、广义逻辑斯蒂、幂律、反正切）计算加速时段原时持续期的闭式表达式。

Result: 1. 得到了加速膨胀的模型无关局部判据：加速度存在当且仅当插值器斜率超过由外曲率和空间里奇曲率决定的临界值；2. 四种标准剖面均给出加速持续期的解析表达式，证明剖面形状直接控制加速时段长度；3. 证明该机制在转变超曲面上曲率不变量有限（无奇点），且与无边界型边界条件兼容。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种无需暴涨子的早期宇宙加速膨胀几何起源机制，通过度规符号的平滑转变自然产生有限时段的暴胀阶段。其优点包括：无奇点、与量子引力边界条件兼容、加速终止条件由几何量决定而非精细调节。未来方向包括将该机制嵌入完全量子引力框架及检验其可观测后果。

Abstract: We present a covariant mechanism in which a smooth change of metric signature, from a Euclidean to a Lorentzian regime, drives a finite interval of accelerated expansion. The transition, encoded by a scalar interpolator along a timelike congruence, occurs on a codimension-one hypersurface where the continued metric is degenerate but curvature invariants remain finite, so the surface is curvature-regular. Using this covariant continuation, we rewrite the Einstein tensor of the continued metric as a localized, interpolator-dependent effective source for the post-transition Lorentzian branch, yielding a purely geometric stress tensor supported near the crossing. In the Lorentzian regime, we derive a model-independent, local criterion for acceleration: inflation persists while the interpolator's slope exceeds a critical value fixed by the extrinsic curvature and the spatial Ricci curvature on the initial hypersurface, and ends when this inequality is first saturated. Standard smooth profiles (tanh, generalized logistic, and power-law/arctan) admit closed-form expressions for the proper-time duration of the accelerated epoch, showing that, for fixed geometric data, the profile shape controls this duration. The construction provides a non-singular, inflaton-free route from a regular Euclidean origin to an early Lorentzian phase of accelerated expansion, in a manner compatible with no--boundary--type boundary conditions.

</details>


### [2] [Hypersurface Anchored Variational Principle for General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01285)
*Prasanta Sahoo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义相对论的超曲面锚定变分延拓，通过在爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量中添加一个微分同胚不变的超曲面泛函，并独立变化超曲面的嵌入，得到了包含局域分布贡献的爱因斯坦方程和确定可容许嵌入的锚定方程。该方法不引入额外传播自由度，锚定条件不等同于规范选择，而是真正的变分约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在不引入额外传播自由度的情况下，通过变分方法对广义相对论中的嵌入超曲面施加物理约束，从而构造经典解空间中的一个受限扇区。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量基础上附加一个微分同胚不变的超曲面泛函，将超曲面嵌入作为独立变量与时空度量一同变分，导出欧拉-拉格朗日方程组。

Result: 体区域的爱因斯坦方程保持二阶主部结构且不引入额外传播自由度；在椭球性和可逆性假设下，锚定超曲面具有局部持久性和线性稳定性；锚定条件与局部切片规范条件不等价；哈密顿约束获得超曲面支撑项；正则生成元在弱意义下闭合，狄拉克代数在体区域恢复，偏差局域化在表面贡献中。

Conclusion: 该构造定义了广义相对论经典解空间中的一个受限扇区，其时空允许至少一个满足锚定方程的嵌入超曲面；在均匀宇宙学中，锚定表面两侧可产生膨胀率的有限跃变，同时保持标准演化。

Abstract: A hypersurface anchored variational extension of General Relativity is formulated in which the Einstein-Hilbert action is supplemented by a diffeomorphism invariant functional supported on an embedded spacelike hypersurface whose embedding is varied independently of the spacetime metric. The resulting Euler-Lagrange system consists of the Einstein equations with a localized distributional contribution together with an anchoring equation determining admissible embeddings. For the admissible class of hypersurface functionals considered here, the bulk field equations retain the standard second order principal structure away from the hypersurface and no additional propagating bulk gravitational degrees of freedom are introduced. Under ellipticity and invertibility assumptions, local persistence and linear stability of anchoring hypersurfaces follow from standard implicit function and elliptic estimates. The anchoring condition is generically inequivalent to local slicing gauge conditions and therefore defines a genuine variational restriction rather than a coordinate choice. In the canonical formulation, the momentum constraints retain their standard form, whereas the Hamiltonian constraint acquires a hypersurface supported term. The corresponding smeared generators close in the weak sense: the Dirac algebra is recovered in the bulk, with deviations confined to localized distributional surface contributions. The construction therefore defines a constrained sector of the classical solution space of General Relativity consisting of spacetimes that admit at least one embedded hypersurface satisfying the anchoring equation. In homogeneous cosmology, the localized term induces matching conditions across the anchoring surface, allowing finite transitions in the expansion rate while preserving standard evolution away from the transition hypersurface.

</details>


### [3] [Impact of Spacecraft Orbit Uncertainties and Velocity Mismodeling on the LISA Gravitational-Wave Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01318)
*Lorenzo Speri,Olaf Hartwig,Waldemar Martens,Oliver Jennrich,Eric Joffre,Michele Armano,Martin Hewitson,Nora Lützgendorf*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了LISA引力波探测器中航天器轨道不确定性及速度忽略对探测器响应和参数估计的影响，发现轨道不确定性在高频段影响较小（失配度低于10^{-7}），而忽略航天器速度在10^{-4} Hz附近影响显著（失配度约10^{-4}），但对典型银河系双星系统的参数估计偏差小于1-σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA数据研究中通常假设航天器轨道完全已知，但实际轨道确定过程会引入位置和速度的不确定性，这些不确定性的传播效应尚未被充分表征。

Method: 利用失配度量化轨道不确定性和忽略航天器速度对LISA响应知识的影响，并通过银河系双星系统参数估计案例评估实际偏差。

Result: 轨道不确定性在高频段影响极小（失配度低于10^{-7}）；忽略航天器速度在10^{-4} Hz附近失配度达10^{-4}量级；对于频率10^{-4} Hz、信噪比200、观测一年的银河系双星，忽略速度导致的参数估计偏差小于1-σ。

Conclusion: 首次完整表征了从引力波应变到探测器输出再到参数估计的LISA响应误差传播链，为未来LISA数据分析中的误差控制提供了定量依据。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is a space-based gravitational wave observatory that consists of three spacecraft in a near-equilateral triangular formation. The spacecraft orbits are typically assumed to be perfectly known in LISA data analysis studies, but in reality, the orbit determination process introduces uncertainties in the spacecraft positions and velocities. In this work, we investigate how these uncertainties propagate into the LISA detector output and the impact of neglecting the spacecraft velocities. We quantify these errors in the knowledge of the LISA response using mismatches and discuss the implications for gravitational wave data analysis. We find that spacecraft orbit uncertainties impact the LISA response knowledge at high frequencies with worst mismatch below $10^{-7}$. The effect of neglecting the spacecraft velocities is largest at frequencies around $10^{-4}$ Hz with mismatches of order $10^{-4}$. For a galactic binary with frequency $10^{-4}$ Hz and SNR=200 observed for one year, we find that neglecting the spacecraft velocities in the response leads to less than 1-$σ$ biases in the parameter estimates. This work provides the first characterization of how errors in the LISA gravitational wave response propagate from gravitational wave strain through detector output to estimated parameters.

</details>

*Daniel Gálvez-García,Nora Bretón*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在极强电磁场下真空折射率改变导致光速小于c时，超相对论带电粒子可产生切伦科夫辐射的现象，比较了欧拉-海森堡理论在临界和超临界磁场下的切伦科夫辐射，并对比了切伦科夫辐射与同步辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在极强电磁场（临界和超临界磁场）环境下真空折射率变化引发的切伦科夫辐射特性，并对比其与同步辐射的差异。

Method: 基于欧拉-海森堡有效场论，分析临界和超临界磁场条件下真空中的切伦科夫辐射，并与同步辐射进行理论比较。

Result: 给出了欧拉-海森堡理论在临界和超临界磁场下切伦科夫辐射的具体对比结果，以及切伦科夫辐射与同步辐射的差异。

Conclusion: 强电磁场下真空折射率改变可导致切伦科夫辐射，且不同磁场强度下辐射特性不同，与同步辐射存在显著差异。

Abstract: In the presence of very intense electromagnetic fields, the refractive index of vacuum is modified such that light velocity is less than $c$ and ultrarelativistic charged particles can be faster than light and can induce Cherenkov radiation. We present the comparison of the Cherenkov radiation produced by the Euler-Heisenberg theory for critical and supercritical magnetic fields. We also make the comparison between the Cherenkov and the synchrotron radiation produced by the charged particles.

</details>


### [5] [Stability regions of glued wormholes with massless Kim-Lee backreacted spacetimes as interior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01687)
*G. F. Akhtaryanova,R. Kh. Karimov,R. N. Izmailov,A. Bhattacharya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了零ADM质量虫洞（如Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞）通过与外部大质量时空（Schwarzschild真空和Reissner-Nordström时空）通过薄壳粘合形成可穿越粘合虫洞的稳定性问题。利用Garcia、Lobo和Visser提出的薄壳"质量"和"外力"约束概念，分析了两种内部Kim-Lee时空（标量荷和电荷）粘合虫洞的稳定性区域，发现两者稳定性区域几乎相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零ADM质量虫洞在银河系微引力透镜等天体物理应用中有重要价值，但单独存在时对小扰动不稳定。作者希望探索通过将零质量内部与观测相关的大质量外部时空粘合，使内部保持零质量的同时获得整体稳定性。

Method: 采用Garcia、Lobo和Visser提出的薄壳"质量"和"外力"约束概念，通过球形对称薄壳将零ADM质量的Kim-Lee虫洞内部与外部Schwarzschild真空和Reissner-Nordström时空分别粘合，分析薄壳动力学来确定粘合虫洞的稳定性区域。

Result: 两种情况下（内部为标量荷Kim-Lee虫洞和电荷Kim-Lee虫洞）的稳定性区域几乎相似，尽管两种内部时空物理性质截然不同。详细给出了粘合虫洞能够实现稳定的条件。

Conclusion: 零ADM质量内部虫洞通过与外部大质量时空粘合可以克服单独存在的不稳定性，且稳定性区域对内部虫洞的荷类型（标量或电荷）不敏感，表明这种粘合机制具有普适性。

Abstract: Asymptotic zero Arnowitt-Deser-Misner (ADM) mass wormholes, such as the zero-mass traversable Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole, are of great interest for astrophysical applications such as in the galactic microlensing. However, when considered individually, they are unstable to small perturbations. On the other hand, there is a possibility that they can be stable as an interior partner of a traversable glued wormhole obtained by suitably gluing the interior to the observationally relevant massive exterior spacetimes across spherically symmetric thin shells. Although the exterior spacetime has non-zero ADM mass, massless interior partner remains massless sharing the stability of the glued wormhole. The dynamics of the thin-shell then demarcates the stability regions of the glued wormhole that we wish to study here by employing the novel concepts of thin-shell "mass" and of "external force" constraints discovered by Garcia, Lobo and Visser. We shall consider two classes, where the zero ADM mass interior are Kim-Lee wormholes glued to the exterior Schwarzschild vacuum and Reissner-Nordström spacetime respectively. It turns out that the stability regions in both cases are almost similar although the two interior Kim-Lee spacetimes are physically very different, one scalar charged and the other electrically charged. The conditions under which the stability of glued wormholes could be achieved are analyzed in detail.

</details>

*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,Sardor Murodov,Bekzod Rahmatov,Javlon Rayimbaev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kalb-Ramond引力中带电Reissner-Nordström-like黑洞周围的带电测试粒子动力学与准周期振荡，发现黑洞电荷、KR参数ℓ、粒子电荷比ε和磁耦合β共同影响ISCO和QPO频率，并通过MCMC分析约束了模型参数，表明QPO观测可作为探测Lorentz破缺黑洞几何的有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨Lorentz破缺引力（Kalb-Ramond背景）中带电黑洞周围粒子运动与QPO特征，并利用实测QPO数据约束模型参数，检验该引力理论在强引力环境中的可观测效应。

Method: 在Kalb-Ramond引力背景中构造外部磁场（基于无源Maxwell方程），推导带电测试粒子的运动方程、有效势、圆轨道条件和轨道/径向epicyclic频率，并应用于相对论进动模型；采用MCMC方法对GRO J1655-40、XTE J1550-564和M82 X-1的QPO数据进行参数约束。

Result: Q/M、ℓ、ε和β共同影响ISCO位置和QPO频率；后验约束表明带电KR黑洞模型能在选定参数范围内一致再现观测QPO对。

Conclusion: QPO观测可作为探测Lorentz破缺黑洞几何与强引力电磁效应的有效现象学工具。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged test particles and quasi-periodic oscillations around a Reissner--Nordström-like black hole in Kalb--Ramond (KR) gravity in the presence of an external magnetic test field. The KR background introduces a Lorentz-violating parameter $\ell$, which modifies the spacetime geometry, horizon structure, circular orbits, and characteristic frequencies of particle motion. In contrast to the standard Wald-type prescription, the magnetic-field configuration is constructed from the source-free Maxwell equation on the charged KR background, allowing the magnetic profile to be consistently adapted to the modified geometry. We derive the equations of motion, the effective potential, the conditions for circular orbits, and the orbital and radial epicyclic frequencies of charged particles. The results show that the black-hole charge $Q/M$, the KR parameter $\ell$, the specific particle charge $ε$, and the magnetic coupling $β=bM$ jointly affect the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and the quasi-periodic oscillation (QPO) frequencies. We then apply the obtained frequencies to the relativistic precession model, where the upper QPO frequency is identified with the orbital frequency and the lower one with the periastron-precession frequency. Using the observed twin-peak QPO data of GRO J1655--40, XTE J1550--564, and M82 X-1, we perform a Markov chain Monte Carlo analysis to constrain the model parameters. The obtained posterior constraints indicate that the charged KR black-hole model with an external magnetic field can consistently reproduce the observed QPO pairs within the adopted parameter ranges. These findings suggest that QPO observations may serve as a useful phenomenological tool for probing Lorentz-violating black-hole geometries and electromagnetic effects in strong-gravity environments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在经典 Einstein 引力与圈量子宇宙学框架下，分别考虑超流暗物质（广义三次状态方程）与标准无压流体两种暗物质模型，以及广义非线性状态方程与常数状态方程两种暗能量模型，引入两种相互作用项 $Q=α\dotρ_m$ 和 $Q=β\dotρ_d$，通过动力学系统相空间分析研究临界点及其稳定性。

Conclusion: 暗物质状态方程性质与量子引力修正均对相互作用暗区宇宙学的渐近演化有显著影响，圈量子宇宙学效应从根本上改变了系统的全局动力学结构。

Abstract: We study the cosmological dynamics of interacting dark energy and dark matter in Classical Einstein Gravity and Loop Quantum Cosmology. Two dark matter scenarios are considered: superfluid dark matter described by a generalized cubic equation of state and the standard pressureless fluid. The dark energy component is modeled using both a generalized nonlinear equation of state and a constant equation of state. We examine two phenomenological interaction terms, $Q=α\dotρ_m$ and $Q=β\dotρ_d$, which govern the energy transfer between the dark sectors. In classical gravity, the pressureless matter model exhibits stable late-time attractors, whereas the superfluid dark matter model admits only saddle and non-hyperbolic critical points. Extending the analysis to Loop Quantum Cosmology, quantum geometric corrections replace the Big Bang singularity with a nonsingular quantum bounce, and significantly modify the phase-space dynamics. As a result, the stable attractors of the classical pressureless matter model disappear, and all interacting models possess only saddle and non-hyperbolic critical points. These findings highlight the significant influence of both dark matter properties and quantum gravitational effects on the asymptotic evolution of interacting dark-sectors.

</details>

*Yu-Qi Chen,Jin-Yang Shen,Hai-Shan Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在五维爱因斯坦-蜜蜂引力理论中构建了等角动量旋转黑洞，通过Wald形式主义和Komar积分两种方法研究其热力学性质，发现两者计算的守恒量存在仅由蜜蜂耦合常数决定的差异，并应用Kerr/CFT对应发现微观熵精确匹配Komar积分版本的熵而非Wald熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

We consider two observers (Alice and Rob) initially sharing a maximally entangled Bell state: Alice freely falls into the black hole (inertial Kruskal frame), while Rob accelerates outside the horizon (non-inertial Schwarzschild-like frame). The Unruh-Hawking effect modifies Rob's field modes, requiring Bogoliubov transformations to relate the two frames. We derive the mixed bipartite density matrix for fermionic fields and analytical expressions for NAQC and BN, which depend on Hawking temperature (itself governed by $α$, $d$, and $r_h$). Our results show both correlations degrade monotonically with increasing Hawking temperature, confirm the NAQC-BN hierarchical relationship persists in EGB spacetime, and highlighting the impact of high curvature corrections on quantum resources.

Abstract: Recently, a distinct non--quasinormal mode component of black hole binary merger radiation, named the direct wave, has been identified. The frequency and damping time of the direct wave have been associated with properties of the remnant horizon. This has led to direct-wave based analysis of GW250114, including a test of Hawking's area law. However, as we demonstrate here using numerical relativity strain data, the direct wave frequency is not correlated with the horizon frequency or surface gravity, other than an incidental crossing around $χ_f \approx 0.7$ corresponding closely to the remnant spin of GW250114. We show that while the instantaneous frequency of the direct wave is quasi-stable, the damping time shows significant evolution and therefore a single damped sinusoid model, containing a fixed damping time, is not appropriate. We further show that an evolving frequency model based on horizon properties also does not model the direct wave component for systems with large remnant spins. We demonstrate that testing Hawking's area law with a horizon frequency based on the direct wave interpretation will lead to apparent violations of Hawking's area law when no violation actually occurs. Our results therefore indicate that the direct wave is not a reliable probe of the remnant horizon's properties.

Abstract: We construct a perturbative scalar-tensor solution describing a central inhomogeneity embedded in an evolving cosmological background, with the aim of studying black hole persistence through a nonsingular bounce. Scalar-tensor gravity provides a natural framework for realizing bouncing cosmologies, while the inclusion of a localized inhomogeneity makes the field equations substantially more difficult to solve. We therefore adopt a perturbative scheme, with perturbative parameter $ε$, in which the leading-order equations are solved by a spatially flat bouncing FLRW spacetime sourced by a radiation perfect fluid. At next order, a central inhomogeneity is introduced through a generalized McVittie geometry, with the perturbations encoded in the corresponding first-order metric and scalar-field functions. We first allow an anisotropic fluid with radial and tangential pressures, whose diagonal components solve the diagonal field equations. The field equations are solved as a series expansion up to $\mathcal{O}(η^4)$ near the bounce at $η=0$. The resulting perfect fluid solution contains three arbitrary functions which are constrained by requiring the spacetime to asymptote to FLRW as $r\to\infty$. With suitable initial conditions preserving the parabolic structure of the bounce, the integration constant $d_0$ emerges as the true perturbative parameter: all perturbations vanish as $d_0\to0$. Finally, we find a small evolving horizon, $r_h\sim d_0$, which we interpret as the horizon of the central inhomogeneity. Its persistence through the bounce supports the interpretation of a black hole surviving the cosmological transition, and its evolution is not symmetric about $η=0$.

</details>

### [16] [An Optimisation Framework for the Well-Conditioned Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02194)
*Joseph Webb,Sadok Jerad,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DSGNAR是一个双草图高斯-牛顿自适应比优化框架，通过解决PINNs中严重病态的损失景观问题，实现了前所未有的精度和速度，在多种PDE问题上将相对ℓ2误差降至3×10⁻¹⁶，并在Burgers方程和高维泊松问题上分别提升了5个和8个数量级的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络在求解偏微分方程时因损失景观严重病态导致优化困难，难以达到传统求解器的精度，迫切需要一种能有效应对病态条件的优化方法。

Method: 提出DSGNAR框架，核心包括：(1) 双草图高斯-牛顿模型降低计算复杂度；(2) 自适应比策略精确控制正则化和步长，从而有效应对损失景观的病态性。

Result: 在非线性、混沌、多尺度、高维和Navier-Stokes等PDE问题上，DSGNAR实现了相对ℓ2误差低至3×10⁻¹⁶；在Burgers方程上提升精度5个数量级；在高维泊松问题上提升8个数量级；单精度下Burgers方程在10秒内达到ℓ2_rel = 4.75×10⁻⁷；对架构、精度和超参数选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DSGNAR通过二阶优化方法有效克服了PINNs的病态损失景观问题，在精度和速度上显著超越现有方法，为PINNs达到传统求解器精度提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising route to solve partial differential equations, yet they have struggled to reach the precision of classical solvers. The obstacle is increasingly understood to be one of optimisation, owing to the severely ill-conditioned loss landscape. We present $\textbf{DSGNAR}$: Doubly-Sketched Gauss-Newton with Adaptive Ratio, a scalable second-order optimisation framework that confronts this ill-conditioning and, in doing so, obtains unprecedented accuracy and speed. $\textbf{DSGNAR}$ couples a doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton model with a novel strategy that carefully controls both regularisation and step length. Across a suite of problems spanning nonlinear, chaotic, multi-scale, high-dimensional, and Navier-Stokes, the framework greatly improves on the state of the art: able to attain relative $\ell_2$ errors as low as $3\times10^{-16}$ in double precision, improve contemporary results by five orders of magnitude on the canonical Burgers' equation, and as much as eight orders on a high-dimensional Poisson problem, while remaining markedly faster. We further show that, in single precision, solutions at the limit of round-off error can be obtained very quickly: Burgers' equation to $\ell_2^{\text{rel}} = 4.75 \times 10^{-7}$ in under ten seconds. The framework is also robust to the choice of architecture, arithmetic precision, and initial hyperparameters.
  The code is available at https://www.github.com/wephy/physics-informed-neural-networks

</details>


### [17] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Muon、SOAP和SOAP-Muon等矩阵结构优化器与Adam在训练NequIP和Allegro MLIP模型上的表现，发现这些优化器在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，其中SOAP和SOAP-Muon表现最为稳健且优秀，尤其在部分力监督条件下提升更为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP领域的新架构和数据集研究已广泛开展，但优化器选择长期被忽视，默认使用Adam及其变体，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 实现并系统比较了Muon、SOAP及混合SOAP-Muon三种矩阵结构优化器，在NequIP和Allegro两种MLIP模型上进行训练对比，评估收敛速度和最终精度。

Result: SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam；Muon仅提供部分改进；在部分力监督条件下优化器带来的性能提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但影响显著的设计维度，矩阵结构优化器有望成为更优的默认选择。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [18] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络（M-QCDNet），将认知诊断模型的结构可解释性与深度学习相结合，通过Q矩阵作为结构先验确保潜在掌握特征的可解释性，并设计了带L2惩罚的损失函数来平衡预测性能和结构对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型（CDMs）具有结构可解释性但表征能力有限，深度神经网络（NN）预测能力强但缺乏认知理论层面的可解释性，二者难以兼顾。M-QCDNet旨在融合两者的优势，实现既保持预测性能又确保诊断结果符合认知理论结构。

Method: 1) 多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络结构，将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入网络，确保潜在掌握特征的可解释性；2) 设计带L2惩罚的损失函数，对与Q矩阵不对齐的技能进行惩罚；3) 开发基于可解释对齐的评估指标，量化预测技能激活与项目级技能之间的对应程度。

Result: 通过可解释对齐指标验证了模型在预测性能与结构对齐之间的平衡，能够量化预测技能激活与Q矩阵定义的项目技能之间的关系，实现了认知诊断的结构透明性和神经网络的灵活性。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet通过将诊断有效性嵌入模型设计，弥合了心理测量透明性与神经灵活性之间的鸿沟，为课堂实践提供了早期学习困难检测和掌握导向干预的实用工具，推动了解释性、公平性和可操作性的AI认知诊断发展。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [19] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IIRMAnet的脑电应激检测方法，通过构建频点级空间协方差矩阵、频率聚类聚合和切片间注意力机制，在三个数据集上以1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs实现了最高82.78%的平衡准确率，优于五种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试脑电应激检测仍具挑战性，因为判别性应激模式兼具被试依赖性和频率特异性。传统黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了高频认知状态解码关键神经振荡；而标准时间tokenization常破坏切片段间时间连贯性。

Method: 1) 在每个频点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道几何与频率特异性判别信息；2) 频率聚类聚合，选择信息量大的频谱分量形成与脑电节律对齐的紧凑数据驱动频率簇；3) 切片内-间注意力模块，自适应整合局部切片级频谱动态与全局时间上下文。

Result: 在三个数据集上，IIRMAnet一致优于五种SOTA基线，最高达到82.78%平衡准确率，模型仅1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过频域黎曼建模与注意力机制有效解决了跨被试脑电应激检测中的频率特异性和被试依赖性问题，以轻量级架构实现了优越性能。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [20] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究编程-by-示例系统中的对抗性鲁棒性，定义了一种新的失败模式——攻击者故意选择最坏的示例来破坏合成程序，并提出版本空间分区聚合(VPA)作为防御手段，但发现其在现实任务中效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PBE系统假设错误示例来自随机噪声，忽视了攻击者可以针对性选择最坏示例进行破坏的情况。本文旨在形式化并研究这种对抗性失败模式。

Method: 形式化固定集最坏情况损坏框架；实现精确有界池和启发式损坏搜索（针对字符串转换DSL）；提出VPA防御方法（在不相交示例组上合成并通过语义签名投票）；在多种基准上评估（SyGuS PBE_SLIA、SYNTRA Playgol v2等）。

Result: 低裕度PBE任务存在对抗鲁棒性维度，随机噪声评估无法捕捉；VPA仅在干净语义保持分区投票裕度时有效，而这在现实任务中常失败；一个精心设计的编辑即可翻转8个尖峰任务，而随机控制仅成功10.3%-16.7%；在公开SyGuS上投票裕度接近1，自适应攻击者使VPA精度降至零。

Conclusion: PBE系统的对抗鲁棒性是一个真实且被忽视的问题；VPA是一种有条件的防御手段，但远非通用解决方案；需要进一步研究对抗性设置下的PBE鲁棒性。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [21] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于领域知识（PRQST关键点）的双流有向图卷积网络用于ECG识别，在九分类任务上F1达88.1%，稀有类别F1达76.3%，优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在医疗等专业领域（如ECG识别）中可解释性不足，纯端到端CNN难以融入领域知识。

Method: 利用PRQST关键点作为领域知识，构建双流有向图：空间有向图建模关键点间位置关系，时间有向图建模相邻周期间的时序依赖。

Result: 在第一届中国心电图智能竞赛数据集（九分类）上，总体平均F1为88.1%，稀有类别平均F1为76.3%，均优于SOTA模型。

Conclusion: 引入领域知识（PRQST关键点）能有效提升ECG识别性能，尤其是稀有类别的检测效果。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [22] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多探针网格算法在近似最近邻搜索中的性能，发现该算法在GloVe嵌入族上展现出独特的维度缩放交叉现象——保持近似恒定的维度缩放指数，而图、树和分区方法吞吐量下降。该算法具有近线性查询缩放和较低索引成本，在重建密集型或高维场景中具有竞争力，并可能为高效Transformer架构的成本分析提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于网格的ANN方法在现代扩展分析中缺位，需要系统评估其在大数据集和高维场景下的性能特性，同时最近的注意力机制与ANN形式化联系也促使探索ANN算法的扩展属性对Transformer架构的指导意义。

Method: 系统性地表征多探针网格算法在数据集规模N和维度d上的性能，通过与图、树和分区方法进行对比实验，在GloVe嵌入族上测量吞吐量和缩放指数。

Result: 在GloVe嵌入族上发现维度缩放交叉现象：多探针网格保持近似恒定的维度缩放指数，而图、树和分区方法的吞吐量随维度增加而下降；该算法在N上呈近线性查询缩放，且索引成本低于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 基于网格的方法（如多探针网格）在重建密集型或高维设置中具有竞争力；ANN算法的N和d缩放属性可指导高效Transformer架构的成本分析。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [23] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IonSense-QKG框架，通过为公共锂离子电池数据集添加量子相关元数据（包括量子编码、量子比特范围、NISQ可行性等），并引入量子就绪评分（Quantum Readiness Score），以支持混合量子-经典机器学习工作流的数据集发现与选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共锂离子电池数据集在化学体系、模态、规模、标签质量、序列结构和预处理复杂度等方面差异巨大，这些差异直接影响数据集是否适用于近期的混合量子-经典机器学习工作流，但目前缺乏系统评估数据集量子就绪性的元数据框架。

Method: 基于EV-Battery-IonSense索引，为电池数据集记录添加量子相关元数据（任务类型、感知模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围、NISQ可行性），并提出量子就绪评分（Quantum Readiness Score）作为数据集排序启发式指标，支持基于查询的数据集发现。

Result: 实现了基于丰富元数据的查询式发现功能，可识别适用于紧凑量子特征图、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测及未来电池健康基准测试的数据集。发布了包含元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL风格查询示例的制品。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG将数据集选择重构为数据管理问题，为数据驱动的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础设施，其评分仅作为数据集选择启发式，而非量子优势的证据。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [24] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归的甲基化图谱分类方法，在CNS肿瘤分类任务中，在2,801样本参考队列上达到96%的交叉验证准确率，在1,104样本独立临床队列上达到86%（91类级）和93%（家族级），较现有最优方法分别提升约4和5个百分点，具有临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有甲基化谱CNS肿瘤分类面临跨队列可迁移性差、方法学正确性不足以及鲁棒的多类评估挑战，亟需一种方法学上更严谨且性能更优的分类方案。

Method: 采用稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）进行降维，结合多项逻辑回归（Multinomial Logistic Regression）进行分类，并在与参考分类器相同的实验设置下进行评估。

Result: 参考队列（2,801样本）上平均准确率96%（3折分层交叉验证）；独立临床队列（1,104样本）上91类分类准确率86%，甲基化家族级准确率93%。相比现有最优方法的82%（类级）和88%（家族级），分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提模型在更强的方法学实践基础上，在所有评估设置下持续优于先前最优方法，能够实质性提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性，对临床治疗选择和决策具有积极影响。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [25] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书从近似理论到涌现现象，系统性地构建了现代深度学习理论的统一数学框架，涵盖过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐与涌现等核心主题，并为每个理论提供受控对象、成立假设与未解释现象的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已超越任何单一数学解释，现有文献碎片化，缺乏统一的理论视角。本书旨在将分散的研究成果组织成连贯的研究叙事，为研究者提供一张严谨的深度学习理论地图。

Method: 采用证明导向的统一论述方式，沿"近似—优化—泛化"的经典基础到过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐与涌现等当代机制的路径展开。每个理论通过其控制的对象、成立的前提假设以及未能解释的现象三个维度进行审视。

Result: 构建了一个涵盖从经典理论到前沿涌现现象的深度学习理论体系，揭示了各机制之间的内在联系与边界条件。

Conclusion: 深度学习理论正处于强大但不完备的阶段，其核心问题日益聚焦于：学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现出来。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [26] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NightVision攻击方法，利用当前限制性的LLM API（仅返回单logit且无偏置功能），通过共同集提示技术和频谱分析估计模型的隐藏维度，结合首token到达时间（TTFT）估计深度和参数量。在32个开源LLM上验证，隐藏维度平均相对误差23%（MoE模型9%），30亿参数以上模型深度和参数量误差53%。结果表明当前API限制不足以完全隐藏模型架构细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM提供商限制API仅返回单个logit且禁止logit偏置功能，但仍需探究在这些限制下是否能恢复模型架构信息，以评估当前API的安全隐私风险。

Method: 提出NightVision攻击方法，包含两个核心步骤：(1) 共同集提示技术——使用多个提示让模型对同一组输出token返回log概率，对结果进行频谱分析以推断隐藏维度；(2) 利用估计的隐藏维度和端到端的首token到达时间（TTFT）测量值来估计模型深度和总参数量。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上，隐藏维度平均相对误差23%（MoE模型9%）；对30亿参数以上模型，深度和参数量估计误差53%。通过消融实验展示了精度随token预算和模型性质的变化规律。

Conclusion: 当前商业LLM API的限制不足以完全隐藏模型架构细节，NightVision证明在仅能获取单个logit的限制下仍能恢复关键架构参数，提示提供商需要更严格的API保护措施。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [27] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态AI管线，利用铁路道口图像和事故报告等结构化数据，评估铁路道口安全等级，所提系统在HIGH-RISK/LOW-RISK分类上达到macro F1=0.757，FRA安全评分预测RMSE=0.078，相关系数0.492。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否仅凭铁路道口图像视觉线索稳健评估其安全性，以及引入事故报告等结构化数据能否提升评估能力，最终构建与专家意见和FRA安全评分对齐的多模态AI安全评估系统。

Method: 构建了一个概念验证管线，包含数据准备、多模态学习范式等环节，采用路由微调的紧凑型视觉语言模型（VLM）管线，融合图像与结构化事故报告数据。

Result: 系统在HIGH-RISK/LOW-RISK二分类上macro F1=0.757；FRA安全评分预测RMSE=0.078，相关系数0.492；定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 所提多模态管线能够有效对齐专家意见和官方安全评分标准，为铁路道口安全评估提供了可行的AI解决方案。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [28] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer在处理电子健康记录（EHR）数据时，对数值编码的离散、连续和混合策略。研究发现：显式建模数值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感任务上表现最佳，但混合token方法（保留数值但分箱后投影）更具鲁棒性和普适性，且最优分箱数遵循关于数据集大小的幂律分布。模型表现出"足够好"的近似计算而非精确算术，实际部署中鲁棒性比数值精度更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在处理电子健康记录数据时，如何对数值进行有效编码是一个关键问题。不同编码策略（离散、连续、混合）在数值精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性之间存在权衡，需要系统的比较研究来指导实际选择。

Method: 在真实EHR数据中嵌入合成算术任务，并结合真实临床预测任务，系统比较离散编码、连续编码和混合编码策略的性能。混合token方法采用保留数值但在投影前进行分箱处理的策略。

Result: 1) 显式建模数值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感算术任务上表现最优，但受架构约束；2) 混合token分箱方法更具鲁棒性和普适性；3) 最优分箱数遵循关于数据集大小的幂律分布；4) 模型表现出"足够好"的近似计算而非精确算术；5) 引入实验室指标的临床收益具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: 在实际部署中，模型的鲁棒性和可用性通常比最大化的数值精度更重要，因此混合token方法可作为实际应用中的默认选择。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [29] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroBridge是一个基于临床指导的多任务MRI框架，通过自监督预训练、海马体分割、萎缩分类和重建目标结合门控融合微调，在AD和MCI诊断中超越传统单任务方法，分别在ADNI和OASIS数据集上达到88.17%和82.78%的准确率，并展现出良好的跨队列泛化能力和概率驱动的机会性筛查可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于MRI的阿尔茨海默病、轻度认知障碍及相关痴呆症诊断面临挑战，因为疾病相关的结构变化往往细微且异质，单任务方法难以充分捕捉这些复杂特征，亟需一种更强大、可扩展的诊断框架。

Method: NeuroBridge框架包含三个阶段：①大规模自监督MRI预训练；②多任务学习阶段，同时优化海马体分割、海马体萎缩分类和图像重建三个目标；③门控融合微调，整合多任务特征进行最终分类诊断。

Result: 在ADNI数据集中AD vs 正常对照分类准确率达88.17%，在OASIS中达82.78%。在MCI相关和混合诊断场景中获得最大性能提升。框架展现出强大的跨队列泛化能力，预测概率与准确性之间存在系统性的关联，并验证了基于概率的机会性筛查的可行性。

Conclusion: 临床指导的多任务表示学习能够显著提升神经退行性疾病的MRI诊断性能，超越传统单任务方法。NeuroBridge为痴呆评估和基于MRI的机会性筛查提供了一个鲁棒且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [30] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpinGTP通过引入自旋加权球谐函数（SWSH）推广Gaunt张量积（GTP），在保持GTP渐近效率的同时恢复了缺失的反反对称交互路径，实现了与完整CGTP相当的性能，特别在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何的任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E(3)-等变网络的CGTP具有O(L^6)复杂度限制了可扩展性；GTP虽降低了复杂度但无法捕获反对称路径，导致表达能力不完整。

Method: 从标量函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数（SWSH），利用SWSH的代数性质恢复缺失的反对称交互，同时保持GTP的渐近效率；构建更具表达力的等变基，自然处理张量积中的奇宇称分量。

Result: 在Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF和OC20等多个基准上，SpinGTP达到了与完整CGTP相当的精度；通过显式捕获反对称路径，在手性材料和非中心对称几何任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: SpinGTP提供了一种完整、可扩展且数学严谨的路径，用于实现大规模3D原子系统模拟中的高阶等变性。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [31] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种代码智能体强化学习执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）的冷启动延迟和资源效率，发现冷启动延迟差异可达110倍，百万轨迹的预估工时差异达1.8倍，建议将执行基础设施作为训练系统的一部分进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体强化学习将执行基础设施视为背景实现细节，忽视了基础设施开销在大规模RL后训练中可带来的效率提升机会。

Method: 对四种执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）进行对比研究，测量冷启动延迟和百万条150步轨迹的预估工时。

Result: 四种执行环境间存在最高110倍的冷启动延迟差异，以及1.8倍的预估工人时差异。

Conclusion: 未来的代码智能体强化学习系统应将执行环境优化作为训练系统本身的一部分，而非仅视为部署管道。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [32] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了条件推理树(CIT)和条件推理森林(CIF)作为Top-k特征排序方法在下游预测任务中的表现，通过真实数据基准、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复实验，分析了CIF在22个分类数据集和8个回归数据集上的排名，并揭示了自适应停止、阈值搜索数等对运行时间的影响，以及森林特征采样可能遗漏重要特征的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件推理树和条件推理森林通过测试特征后再选择分裂阈值来减少分裂选择偏差，但重复的置换检验和阈值搜索导致计算开销巨大。本文旨在系统评估CIT和CIF作为Top-k特征排序方法在下游预测中的表现，并分析其计算效率与精度之间的权衡。

Method: 使用真实数据基准测试、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复实验。在固定节点处，若特征和置换预算不依赖于节点响应，则使用Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值来控制节点级别的拒绝率。通过消融实验分析自适应停止、阈值搜索数等对运行时间的影响，并使用稀疏高维模拟研究森林特征采样行为。

Result: CIF在22个分类数据集上排名第4，在8个回归数据集上排名第3（共18种方法）。关闭自适应停止和采用精确阈值搜索分别使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍和1.9-10.8倍，但下游评分变化最多仅0.011。稀疏高维模拟表明，森林特征采样可能使重要特征在多次分裂决策中被遗漏。

Conclusion: 总体而言，结果支持CIF作为Top-k特征排序方法在评估的下游预测基准中表现良好，但需注意计算效率与特征采样之间的权衡。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [33] [On the Utility and Factual Reliability of Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Models in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01444)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合专家模型在领域特定专家剪枝后，在生物医学等高风险领域的实用性与事实可靠性变化，发现适度剪枝可保持领域内效用且可靠性不立即下降，但极端剪枝会增加幻觉风险，跨领域时效用和可靠性均快速退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要评估剪枝后的基准效用，忽略了剪枝对事实可靠性的影响，特别是在生物医学等高风险领域，安全部署需要同时评估效用与可靠性。

Method: 对四种混合专家模型、六种剪枝方法和多种剪枝比例，在生物医学领域内和跨领域场景下，进行生成任务和分类任务的效用与可靠性评估。

Result: 适度剪枝可保持领域内效用且可靠性不立即下降，但极端剪枝比例下幻觉风险增加；跨领域到通用领域时，效用和可靠性均快速退化。

Conclusion: 安全压缩高度依赖于任务和领域，仅凭效用评估不足以判断剪枝后的混合专家模型是否适合高风险场景部署，必须同时进行可靠性评估。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [34] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何感知的R-结构Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（GRS-KAN），通过将Rvachev的R-函数集成到KAN框架中，利用可微逻辑运算编码几何约束，在含间断点的回归问题上相比标准KAN将测试RMSE降低高达67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准KAN虽然能学习平滑非线性结构，但缺乏显式编码已知几何或逻辑约束（如间断点、可行区域、几何边界）的能力，导致在含结构先验信息的回归任务中性能受限且可解释性不足。

Method: 将Rvachev的R-函数（实现可微逻辑运算的R-合取与R-析取）集成到KAN框架中，提出三种变体：加性、乘性和不可知分支加权架构。KAN分支学习平滑非线性结构，R-函数分支以解析形式显式编码几何/逻辑约束。

Result: 在含圆形和矩形支撑间断点的回归基准测试中，GRS-KAN相比标准KAN将测试RMSE降低高达67%，边界定位精度显著提升，且不可知变体能自动判断几何先验是否有利于当前学习任务。

Conclusion: 显式几何编码能同时提升预测精度、边界定位能力和模型可解释性；不可知变体为自动化几何先验选择提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [35] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Ember优化器，针对嵌入表和LM-head矩阵进行轻量化优化，利用其独特的梯度几何特性，仅需KB级优化器状态即可提升监督微调、强化学习和预训练的Pareto前沿，并发现token优化轨迹可被一维射线描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的嵌入表和LM-head作为连续程序与离散符号之间的读写接口，其梯度几何特性与密集隐藏权重不同，现有Adam优化器对此接口的优化开销过大（O(2VD)），且需要分片token表优化器状态。作者希望利用这一特性设计更高效的优化器。

Method: 提出Ember轻量优化器，仅优化嵌入表和LM-head矩阵，使用O(V + D)显存而非Adam的O(2VD)；不需要分片token表优化器状态；通过实验验证了token优化轨迹可被简单的一维射线描述。

Result: Ember在监督微调、强化学习和预训练中均能改善Pareto前沿；有效扩展到不同批大小和参数量；揭示了token优化轨迹的一维射线特性；证明了Transformer训练所需优化器空间出乎意料地狭窄。

Conclusion: 嵌入表和LM-head的梯度几何特性与密集权重不同，可被专门设计的轻量优化器利用；Ember作为实用工具可无缝集成现有ZeRO/FSDP框架；对神经网络优化景观的传统非凸观点提出挑战。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [36] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCGNM客户优化器，通过类分组归一化动量解决联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题，并引入FedHOO算法优化小客户场景下的重采样率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中类别不平衡导致少数类性能差，而标准集中式技术因隐私和异构性约束无法直接应用。

Method: FedCGNM将类别按最小组内方差分群，每群维护动量并归一化至单位长度，求和作为更新方向，平衡多数/少数类梯度幅值并降低噪声；FedHOO基于X-armed-bandit算法，利用联邦并行性以线性成本评估每个客户两个候选速率的组合。

Result: 在四个公开长尾基准和一个专有芯片缺陷数据集上，FedCGNM持续优于基线，FedHOO在小型联邦中带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: FedCGNM与FedHOO有效解决了FL中的类别不平衡问题，尤其在小客户场景下表现优异。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction. This design both equalizes gradient magnitude across majority and minority groups and mitigates the noise inherent in rare-class gradients. We further provide a theoretical convergence analysis explicitly accounting for time-varying resampling-rates. Additionally, to efficiently optimize these rates in small-client regimes, we introduce FedHOO, an X-armed-bandit (XAB) based algorithm that exploits federated parallelism that evaluates many combinations of two candidate rates per client at linear cost. Empirical evaluation on four public long-tailed benchmarks and a proprietary chip-defect dataset demonstrates that FedCGNM consistently outperforms baselines, with FedHOO yielding further gains in small-scale federations.

</details>


### [37] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种将模型规模、训练数据拆分为训练步数和批大小的三项缩放定律，能更高效地拟合最优批大小，且所需训练次数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律通常仅考虑模型大小和数据量，未显式区分训练步数和批大小对训练效率的影响，导致需要大量最优配置下的训练才能拟合。

Method: 将传统两项缩放定律扩展为三项：模型规模、训练步数和批大小；在大规模训练集上拟合该定律，验证其恢复最优批大小缩放规律的能力。

Result: 三项定律正确恢复了最优批大小的缩放行为；在次优批大小训练数据下仍能稳健拟合，显著减少所需训练次数；可推导次优批大小的缩放规律，并与临界批大小的先前实证结果吻合。

Conclusion: 显式分离批大小为独立变量的三项缩放定律更具普适性和数据效率，为实际训练中的资源配置提供了更简洁可靠的指导。

Abstract: We propose a scaling law that takes into account model size and training data while explicitly splitting the latter into training steps and batch size (called three-term law). Fitting the proposed law on a large set of training runs, we find that it correctly recovers the scaling of the optimal batch size. Moreover, because it makes use of training runs with suboptimal batch size, our proposed law can be robustly fit with a significantly smaller amount of training runs. We further show that the three-term law can be used to derive scaling laws for suboptimal batch sizes, and that it matches previous empirical findings related to the critical batch size.

</details>


### [38] [Don't Let Gains FADE: Breaking Down Policy Gradient Weights in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01490)
*Juliette Decugis,Sean O'Brien,Francis Bach,Gabriel Synnaeve,Taco Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FADE（Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy），一种自适应的优势函数，通过正交分解优势函数的正负梯度和难度维度，动态调整梯度权重，解决了强化学习后训练中的训练不稳定性和多样性坍塌问题。FADE在7B和32B规模上分别比最优静态基线提前20k和2k步骤达到峰值pass@1，并在LiveCodeBench和AIME上实现最佳准确率-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练能显著提升LLM推理能力，但存在训练不稳定性和多样性坍塌问题。优势函数虽能缓解这些问题，但现有方法繁多，不清楚何时使用何种优势函数。

Method: 将优势函数沿两个正交轴（符号轴和难度轴）分解为正负梯度质量。符号轴上不平衡更新会导致熵或权重几何坍塌；难度轴上困难样本聚焦会增强信号但牺牲样本量。基于此提出FADE，一种自适应的优势函数，能读取训练动态自动调度梯度权重。

Result: FADE在7B规模上比最优静态基线提前20k步达到峰值pass@1，在32B规模上提前2k步，并在LiveCodeBench和AIME上所有pass@k指标中实现最佳准确率-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: FADE通过动态自适应优势函数有效解决了训练不稳定性和多样性坍塌问题，显著加速了收敛并提升了性能-多样性权衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training dramatically improves LLM reasoning, but suffers from training instability and diversity collapse. Advantage functions offer an appealing fix: they reshape the training objective, reweight which rollouts drive learning, and are trivial to implement. Yet a proliferation of methods makes it unclear which advantage to use and when. We cut through the confusion with a unifying framework that decomposes any advantage into its positive and negative gradient mass along two orthogonal axes. On the sign axis, imbalanced updates collapse either entropy or weight geometry. On the difficulty axis, hard-problem focus sharpens signal but costs sample size. Both trade-offs shift during training: exploration favors balance and hard focus; exploitation favors suppression and medium focus. This motivates FADE (Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy), a self-adapting advantage that reads training dynamics to schedule the gradient weight automatically. FADE reaches peak pass@1 20k steps earlier than the best static baseline at the 7B scale and 2k steps earlier at the 32B , while achieving the best accuracy-diversity trade-off across all pass@k on LiveCodeBench and AIME.

</details>


### [39] [Unveiling the Non-Monotonic Effect of Privacy on Generalization under Byzantine Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01492)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习中鲁棒性、本地差分隐私与泛化误差之间的权衡关系，发现该权衡依赖于隐私强度：强隐私下增加隐私降低泛化误差，弱隐私下增加隐私则恶化泛化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明分布式学习中拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私与优化误差之间存在根本性三元悖论，但该悖论是否适用于泛化误差尚不清楚，本文旨在探索泛化误差视角下的三者关系。

Method: 通过算法稳定性分析，推导了LDP约束下拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习的泛化误差下界与上界，分别在强隐私（高噪声）和弱隐私（低噪声）两种隐私体制下刻画了隐私与泛化误差的关系。

Result: 强隐私体制下增加隐私减少泛化误差，鲁棒性与隐私无冲突；弱隐私体制下隐私与泛化误差存在逆向关系，鲁棒性与隐私的张力重新出现。给出了匹配的上下界并辅以实验验证。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性与差分隐私在泛化误差上的关系是非单调的，取决于所处隐私体制，打破了之前认为存在普遍三元悖论的观点。

Abstract: Recent work has established a fundamental trilemma between Byzantine robustness, local differential privacy (LDP), and optimization error in distributed learning. We show that this trilemma does not universally extend to generalization error, but instead depends critically on the privacy regime. Specifically, in the high-noise regime (strong privacy), we prove that increasing privacy reduces the generalization error, i.e., there is no tension between robustness and privacy. In the low-noise regime (weaker privacy), however, the tension between robustness and privacy reappears and increasing privacy indeed degrades generalization. Our theory explains this surprising non-monotonic behavior of the generalization error via matching lower and upper bounds on the algorithmic stability of Byzantine-robust distributed learning under LDP constraints. We corroborate and further analyze these theoretical findings with empirical evaluations.

</details>

*Kevin Wang,Kevin Yang,Arjun Prakash,Amy Greenwald*

TL;DR: 本文研究了双人零和不完美信息博弈中策略表示（嵌入）的学习问题，提出了策略数据集构建方法、策略表示学习方法以及下游评估任务，并在Kuhn和Leduc扑克上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对博弈中策略表示进行系统性自监督学习的研究，需要探索如何从策略中学习有用的行为表示。

Method: 1) 提出多种策略数据集构建方法；2) 提出多种策略嵌入学习方法；3) 设计下游任务用于评估表示质量。在Kuhn和Leduc扑克上对每种数据集方法、嵌入方法和下游任务进行系统评估。

Result: 尽管所用方法十分基础，但实验表明所学嵌入中确实包含了有意义的策略行为表示。

Conclusion: 该工作是首批系统比较自监督学习技术在博弈策略表示学习中效果的研究之一，并开源了代码以供后续扩展。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of learning useful policy representations (embeddings) in two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games. We make three contributions: First, we introduce methods of creating datasets of policies for a given game. Second, we propose methods to learn policy representations. Third, we introduce downstream tasks to evaluate the effectiveness of such representations.
  We evaluate each dataset method, embedding method, and downstream task on Kuhn and Leduc Poker. Although our methods are very basic, we demonstrate that useful behavioral representations are present in the learned embeddings. To our knowledge, this work is among the first to systematically compare self-supervised learning techniques for learning policy representations in games. Our code is available at https://github.com/VitamintK/ssl-project for others to extend.

</details>


### [41] [The risk of KV cache compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01520)
*Lukas Haverbeck,Carmen Amo Alonso,Andres Felipe Posada-Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过刻画KV缓存压缩的极小化极大风险，揭示了缓存内在可压缩性与准确压缩可行性的关系，提出了在因果掩码下达到极小化最优风险的设计原则，并在LongBench上展示了实际算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法主要依赖经验实验，缺乏理论指导；最坏情况下压缩可能不可行，但实际场景中往往存在可压缩性，需要系统性的理论框架来指导算法设计。

Method: 通过极小化极大风险（minimax risk）刻画KV缓存压缩的理论界限，建立缓存内在可压缩性与压缩精度之间的定量关系，并在此基础上推导出符合因果掩码约束的设计原则，这些原则可高效映射到预填充和自回归解码阶段。

Result: 提出了在因果掩码下实现极小化最优风险的KV缓存压缩算法，在LongBench上的定向实验中取得了有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: 本文为KV缓存压缩提供了理论保证的设计原则，填补了实践经验与理论分析之间的鸿沟，为后续实用压缩算法设计提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Transformer inference on long sequences is expensive because softmax attention repeatedly reads from a large KV cache. The prevalent approach to this bottleneck is KV cache compression, which replaces the full cache with a compact summary. Despite its practical importance, the design of such summaries is largely driven by empirical experimentation. On the theoretical side, existing results show that KV cache compression can be impossible in the worst case, but offer little systematic guidance for designing algorithms in regimes where accurate compression is possible. We bridge this gap by characterizing the minimax risk of KV cache compression in terms of the intrinsic compressibility of a cache, revealing when and how accurate compression is possible. These results yield novel design principles for KV cache compression under causal masking that map efficiently to prefill and autoregressive decoding while achieving minimax-optimal risk. We instantiate these principles in a practical algorithm and report promising performance on LongBench in targeted experiments. Overall, our results provide a principled avenue for practical KV cache compression with theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [42] [Multi-Head Recurrent Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01523)
*Jiatong Li,Samuel Yeh,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多头循环记忆（MHM）框架，通过将记忆划分为独立头并采用阶段性“选择-更新”策略，从根本上解决循环记忆智能体在超长上下文场景下因记忆覆盖导致的性能退化问题。轻量实例化版本MHM-LRU在896K token长度上，将记忆保留率从不足30%提升至73.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环记忆智能体虽然能扩展LLM至任意长上下文，但存在系统性可靠性问题：随着上下文长度增长，端到端性能持续下降。现有研究未系统诊断这一退化的根本原因。

Method: 将性能分解为记忆捕获和记忆保持两个因子，量化发现保持是主导瓶颈。提出通用无训练框架MHM：将记忆划分为多个独立头，采用逐阶段"选择-更新"策略，每步仅更新一个头，其余头结构上防止覆盖。MHM-LRU基于最近最少更新策略选择头，保证均匀利用且零额外token开销。

Result: 在100K-1M token范围的长上下文基准测试中，MHM-LRU显著改善记忆保持和端到端精度。RULER-HQA中896K token长度下记忆保留率<30%→73.96%。效果跨模型家族、规模及任务类型泛化。

Conclusion: 架构优化是解决长上下文循环记忆可靠性的实用且低成本的路径，将记忆保持负担从模型行为转移至架构设计。

Abstract: Recurrent memory agents extend LLMs to arbitrarily long contexts by iteratively consolidating input into a fixed-size memory window. Despite their scalability, these agents exhibit a well-documented reliability problem: end-to-end performance degrades systematically as context length grows. We diagnose this failure by decomposing performance into two factors--memory capture and memory retention--and quantitatively confirm that retention is the dominant bottleneck. Retention collapses because existing designs maintain memory as a monolithic text block, forcing every update to risk overwriting previously retained content. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Multi-Head Recurrent Memory (MHM), a general, training-free framework that partitions memory into independent heads governed by a stage-wise select-then-update strategy. At each step, exactly one head is selected for update while the remaining heads are structurally shielded from overwriting, shifting the burden of retention from model behavior to architectural design. As a lightweight instantiation, we introduce Least-Recently-Updated MHM (MHM-LRU), which guarantees uniform head utilization with zero additional token overhead. Extensive experiments on long-context benchmarks show that MHM-LRU substantially improves both retention and end-to-end accuracy across the 100K--1M token range, where baselines degrade sharply. On RULER-HQA at 896K tokens, MHM-LRU improves the memory retention rate from less than 30% to 73.96%. These gains generalize across model families, scales, and task types, positioning architectural optimization as a practical and cost-efficient path toward reliable long-context recurrent memory.

</details>


### [43] [Wind-Aware Reinforcement Learning Control of a Small Quadrotor Using Learned Onboard Wind Estimation in Simulated Atmospheric Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01528)

Abstract: Small multirotor aircraft are increasingly tasked with operations in the atmospheric boundary layer, where turbulent winds comparable to the vehicle's airspeed degrade trajectory tracking and can defeat conventional feedback control. This work illustrates a two-stage learning pipeline that first estimates the local wind from onboard kinematics and dynamics and then exploits that estimate inside a reinforcement learning (RL) flight controller. The wind estimator, an attention-augmented gated recurrent network trained on thousands of simulated flights through von Karman turbulence with power-law shear and veer, recovers the horizontal wind vector with a per-flight root-mean-square error of 0.40 m/s and a direction error of 3.2 degrees on unseen wind regimes, an accuracy near the floor imposed by unresolved turbulence, and generalizes to vertical ascent profiles with a skill score of 0.861 over a constant-wind reference. A proximal policy optimization controller receiving the frozen estimator's output reduces horizontal trajectory tracking error by 48% relative to a wind-blind proportional-derivative baseline across mean winds of 4 m/s to 12 m/s, winning on 100% of evaluation episodes. A three-way ablation decomposes this improvement into a kinematic component, available without wind information, and a wind-perception component; the perception share rises with wind speed, from small in light winds toward roughly half the total benefit in strong winds, consistent with the quadratic scaling of aerodynamic drag. The controller degrades gracefully on out-of-distribution winds of 13 m/s to 15 m/s, where the baseline fails catastrophically.

</details>

*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于认证世界模型的感知时钟机制，通过漂移感知的有效性包络为智能体设定无感知区间截止时间，在冻结的3D VN-JEPA模型上验证了其保持证书有效性的能力，并在定理实验床上相比精确混合期望调度显著减少了事件尾违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型预测的可靠性随时间衰减，需要一种机制来明确智能体何时应当停止依赖模型预测并重新感知环境，以确保证书有效性。

Method: 从经审计的等变世界模型出发，推导出无感知区间的截止时间；提出部署时需使用校准的本机展开漂移包络（而非仅用流形上的Lyapunov率）；在冻结的3D VN-JEPA模型和定理实验床上验证该感知时钟的有效性。

Result: 在冻结3D VN-JEPA上，该时钟跨种子和数据分片保持证书有效性；在定理实验床上，在同等感知预算下相比精确混合期望调度显著降低事件尾违规。在短视界冻结VN-JEPA场景中，经验共形视界与部署时钟在有效性和预算上匹配。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提出了一个认证的感知时钟原语和漂移感知的部署方法，而非声称谱时钟在经验上优于所有非谱调度器。

Abstract: Certified world models estimate how long their predictions remain valid. We turn this validity horizon into an operational sensing clock: a rule for when an agent should stop coasting and re-sense. Starting from an audited equivariant world model, we derive a deadline for no-sensing intervals and show that deployable deadlines in learned world models must be drift-aware: on-manifold Lyapunov rates alone overestimate coasting validity, while calibrated native rollout-drift envelopes carry the deployed guarantee. On a frozen 3D VN-JEPA model, the resulting clock controls held-out interval-simultaneous certificate violation across seeds and data shards. In a cue-conditioned theorem-bed (a synthetic bench where all schedulers share the exact model, isolating the scheduling rule), the clock remains valid on the deployment distribution and substantially reduces eventful-tail violations relative to exact-mixture expected-belief scheduling at matched sensing budget. We also report limits: in the short-horizon frozen VN-JEPA regime, empirical conformal horizons match the deployed clock on validity and budget, and a partial-reset exploration finds no clean budget-matched advantage for the spectral term. Thus the contribution is a certified sensing-clock primitive and drift-aware deployment method, not a claim that spectral clocks empirically dominate all non-spectral schedulers.

</details>


### [45] [Evolutionary Feature Engineering for Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01548)
*Ege Onur Taga,Yilin Zhuang,M. Emrullah Ildiz,Petros Mol,Abhimanyu Das,Karthik Duraisamy,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出进化特征工程（EFE）框架，利用基于LLM的进化优化为结构化数据自动发现预处理变换。EFE将变换表示为Python程序，通过标准化接口集成到ML管道中，进化过程中利用数据集上下文、统计摘要和验证集性能反馈优化候选程序。在时间序列预测中（EFE-Time），学习可逆数据集特定归一化，平均降低3%以上预测误差（最高19%）；在表格预测中（EFE-Tab），演化紧凑特征程序，提升或匹配现有LLM特征工程方法，尤其对决策树效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于进化优化中的开放式搜索，但如何利用LLM自动发现结构化数据（时间序列和表格数据）的预处理变换以提升下游任务性能，仍是一个有待探索的问题。

Method: 提出EFE框架，将特征变换表示为具有标准化fit/transform接口的Python程序，通过LLM驱动的进化优化来迭代改进这些程序，优化依据包括数据集上下文、统计摘要和验证集性能反馈。在时间序列领域实例化为EFE-Time（学习可逆归一化），在表格领域实例化为EFE-Tab（演化紧凑特征程序）。

Result: EFE-Time在多个时间序列数据集上平均降低预测误差（MASE, WQL, MAE）3%以上，在COVID-Deaths数据集上最高改善达19%，对Chronos-2等最新TSFM有效。EFE-Tab在表格预测中提升或匹配现有LLM特征工程方法，尤其在经典决策树上，小规模演化特征集在保持可解释性的同时达到竞争性精度。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的进化优化能够同时提升结构化数据任务的准确性和可解释性，EFE框架为自动特征工程提供了有效且实用的新范式。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as open-ended search operators in evolutionary optimization. We introduce Evolutionary Feature Engineering (EFE), a framework for using LLM-based evolution to discover preprocessing transformations for structured data. EFE represents transformations as Python programs with a standardized fit/transform interface, allowing them to be inserted directly into existing machine learning pipelines. During evolution, candidate programs are refined using dataset context, summary statistics, and downstream performance feedback on validation set. We instantiate EFE in two settings.
