### [118] [Verification and Performance Assessment of NuDEAL, a GPU-Accelerated Deterministic Transport Framework on Unstructured Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01591)
*Kyung Min Kim,Jaeuk Im,Han Gyu Lee,Yeon Sang Jung*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在统一框架NuDEAL中开发的三种GPU加速确定性求解器（MOC/HFEM、DGMOC、DFEM-SN），用于解决先进反应堆的高保真中子学分析问题。DFEM-SN精度最高（特征值误差<50 pcm），而MOC/HFEM和DGMOC效率更优，单GPU运行时间可与大型CPU集群媲美。该框架为未来瞬态和多物理场分析奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进反应堆的高保真中子学分析需要能够在复杂非结构化网格上高效计算的确定性输运求解器，而现有方法在精度、计算成本和内存需求之间难以取得平衡。

Method: 在统一框架NuDEAL中实现了三种GPU加速确定性求解器：平面特征线法耦合混合有限元法（MOC/HFEM）、间断伽辽金特征线法（DGMOC）和间断有限元离散纵标法（DFEM-SN）。所有方法通过内存对齐、压缩通量存储和顺序方位角扫描实现高效GPU执行。

Result: 在C5G7基准题及ABTR、Empire微反应堆、MSRE等先进反应堆问题上验证。DFEM-SN特征值误差低于50 pcm，精度最高；MOC/HFEM和DGMOC的单GPU运行时间可与大型CPU集群相当，效率最优。

Conclusion: 非结构化网格上的确定性GPU求解器能够兼顾精度和可扩展性，实现异构先进反应堆的实际全堆芯模拟。NuDEAL统一框架为未来在大型GPU架构上开展瞬态和多物理场分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: High-fidelity neutronic analyses of advanced reactors require deterministic transport solvers capable of handling complex unstructured geometries while maintaining computational efficiency. This work presents the development and verification of three GPU-accelerated deterministic solvers implemented within a unified framework, Neutronics using Deterministic Finite Element Algorithm (NuDEAL): the planar Method of Characteristics coupled with the Hybrid Finite Element Method (MOC/HFEM), the Discontinuous Galerkin Method of Characteristics (DGMOC), and the Discontinuous Finite Element discrete ordinate method (DFEM-SN). These solvers provide complementary capabilities for consistently solving the multigroup transport equation and can be selectively employed to balance accuracy, computational cost, and memory requirements for a given problem. All methods emphasize efficient GPU execution by leveraging memory alignment, compressed-flux storage, and sequential azimuthal sweeps. The solvers are validated on the C5G7 benchmark and applied to advanced reactor problems, including the ABTR, Empire microreactor, and MSRE. DFEM-SN achieved the highest accuracy, with eigenvalue errors below 50 pcm, while MOC/HFEM and DGMOC provided superior efficiency, with single-GPU runtimes comparable to those of large CPU clusters. The results demonstrate that deterministic GPU solvers on unstructured meshes can deliver both accuracy and scalability, enabling practical whole-core simulations for heterogeneous advanced reactors. The unified NuDEAL framework establishes a foundation for future extensions toward transient and multiphysics analyses on large-scale GPU architectures.

</details>

### [119] [Exact quantum time compatible with positive energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01296)
*Ovidiu Cristinel Stoica*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在量子力学中，尽管存在Unruh-Wald和Hegerfeldt-Ruijsenaars不可能性定理（禁止完美单调时钟和不可逆变化），但通过内禀视角解决“时间叠加”悖论，论证了尖锐的不可逆变化和内禀时间演化是可能的，并导出了不含引力的Wheeler-DeWitt型方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unruh-Wald（1989）证明，若能量有下界，则没有可观测量能随薛定谔时间参数t单调增加；Hegerfeldt-Ruijsenaars（1980）也指出完美单调时钟和不可逆变化似乎不可能。但这一结论与我们的日常时间体验相矛盾。作者旨在解决这一矛盾，解释不可逆时钟和时间变化如何成为可能。

Method: 从内禀视角（intrinsic perspective）出发，认为世界处于不同内禀时钟态（指示不同时间和相反时间方向）的叠加中。宏观指针态通过退相干解决这种时间叠加。引入无界的**内禀时间平移生成元**替代哈密顿量，生成仅沿内禀时间方向的前向演化，而非薛定谔参数t方向的演化。

Result: （1）证明了内禀视角下尖锐的不可逆变化确实发生，因为宏观指针态分辨了不同时间的叠加；大尺度时间反演或不连续跃迁在内部记录中不可观测。（2）内禀时间生成元无界，可产生尖锐的时间可观测量，即使外部哈密顿量有下界。（3）导出了不含引力假设的、满足Wheeler-DeWitt型方程的静止宇宙波函数。

Conclusion: 薛定谔参数t不具备作为真正时间角色的正当性；内禀时间才是物理上有意义的时间。该工作统一了量子力学中的时间问题，提供了在不引入引力的情况下理解宇宙波函数静止性的新途径。

Abstract: What would it be like to be in a superposition of yesterday, today, and tomorrow? This question may seem at best entertaining, but it is necessary, and exploring it allows us to understand how exact irreversible clocks and change are possible, despite the Unruh-Wald and Hegerfeldt-Ruijsenaars no-go theorems forbidding them.
  Unruh and Wald (1989) proved that if energy is bounded from below, no observable can increase monotonically with the Schrödinger time parameter t. Perfectly monotonic clocks and irreversible observable changes (Hegerfeldt-Ruijsenaars, 1980) seem impossible. From the perspective of the Schrödinger time, the world appears in a superposition of different intrinsic clock states indicating different times and opposite time directions. This seems to directly contradict our daily experiences of time and change.
  I show that there is no contradiction: from an intrinsic perspective of the world, sharp irreversible changes do happen, because the macroscopic pointer states resolve the superposition of different times. Large-scale time-reversing or discontinuous transitions are not internally observable in the records. From the intrinsic perspective, an unbounded intrinsic-time translation generator plays the role of the Hamiltonian, generating only forward time evolution with respect to the intrinsic time, but not to the Schrödinger parameter t, which is thus not justified to play the role of time. This allows sharp time observables even if the external Hamiltonian is bounded from below. In addition, this leads to a stationary wavefunction of the universe satisfying a Wheeler-DeWitt-type equation, without assuming gravity.

</details>


### [120] [Logarithmic negativity typically equals exact entanglement cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01320)
*Bowen Ouyang,Jonah Kudler-Flam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于大随机诱导混合态，对数负性（一种高效可计算的纠缠度量）在正部分转置保持操作下与精确纠缠成本一致，从而获得了精确的操作性解释，为复杂量子系统中的纠缠量化提供了可行途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠在理解物理系统中至关重要，但现有的操作意义上的纠缠度量在大量子系统中因缺乏高效计算算法而难以评估，亟需一种既具有操作意义又计算可行的纠缠量化方法。

Method: 通过数学证明，展示了对于大随机诱导混合态，对数负性（一种高效可计算的纠缠度量）在正部分转置保持（PPT）操作下与精确纠缠成本（entanglement cost）一致。

Result: 证实了对数负性在一般多体态中可作为纠缠的精确刻画，为大随机诱导混合态提供了操作意义下的纠缠量化。

Conclusion: 对数负性是一种既高效可计算又具有精确操作解释的纠缠度量，为复杂量子系统中纠缠的量化提供了可循路径。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement plays a leading role in modern understanding of physical systems, from quantum phases of matter to quantum gravity. In quantum information theory, one seeks operationally meaningful quantifiers of entanglement, which for large quantum systems are notoriously difficult to evaluate due to the lack of computationally efficient algorithms. In this Letter, we show that for large random induced mixed states the logarithmic negativity, an efficiently computable entanglement measure, generically coincides with the exact entanglement cost under positive-partial-transpose-preserving operations, thereby acquiring a precise operational interpretation. Our results establish logarithmic negativity as an exact characterization of entanglement in generic many-body states and provide a tractable route for quantifying entanglement in complex quantum systems.

</details>


### [121] [Noisy quantum circuit simulation with the tensor jump method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01323)
*Maximilian Fröhlich,Aaron Sander,Martin Eigel,Robert Wille,Michael Hintermüller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方差感知的张量网络框架，结合张量跳跃方法与局部TDVP门演化及稀疏Pauli-Lindblad硬件噪声模型，实现了对含噪量子电路的高效经典模拟。该方法支持多量子比特关联Lindblad噪声，并开发了两种无偏方差感知解缠（模拟酉混合解缠和投影仪跳跃解缠），在25量子比特和127量子比特基准测试上展现出比标准Kraus插入基线更低的蒙特卡洛方差和更优的MPS键维增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含噪量子电路的经典模拟对于验证算法、基准测试硬件和评估纠错策略至关重要，但密度矩阵方法的指数级成本和标准轨迹采样的高方差限制了其可扩展性。

Method: 提出方差感知的张量网络框架，核心包括：(1) 张量跳跃方法结合局部TDVP门演化作用于矩阵乘积态（MPS）；(2) 稀疏Pauli-Lindblad硬件噪声模型；(3) 两种无偏方差感知解缠：模拟酉混合解缠（精确匹配Lindblad生成元）和投影仪跳跃解缠（状态无关风险与闭式方差律）；(4) 支持相关多量子比特Lindblad噪声及长程算符模拟串扰。

Result: 在25量子比特含噪XY淬火和IBM 127量子比特踢伊辛基准测试中，该方法相比标准Kraus插入基线，实现了更低的蒙特卡洛方差和更有利的MPS键维增长，证明了准确且可扩展的含噪电路模拟能力。

Conclusion: 投影仪跳跃采样在多种电路架构下能显著降低轨迹方差和键维增长，而模拟采样在弱噪声下最有效。两种解缠均保留了标准1/√N蒙特卡洛收敛性但具有更小的前置因子，为大规模含噪量子电路模拟提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Classical simulation of noisy quantum circuits is essential for validating algorithms, benchmarking hardware, and assessing error-mitigation strategies, but remains limited by the exponential cost of density-matrix methods and the high variance of standard trajectory sampling. We introduce a variance-aware tensor network framework that combines the tensor jump method with local TDVP gate evolution on matrix product states and sparse Pauli-Lindblad hardware noise models. Gates are applied as short variational evolutions on the MPS manifold, while noise is sampled per circuit window from Pauli-Lindblad jump sets with state-independent hazards and dissipative contractions that reduce to irrelevant global factors after renormalization. The method supports correlated multi-qubit Lindblad noise consistent with hardware connectivity, including long-range operators on non-adjacent qubits, enabling direct simulation of crosstalk and other connectivity-induced errors beyond local noise models. We develop two unbiased variance-aware unravelings. An analog unitary-mixture unraveling matches the Lindblad generator exactly under symmetric Gaussian or two-point angle laws, while a projector-jump unraveling yields state-independent hazards and closed-form variance laws. Both retain the standard 1/sqrt(N) Monte Carlo convergence but with reduced prefactors. Empirically, projector sampling strongly reduces trajectory variance and bond-dimension growth across many circuit architectures, whereas analog sampling is most effective at weak noise. We demonstrate accurate, scalable noisy-circuit simulation on a 25-qubit noisy XY quench and IBM's 127-qubit kicked-Ising benchmark with long-range depolarizing noise, achieving reduced Monte Carlo variance and favorable MPS bond-dimension growth compared with standard Kraus-insertion baselines.

</details>


### [122] [Integrated Photon-Memory Entanglement Generation using Dual Photonic Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01324)
*Alexander Kolar,Ian Chin,Conner Fong,Daniil M. Lukin,Melissa A. Guidry,Milan Palei,Jelena Vučković,Tian Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在碳化硅平台上集成了双微环谐振器，分别作为纠缠光子对源和腔增强原子频率梳量子存储器，实现了电信波段光子-存储器纠缠，展示了高达63个时间模式的高维纠缠，以及88.1%的单对干涉可见度和5.6 kEbits s⁻¹的片上纠缠率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子网络需要高效的光子-量子存储器纠缠生成与同步，但现有方案缺乏在同一平台内集成光谱匹配的光子源和量子存储器的能力，这是实现高度复用量子中继器的关键挑战。

Method: 在碳化硅平台上设计双微环谐振器架构：一个谐振器作为纠缠光子对源（自发四波混频），另一个作为腔增强原子频率梳量子存储器（基于掺铒离子），两者通过同一平台实现本征光谱匹配，无需额外光谱整形。

Result: 存储器腔体协同性达1.9；单对纠缠干涉可见度88.1±10.6%；实现了最多63个时间模式的高维光子-存储器量子纠缠，光子信息效率达5.1 Ebits/探测光子，片上纠缠率达5.6 kEbits s⁻¹。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个集成化的光子-存储器纠缠生成平台，为芯片级量子中继器和基于存储器的可扩展量子网络提供了可行路径，可兼容现有电信基础设施。

Abstract: Scalable quantum networks require the efficient generation, storage, and synchronization of entanglement between photonic qubits and quantum memories. Quantum repeater architectures based on absorptive rare-earth-ion photonic memories offer a promising route toward highly multiplexed quantum networking, but integrating spectrally matched photon sources and quantum memories within a common platform remains a major challenge. Here we demonstrate an integrated photonic architecture for telecom photon-memory entanglement generation based on dual silicon-carbide microring resonators. One resonator operates as an entangled photon-pair source, while the other functions as a cavity-enhanced atomic-frequency-comb quantum memory. The memory resonator achieves an ensemble cooperativity of 1.9 and is intrinsically spectrally matched to the photon source, enabling storage of entangled telecom photons without spectral modification. We generate and verify photon-memory entanglement with a single-pair interference visibility of 88.1 $\pm$ 10.6%. By exploiting the multimode capacity of the memory, we demonstrate high-dimensional photon-memory qudit entanglement spanning up to 63 temporal modes, leading to a maximum photon information efficiency of 5.1 Ebits per detected photon and a peak on-chip photon-memory entanglement rate of 5.6 kEbits s$^{-1}$. These results establish the first integrated platform for photon-memory entanglement generation and provide a scalable route toward chip-scale quantum repeaters and memory-enabled quantum networks operating over telecommunications infrastructure.

</details>


### [123] [One-Body Purity, Non-Gaussianity, and Entanglement in Interacting Integrable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01326)
*Rafał Świętek,Maksymilian Kliczkowski,Lev Vidmar,Marcos Rigol*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用多项式多个高斯态的随机叠加来描述可积多体系统本征态的纠缠特性，而指数多个高斯态的随机叠加则描述不可积系统本征态，并通过单体纯度、非高斯性和纠缠熵的分析和数值结果加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解可积与非可积多体晶格哈密顿量本征态中纠缠特性的不同范式，特别是可积相互作用系统中本征态虽非高斯但表现出高斯态纠缠行为这一矛盾。

Method: 通过比较随机叠加态（多项式/指数多个高斯态的随机叠加）与哈密顿量本征态在单体纯度、非高斯性和纠缠熵上的解析与数值结果。

Result: 可积系统本征态可由多项式多个高斯态的随机叠加描述，而非可积系统本征态则需指数多个高斯态的随机叠加。

Conclusion: 随机叠加高斯态为描述可积与非可积多体系统本征态的纠缠和非高斯性提供了统一的范式。

Abstract: When describing entanglement in typical midspectrum eigenstates of many-body lattice Hamiltonians, two paradigms have emerged that capture the behavior observed in integrable and nonintegrable systems, Haar-random fermionic Gaussian states and Haar-random pure states, respectively. Remarkably, the former capture the behavior of interacting integrable systems, whose eigenstates are non-Gaussian. We argue that the paradigm that captures both the entanglement properties and the lack of Gaussianity in integrable systems is that of random superpositions of polynomially many Gaussian states. In contrast, eigenstates of nonintegrable systems are consistent with being described by random superpositions of exponentially many Gaussian states. We gain this understanding by comparing analytical and numerical results for the one-body purity, the non-Gaussianity, and the entanglement entropy of the random superpositions and the Hamiltonian eigenstates.

</details>


### [124] [Ravines in quantum cost landscapes: opportunities for improved VQA predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01329)
*Felix J. Beckmann,João F. Bravo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于改进的 nudged elastic band (NEB) 算法来分析和利用变分量子算法中量子代价景观（QCL）的“沟壑”（ravine）结构——即连接局部极小值的低成本路径。通过在量子神经网络分类任务中应用该算法，作者构建了沿NEB路径的集成预测框架，并引入了一种轻量级的预训练指标（局部预测变异性）作为VQA性能的强预测因子。实验表明，基于NEB的量子集成方法在性能和计算成本上均优于经典和朴素量子替代方案，且沟壑结构在深度和量子比特扩展下依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法（VQAs）的性能受量子代价景观（QCL）的几何与拓扑结构制约，而现有研究对QCL中连接局部极小值的低成本路径（沟壑）的系统分析尚不充分。本文旨在通过引入计算化学中的NEB算法，系统识别和利用QCL的沟壑结构，以提升量子神经网络的预测性能并降低计算开销。

Method: 采用改进的 nudged elastic band (NEB) 算法（源自理论化学）分析QCL中的沟壑结构；在硬件高效拟设的量子神经网络上训练分类任务（对量子态的集中纠缠进行分类）；构建沿NEB低成本路径的集成预测框架；引入基于局部预测变异性的轻量级预训练性能指标；进行深度与量子比特扩展分析。

Result: 数值实验识别出QCL中的沟壑结构；基于高局部预测变异性初始化的NEB量子集成在性能上超越经典和朴素量子基线；复杂度分析表明NEB方法显著降低计算成本；深度和量子比特扩展分析显示沟壑结构持续存在，且NEB方法加速收敛。

Conclusion: QCL的沟壑结构是可利用的几何特征，NEB集成方法为VQA提供了一种高效且性能优越的方案；所提出的局部预测变异性指标可作为VQA性能的通用预测因子，具有超出本研究的广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: The geometric and topological structure of quantum cost landscapes (QCLs) governs the optimization and thus the predictive power of variational quantum algorithms (VQAs). We systematically analyze ravines - low-cost paths connecting local minima - using an adapted version of the nudged elastic band (NEB) algorithm, a method originating from theoretical chemistry. By training quantum neural networks (QNNs) to classify the concentratable entanglement of quantum states, we apply the NEB algorithm and numerically identify ravine structures in QCLs of hardware-efficient ansatzes. Beyond visualizing these ravines, we construct an ensemble prediction framework by averaging predictions from QNNs parameterized along the low-cost NEB path. We introduce a resource-light pre-training metric which quantifies local-prediction variability and serves as a strong performance indicator for VQAs, even beyond the scope of this study. When base classifiers are drawn from circuit and weight initializations exhibiting high local-prediction variability, the quantum-based NEB ensembles outperform both classical and naive quantum alternatives. Moreover, a complexity analysis shows that leveraging the ravine-like structure of QCLs with the QNN NEB approach substantially reduces computational costs compared to naive QNN ensembling. A depth and qubit scaling analysis indicates that ravines persist across both scalings, and that, despite the expected growth in resource requirements with the qubit scaling, the NEB approach also accelerates convergence over the naive alternative.

</details>


### [125] [Controlling Waiting Time Statistics in Monitored Collective Spins: Mitigating Detector's Resolution Barrier in Measurement-Induced Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01332)
*Tanbir Islam,Fernando Iemini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了集体耗散自旋系统中，通过初始态不均匀性（将系统分为两个旋转θ角的子系综）来控制量子跳变等待时间统计，从而缓解探测器有限时间分辨率问题。研究发现测量诱导相变在非均匀性下仍然存在，且等待时间随θ增加而显著增长（1/N标度但前因子增强数个量级），在θ=π时保持有限，完全解决分辨率障碍，但代价是纠缠饱和时间显著延长，部分重新引入了后选择屏障。研究揭示了探测器分辨率与后选择开销之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体耗散自旋系统中，后选择屏障可以部分缓解，但探测器的有限时间分辨率构成了进一步障碍。本文旨在探究如何通过初始态不均匀性控制量子跳变之间的等待时间统计，从而缓解探测器分辨率问题。

Method: 考虑一个具有边界时间晶相的集体监测自旋模型，通过将系综划分为两个旋转角度为θ的子系综引入不均匀性，分析不同θ下的等待时间统计、纠缠标度行为以及测量诱导相变的存在性。

Result: 测量诱导相变在非均匀性下仍然存在，具有不同的纠缠标度 regime；等待时间随θ增加而增长，标度为1/N但前因子被增强数个量级；在反平行极限θ=π下等待时间保持有限，完全解决了分辨率障碍；但纠缠饱和时间显著延长，部分重新引入后选择屏障。

Conclusion: 探测器分辨率与后选择开销之间存在根本性的权衡关系，这一发现对实验观测测量诱导现象具有直接影响。

Abstract: In collective dissipative spin systems, the postselection barrier can be partially mitigated; however, a further obstacle may be posed by the finite temporal resolution of detectors. In this work, we investigate how initial-state inhomogeneities can control waiting-time statistics between quantum jumps, thereby mitigating the detector-resolution problem. We consider a collectively monitored spin model with a boundary time-crystalline phase, introducing inhomogeneity by partitioning the ensemble into two subsystems rotated by an angle $θ$. We find that the measurement-induced phase transition survives under inhomogeneities, with different entanglement scaling regimes. The waiting time increases with $θ$, scaling as $1/N$ but with a prefactor strongly enhanced by orders of magnitude, and in the anti-aligned limit $θ= π$ it remains finite, fully resolving the resolution barrier. This mitigation, however, comes at a cost: the entanglement saturation time becomes significantly longer, partially reintroducing the postselection barrier. Our results highlight a trade-off between detector resolution and postselection overhead, with direct implications for the experimental observation of measurement-induced phenomena.

</details>


### [126] [Decoupling band topology from criticality in bosonic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01334)
*Mariam Ughrelidze,Lorenza Viola,Emilio Cobanera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文重新定义了二次玻色哈密顿量（QBH）系统中临界性的本质,将其与参数空间中的动力学稳定性边界（而非热力学稳定性边界）联系起来。研究发现,动力学稳定区域的边界点可以是异常点（通常与长程关联相关）或Krein碰撞点（关联可长可短）。通过以玻色SSH模型为例,论文展示了动力学稳定区域可被辛Berry相整数标记,拓扑相变发生在Krein碰撞线上,且在动力学不稳定区域仍存在拓扑分类和体-边界对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典热力学稳定性视角不足以解释QBH系统中的临界行为,需要从动力学稳定性角度重新理解临界性的起源和分类。

Method: 利用伪厄米动力学系统的辅助方法求解海森堡运动方程,结合具体模型（玻色SSH模型）分析和一般性论证,研究动力学稳定性边界（异常点和Krein碰撞）与能带拓扑、边界物理的相互作用。

Result: 1) 揭示了动力学稳定性边界与热力学稳定性边界的分离;2) 识别了两种边界类型（异常点和Krein碰撞）并表征其关联性质;3) 发现动力学稳定区域可被辛Berry相整数标记;4) 证明拓扑相变线与Krein碰撞线重合;5) 证明在动力学不稳定区域仍存在拓扑分类和体-边界对应。

Conclusion: 基于基本指数理论的玻色拓扑物理对动力学稳定性不敏感,因而也对非相互作用临界性不敏感。

Abstract: A new understanding of criticality in systems described by quadratic bosonic Hamiltonians (QBHs) ties the emergence of long-range correlations to boundaries of dynamical, not thermodynamical, stability in the parameter space. This separation occurs because the solution of the Heisenberg equations of motion is determined by an auxiliary pseudo- Hermitian dynamical system. The boundary points of a region of dynamical stability can be either exceptional points, generically associated with long-range correlations, or Krein collisions, where correlations can be either long- or short-range. We investigate the interplay of this landscape of possibilities with band topology and boundary physics, by relying on both specific examples and general arguments. The examples stem from a two-parameter, thermodynamically unstable family of QBHs obtained from the bosonic Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model by breaking particle conservation while preserving a chiral pseudo-symmetry. The dynamically stable regime breaks up into different regions labeled by an integer-valued symplectic analogue of the Berry phase. The topological phase transition is a line of Krein collisions, which coincides with the closing of a band gap at zero and causes the localization length of the topologically mandated boundary zero modes to diverge before disappearing. In the unstable regime, we show that the chiral pseudo-symmetry of our model induces, despite the broken particle-number symmetry, enough structure on its associated dynamical matrices to support a topological classification and a bulk-boundary correspondence, independently of dynamical stability. This strongly suggests that bosonic topological physics extracted from basic index theory is insensitive to dynamical stability and, a posteriori, to non-interacting criticality.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Trajectory Entanglement in Seeded Boundary Time Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01335)
*Mohammad Jafari,Fernando Iemini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了边界时间晶体（BTC）通过集体耗散通道将时间晶体行为“播种”到静态自旋系综时的纠缠动力学，发现了两种截然不同的相：播种BTC相中稳态纠缠熵随系统规模N增长并伴有宏观涨落，而非播种静态相中纠缠及其涨落随N指数衰减，揭示了由播种机制驱动的测量诱导相变（MIPT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究边界时间晶体在通过集体耗散通道将其时间晶体行为播种到原本静态的自旋系综时，纠缠如何在系综间传播，以及这一过程中涌现的量子相变。

Method: 通过分析量子轨迹下的纠缠动力学，研究不同自旋系综间随时间演化的纠缠增长及其稳态性质，区分播种BTC相与非播种静态相的纠缠行为差异。

Result: 发现两种截然不同的标度行为：播种BTC相中稳态纠缠熵随N增长且沿轨迹存在宏观涨落，而非播种静态相中纠缠及其涨落随N指数衰减，表明该系统存在测量诱导相变。

Conclusion: 耗散播种机制可作为开放多体系统中控制量子关联的有效手段，该模型无需后选择壁垒，具有直接实验可行性。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement dynamics along quantum trajectories during the seeding of time-crystalline order in a boundary time crystal (BTC). Specifically, how entanglement spreads among different spin ensembles when a BTC attempts to seed its time-crystalline behavior onto otherwise static spin ensembles, through a collective dissipative channel. We analyse both the dynamical growth of entanglement in time and the steady-state properties of the system. Our results reveal two fundamentally distinct regimes. In the seeded BTC phase, the steady-state entanglement entropy between the ensembles grows with system size $N$, accompanied by macroscopic fluctuations along the trajectories. In contrast, in the non-seeded static phase, both the steady-state entanglement and its fluctuations decay exponentially with $N$. The model thus features a measurement-induced phase transition (MIPT) driven by the seeding mechanism. Furthermore, these findings establish dissipative seeding as a powerful mechanism for controlling quantum correlations in open many-body systems, with direct experimental relevance to this class of model without a postselection barrier.

</details>


### [128] [Mechanistic Interpretability and Causal Feature Steering of Neural Quantum States via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01336)
*Zihao Qi,Christopher Earls*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用稀疏自编码器（Sparse Autoencoders）系统分析神经量子态（NQS）的内部激活，发现其残差流中的特征与物理可观测量（如序参量、交错磁化强度等）高度相关，且这些特征可因果性地调控对应可观测量，表明NQS编码了可解释的物理内部表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经量子态（NQS）作为变分拟设能够准确预测物理可观测量，但其内部机制尚不明确——即NQS在仅受变分目标优化的条件下，如何捕捉从未被显式优化的物理可观测量。作者旨在揭示NQS的内部表示机理。

Method: 采用稀疏自编码器从NQS的残差流中提取潜在特征；通过相关性分析验证这些特征与物理可观测量（序参量、交错磁化强度、半链关联函数）的关联；通过事后干预实验（扰动单个特征）检验特征对观测量的因果影响。

Result: 所提取的特征在无监督条件下与物理可观测量高度相关；针对单个特征的后训练干预能够平滑单调地调控对应的物理可观测量，同时几乎不改变变分能量。

Conclusion: NQS并非单纯的黑箱函数逼近器，而是编码了可解释的物理内部表示。该方法为NQS提供了诊断与干预工具，为构建更可靠、透明的NQS奠定了可解释性基础。

Abstract: Neural Quantum States (NQS) are a remarkably expressive class of variational ansätze for quantum many-body wavefunctions, yet little is understood about their internal mechanisms: trained on variational objectives alone, how do NQS accurately capture physical observables that they have never been explicitly optimized for? In this work, we present a systematic approach to analyze the internal activations of NQS using sparse autoencoders. We extract features from the residual stream and demonstrate that these features strongly correlate with physical observables such as order parameters, staggered magnetization, and half-chain correlators, across both ground state representation and real-time dynamics. Remarkably, the discovery of these features is entirely unsupervised, with no physical labels provided. We further establish that such features causally affect the corresponding observables predicted by NQS, by showing that targeted, post-training intervention on a \textit{single} feature smoothly and monotonically steers the corresponding observable, while leaving the variational energy nearly unchanged. These results demonstrate that NQS are not merely functional approximators, but encode rich, interpretable internal representations of physical information. Our approach provides both a diagnostic and an intervention tool for NQS, and serves as a foundation for using mechanistic interpretability towards more reliable, transparent NQS.

</details>


### [129] [Design rules for fault-tolerant multi-gate teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01342)
*Mathys Rennela*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出多门隐形传态（MGT）方法，通过1 ebit扇出电路将n个远程门打包，相对于顺序门隐形传态节省n-1对纠缠。代价是相关失效模式：单网络故障传播为权重n的Pauli错误。推导了容错数据包大小设计规则：旋转表面码距离d时，相关感知解码器下n_max = ceil(d/2)，朴素解码器下n_max = floor(d/2)。PyMatching仿真表明标准MWPM解码器自然纠正相关错误；当网络-本地噪声比γ = p_net/p_gate高达100时，数据包在中高γ下匹配或超越逐链接顺序的LER，优势随γ和d增长，纠缠成本从n ebits降至1。当网络是瓶颈（γ≫1）时数据包化胜出；γ≈1时本地扇出门开销抵消网络节省。无需自定义解码器，电路级噪声模型已编码相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式量子计算中，远程门操作需要消耗纠缠资源。传统顺序门隐形传态每执行一个远程门消耗一对纠缠，成本高昂。本文旨在通过扇出电路将多个远程门打包到单个纠缠对上，在保持容错性的同时大幅降低纠缠资源消耗。

Method: 提出多门隐形传态（MGT）方案，利用1-ebit扇出量子电路将n个远程门打包。通过分析旋转表面码下的相关错误传播，推导最大可打包门数n_max的设计规则。使用PyMatching进行仿真验证，采用标准MWPM解码器，评估不同网络-本地噪声比γ下的逻辑错误率（LER）。

Result: 1) 相关感知解码器下n_max = ceil(d/2)，朴素解码器下n_max = floor(d/2)；2) 标准MWPM解码器自然纠正相关错误；3) 在γ高达100时，数据包化在中高γ下匹配或超越顺序方案，优势随γ和d增大；4) 纠缠成本从n ebits降至1 ebit；5) γ≈1时本地扇出门开销抵消网络节省。

Conclusion: 多门隐形传态能够在保持容错性的前提下，有效降低分布式量子计算中的纠缠资源消耗。建议噪声感知的分布式电路编译器优先采用扇出数据包化方案。当网络是瓶颈（γ≫1）时，数据包化具有显著优势；当γ≈1时则需权衡本地开销。

Abstract: Multi-gate teleportation (MGT) packages $n$ remote gates into a single ebit via a 1-ebit fan-out quantum circuit, saving $n{-}1$ entangled pairs relative to sequential gate teleportation. The cost is a correlated failure mode: a single network fault propagates through the fan-out tree, injecting a weight-$n$ Pauli error. We derive a design rule for fault-tolerant packet sizes, $\nmax^{\text{corr}}(d) = \lceil d/2 \rceil$ for rotated surface codes of distance~$d$ with a correlation-aware decoder ($\nmax^{\text{naive}} = \lfloor d/2 \rfloor$ without), bounding how many gates can be packaged whilst preserving fault tolerance. Simulation with PyMatching shows that the standard MWPM decoder built from the packet circuit's noise model naturally corrects the correlated error: at network-to-local noise ratios $γ= \pnet/\pgate$ up to $100$, the packet matches or surpasses the per-link sequential LER at moderate-to-high $γ$, with the advantage growing with both $γ$ and $d$, whilst reducing the entanglement cost from $n$ ebits to~$1$. Packetisation wins when the network is the bottleneck ($γ\gg 1$); at $γ\approx 1$ the $n{-}1$ extra local fan-out gates offset the network savings. No custom decoder is required: the circuit-level noise model already encodes the correlation. These results enable noise-aware distributed circuit compilers to favour fan-out packetisation without sacrificing fault tolerance.

</details>


### [130] [Decohered toric code under quantum damping noise and its mapping to a classical spin model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01364)
*Nihar Ranjan Dash,Sanjoy Dutta,R. Srikanth,Subhashish Banerjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究环面码在广义振幅阻尼（GAD）和压缩广义振幅阻尼（SGAD）信道下的性质，通过双重希尔伯特空间形式将退相干环面码映射到统计力学模型，并通过Pauli twirling将GAD/SGAD信道映射为随机Pauli型错误，得到逻辑失效概率与温度、压缩参数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子纠错中，退相干通道常包含阻尼、热效应和非马尔可夫效应，标准Pauli错误模型不足以描述这些 realistic 噪声。本文旨在系统研究环面码在GAD和SGAD这类 realistic 阻尼错误信道下的表现。

Method: 1) 使用双重希尔伯特空间形式将GAD和SGAD信道下的退相干环面码映射到统计力学模型；2) 通过Pauli twirling将GAD和SGAD信道转化为非对称 depolarizing 信道（随机Pauli型错误），得到逻辑失效概率与温度、压缩参数的关系；3) 将GAD和SGAD信道的信道参数与统计力学模型的自旋耦合常数建立关联。

Result: 成功将阻尼信道下的环面码映射到统计力学模型，并建立了信道参数与自旋耦合常数的对应关系；通过Pauli twirling得到了等效的随机Pauli错误模型及逻辑失效概率。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究 realistic 阻尼噪声下拓扑纠错码的性能提供了理论框架，揭示了阻尼信道与统计力学模型的深刻联系，有助于理解非理想噪声环境下的量子纠错阈值。

Abstract: We investigate properties of toric codes under realistic damping error channels, which include squeezing, thermal and non-Markovian effects. First, we map the decohered toric code under the generalized amplitude-damping (GAD) and the squeezed generalized amplitude-damping (SGAD) channels to the statistical-mechanical models using the double Hilbert-space formalism. Second, we map the action of the GAD and SGAD channels on the toric code to stochastic Pauli-type errors via Pauli twirling, yielding asymmetric depolarizing channels, and obtain the logical failure probabilities as a function of temperature and squeezing. In both cases, we relate the channel parameters of the GAD and SGAD channels to the spin-coupling constants of the statistical-mechanical model.

</details>


### [131] [Bit flips are erasures in dissipative cat qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01375)
*Filippo Ferrari,Joachim Cohen,Vincenzo Savona,Fabrizio Minganti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了耗散稳定猫量子比特中的比特翻转并非无声的逻辑错误，而是通过光子爆发可被检测的擦除事件，为自主量子纠错提供了内置故障监测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主量子纠错中耗散事件会向输出通道发射信号，但这些信号通常被丢弃，其是否包含逻辑故障的有用信息尚不清楚。

Method: 使用量子轨迹方法分析耗散稳定猫量子比特，结合过去量子态和数分辨主方程，建立量化逻辑故障可检测性的通用框架。

Result: 比特翻转伴随强时间局域化光子爆发；光子计数和零差监测可宣告逻辑信息丢失；发射信号可作为自主量子纠错的故障监测器。

Conclusion: 耗散稳定猫量子比特的比特翻转是擦除事件而非无声错误，工程化储层的发射信号可作为自主QEC的内置故障监测器，将稀少逻辑故障转化为解码器可用的擦除信息，降低容错开销。

Abstract: Autonomous quantum error correction (QEC) stabilizes a logical manifold through dissipative events that emit into output channels, which are typically accessible to measurement. These signals are often discarded, and whether they contain useful information about logical failures remains generally unclear. Using quantum trajectories, we show that in dissipatively stabilized cat qubits bit flips are not silent logical errors: each flip is accompanied by a strong, time-localized photon burst from the dissipative buffer. Photon counting and homodyne monitoring can therefore herald the loss of logical information without interrupting the autonomous stabilization: bit flips in dissipative cat qubits are erasures. More broadly, our results show that the emitted signals of engineered reservoirs can act as built-in failure monitors for autonomous QEC, turning rare logical faults into erasures available to a decoder and reducing fault-tolerance overhead. To this end, we develop a general framework, based on past quantum states and number-resolved master equations, to quantify the detectability of such logical failures in autonomous QEC from the emitted signal.

</details>


### [132] [Coherent states in minimal-length Quantum Mechanics: inequivalent characterizations and emergent squeezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01393)
*Giuseppe Gaetano Luciano,Pasquale Bosso,Daniel Chemisana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了最小长度量子力学中一维谐振子的相干态，发现其标准表征（湮灭算符本征态、位移真空态、最小不确定波包）在推广不确定原理（GUP）下不再等价，并揭示了相空间轨迹的非平凡形变和本征压缩机制等独特半经典特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GUP的唯象学效应已被广泛探索，但最小长度量子力学中相干态的一致表征仍然缺失，亟需建立统一框架。

Method: 系统分析一维谐振子相干态的三种标准表征在GUP下的等价性，比较广义相干态与GUP饱和态的动力学及半经典行为。

Result: 发现三种相干态表征在存在最小长度时不再等价；GUP效应导致相空间轨迹发生非平凡形变，并产生普通量子力学中不存在的本征压缩机制。

Conclusion: 建立了GUP量子理论中相干性的统一框架，识别了最小长度物理的独特半经典特征，为探测量子引力效应开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Several approaches to quantum gravity suggest the emergence of a fundamental minimal length at the Planck scale. In quantum mechanics, this feature is naturally encoded through deformations of the Heisenberg algebra, leading to the Generalized Uncertainty Principle (GUP). While the phenomenological implications of GUP have been extensively explored, a consistent characterization of coherent states in minimal-length quantum mechanics remains elusive. In this work, we present a systematic analysis of coherent states for the one-dimensional harmonic oscillator. We show that the canonical equivalence among their standard characterizations - as eigenstates of the annihilation operator, displaced vacuum states and minimum-uncertainty wave packets - is generically lost in the presence of a minimal length. We then investigate the dynamical and semiclassical consequences of this inequivalence by comparing the evolution of generalized coherent states with that of states saturating the GUP. In particular, we demonstrate that minimal-length effects induce nontrivial deformations of phase-space trajectories and give rise to an intrinsic squeezing mechanism with no counterpart in ordinary quantum mechanics. These results establish a unified framework for coherence in GUP-based quantum theories and identify distinctive semiclassical signatures of minimal-length physics, opening a new avenue for probing quantum-gravitational effects.

</details>


### [133] [High-Precision Calibration Workflow Achieves Above $99.9\%$ CZ Gate Fidelity on a Scalable Superconducting Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01422)
*Huili Zhang,Meiling Li,Shuang Yang,Yaqing Feng,Yulong Li,Cheng Chen,Pei Liu,Guangming Xue,Haifeng Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种闭环工作流，利用ELEA和CAFE电路，在84量子比特处理器上首次实现了保真度超过99.9%的CZ门，并将相干误差抑制到0.007%，同时展示了72个CZ门的中位保真度为99.25%，并实现了长达9小时的自动校准稳定性监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真通用双量子比特门是构建容错量子计算机的关键。在可扩展超导处理器中，缩短的相干时间会引入更多非相干误差，而在受限的误差预算下，参数偏差导致的相干误差容忍度降低，因此需要更精确的校准方法。

Method: 采用闭环工作流，结合回波泄漏误差放大（ELEA）和重新利用的上下文感知保真度估计（CAFE）电路，抑制非计算态布居泄漏，并实现自动校准。

Result: 在84量子比特处理器上CZ门保真度首次超过99.9%，相干误差抑制到0.007%；72个CZ门中位保真度达99.25%；9小时对比监测实验验证了自动校准的增强稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作流为超导量子系统的高精度、自动化量子计算提供了一条高效路径，且所有结果均在完全国产平台上实现。

Abstract: High-fidelity universal two-qubit gates are critical for building fault-tolerant quantum computers. In scalable superconducting processors, shortened coherence times introduce more incoherent errors in gate operations. With a constrained error budget, there is reduced tolerance for coherent errors stemming from parameter deviations. In this work, we develop a closed-loop workflow to enhance the CZ gate calibration precision. Utilizing the echoed leakage error amplification (ELEA) and the repurposed context-aware fidelity estimation (CAFE) circuits, we suppress the population leakage to non-computational states, and, for the first time, demonstrate a CZ gate fidelity exceeding $99.9\%$ on an 84-qubit processor, with coherent error suppressed to $0.007\%$. Meanwhile, we obtain a median fidelity of $99.25\%$ among 72 CZ gates, demonstrating that the workflow can be generalized to the calibration of parallel CZ gates. Finally, we realize automated calibration and observe enhanced stability of the CZ gate throughout 9-hour comparative monitoring experiments. Our results, realized on a completely domestic platform, establish an efficient and automated route to quantum computation with superconducting quantum systems.

</details>


### [134] [Surface code logical operations on a superconducting quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01473)
*Weiping Lin,Shaojun Guo,Yuwei Ma,Zhengzhong Yi,Kai Zhang,Jiahao Bei,Jianbin Cai,Sirui Cao,Danning Chen,Guoben Chen,Jianguo Chen,Kefu Chen,Xiawei Chen,Zhe Chen,Zhiyuan Chen,Zihua Chen,Wenhao Chu,Hui Deng,Xun Ding,Zhuzhengqi Ding,Yajie Du,Bo Fan,Daojin Fan,Yuanhao Fu,Dongxin Gao,Ming Gong,Jiacheng Gui,Cheng Guo,Lianchen Han,Tan He,Linyin Hong,Yisen Hu,He-Liang Huang,Yong-Heng Huo,Chenyan Jiang,Lei Jiang,Tao Jiang,Wei Jiang,Zuokai Jiang,Dayu Li,Dongdong Li,Jiaqi Li,Jinjin Li,Junyun Li,Shaowei Li,Wei Li,Xu Li,Yuan Li,Yuhuai Li,Futian Liang,Nanxing Liao,Jin Lin,Maliang Liu,Yancheng Liu,Haoxin Lou,Kailiang Nan,Meijuan Nie,Le Niu,Wenyi Peng,Haoran Qian,Hao Rong,Tao Rong,Yanyan Shao,Huiyan Shen,Qiong Shen,Ganlin Song,Hong Su,Feifan Su,Chenyin Sun,De Sun,Liangchao Sun,Tianzuo Sun,Yingxiu Sun,Yimeng Tan,Jun Tan,Shibiao Tang,Yueyang Tang,Wenbing Tu,Jiafei Wang,Biao Wang,Chang Wang,Chen Wang,Chu Wang,Jian Wang,Rui Wang,Shengtao Wang,Xinzhe Wang,Zhi Wang,Zuolin Wei,Gang Wu,Yulin Wu,Hongjun Xia,Shouzhong Xia,Shiyong Xie,Zhilin Xie,Liang Xiong,Jianping Xu,Yan Xu,Yu Xu,Chun Xue,Kai Yan,Xin Yan,Weifeng Yang,Xinpeng Yang,Yang Yang,Yicheng Yang,Yangsen Ye,Zhenping Ye,Jianghan Yin,Chong Ying,Jiale Yu,Qinjing Yu,Chen Zha,Shaoyu Zhan,Cha Zhang,Fang Zhang,Haibin Zhang,He Zhang,Huanmei Zhang,Kaili Zhang,Qipeng Zhang,Shijia Zhang,Wen Zhang,Xin Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Yongzhuo Zhang,Ziying Zhang,Guming Zhao,Xintao Zhao,Youwei Zhao,Zhong Zhao,Luyuan Zheng,Fei Zhou,Liang Zhou,Naibin Zhou,Chengjun Zhu,Qingling Zhu,Yongsheng Zhu,Guihong Zou,Haonan Zou,Qiang Zhang,Chao-Yang Lu,Jianxin Chen,Cheng-Zhi Peng,Fusheng Chen,XiaoBo Zhu,Jian-Wei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在107量子比特超导处理器上实验实现了基于补丁的表面码逻辑操作，包括合并/分裂、扩展/收缩、畴壁和扭结缺陷等基元，并组合实现了逻辑态路由、CNOT门、Hadamard和相位门（构成Clifford生成集），所有操作在距离三的旋转表面码补丁上完成，采用多轮症状提取和神经网络解码，无需后选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要能够在保持量子纠错保护的同时操作编码信息的逻辑运算。在平面表面码架构中，码形变和晶格手术提供了基于局域测量的实现路径，但此前缺乏在超导量子处理器上的完整实验验证。

Method: 在107量子比特超导处理器上，首先实现可复用的基元层（合并/分裂、补丁扩展/收缩、基于畴壁和扭结缺陷的形变），然后组合这些基元实现逻辑态路由、逻辑CNOT门以及Hadamard和相位门（构成Clifford生成集）。所有操作基于距离三的旋转表面码补丁，采用多轮症状提取和神经网络解码。

Result: 成功在实验上实现了所有上述基元和组合逻辑操作，无需后选择，验证了基于补丁的表面码逻辑处理在超导量子处理器上的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作将超导表面码实验从保护的逻辑存储器推进到主动的、基于补丁的容错逻辑操作，为未来更大规模的容错量子计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation requires logical operations that manipulate encoded information while preserving quantum error-correction protection. In planar surface-code architectures, code deformation and lattice surgery provide a local, measurement-based route to such operations. Here we experimentally realize key elements of patch-based surface-code logical processing on a 107-qubit superconducting quantum processor. We first implement a reusable primitive layer comprising merge and split, patch expansion and shrinkage, and deformations mediated by domain walls and twist defects. We then compose these primitives to realize logical state routing, the logical controlled-NOT gate, and the single-qubit Hadamard and phase gates, which together form a Clifford-generating set. All operations are implemented on distance-three rotated surface-code patches with multi-round syndrome extraction and neural-network decoding, without post-selection. Our results advance superconducting surface-code experiments from protected logical memory to active, patch-based fault-tolerant logical operations.

</details>


### [135] [Quantum nonlocal correlations of anomalous weak values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01491)
*Ron Cohen,Avshalom C. Elitzur,Eliahu Cohen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了CHSH算子在预选和后选（PPS）量子系综中的反常弱值，将其视为后选择下Bell型非局域关联的广义界。研究表明后选择和纠缠是两种不同的操作资源：后选择本身可以增强关联，但纠缠是超越对应可分PPS界限所必需的，两者结合可达到最强关联。非局域弱值可作为后选择的纠缠见证器，在理想状态自适应设置下可检测任意具有非零纠缠度的纯两比特源状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究后选择与纠缠在量子关联增强中的不同角色，以及非局域弱值作为纠缠检测工具的能力。

Method: 通过固定预选和后选态之间的重叠，比较三种场景：无约束边界态、一个可分边界态、两个边界态均可分，推导每种情况下的最大弱值及对所有CHSH算子优化后的最大界。

Result: 后选择和纠缠是不同操作资源；后选择可增强关联但纠缠才可超越可分PPS界限；增强程度与优化态的并发度密切相关；非局域弱值可检测纯两比特源状态的纠缠。

Conclusion: 后选择与纠缠的结合能产生最强关联，非局域弱值可作为纠缠见证器，混合协议在量子传感、弱值放大和量子信息处理中有潜在应用。

Abstract: Violations of Bell inequalities are a hallmark of entanglement, with only entangled states capable of exceeding classical bounds in standard Bell tests. Here we analyze anomalous weak values of the CHSH operator in pre- and post-selected (PPS) quantum ensembles, treating them as generalized bounds on Bell-type nonlocal correlations in the presence of post-selection. Fixing the overlap between the pre- and post-selected states, we compare three scenarios: unrestricted boundary states, one separable boundary state, and both boundary states separable. For each case, we derive both the maximal weak value for a fixed Bell operator and the maximal bound obtained by further optimizing over all CHSH operators. Our results show that post-selection and entanglement are distinct operational resources: post-selection alone can enhance correlations, but entanglement is necessary to exceed the corresponding separable PPS bounds, and their combination yields the strongest attainable correlations. We further show that the enhancement beyond the separable bound closely tracks the concurrence of the states that optimize the bounds, identifying entanglement as the source of the additional correlation strength. Finally, we show that nonlocal weak values provide post-selected entanglement witnesses, and we give a constructive protocol that detects every pure two-qubit source state with nonzero concurrence in the ideal state-adapted setting, even in regimes where the corresponding standard CHSH entanglement test is inconclusive. In this state-adapted setting, we explicitly construct the post-selection and CHSH measurements that achieve the largest possible separation from the separable PPS bound. More broadly, our results motivate hybrid protocols that combine post-selection and entanglement, with possible applications to improved quantum sensing, weak-value amplification, and quantum information processing.

</details>


### [136] [Lamb Shift of a Static Atom Facing a Rotating Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01495)
*César D. Fosco,Fernando C. Lombardo,Francisco D. Mazzitelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转平面表面对静态原子Lamb位移的修正，揭示了自旋和轨道两种贡献机制，并分析了不同材料（石墨烯、Drude导体、等离子体导体、掺杂半导体）下的Casimir-Polder相互作用增强或减弱效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究旋转表面对原子Lamb位移的修正机制，区分轨道与自旋角动量贡献，并分析不同材料下旋转对Casimir-Polder相互作用的影响。

Method: 推导了基于角向多普勒位移反射系数的Lamb位移通用公式，展开至Ω²阶，识别出轨道贡献（∝(Ωρ)²）和自旋贡献（∝(aΩ)²），并应用于石墨烯、Drude导体、等离子体导体和掺杂半导体等材料。

Result: 旋转增强石墨烯和金属表面的Casimir-Polder相互作用，但减弱掺杂半导体的相互作用；旋转会引入原子能级线宽，可作为量子摩擦的光谱信号。

Conclusion: 旋转效应通过自旋与轨道两种机制修正Lamb位移，材料等离子体频率与近场尺度1/a的竞争关系决定增强或减弱效应，且存在阈值角速度激发量子摩擦光谱特征。

Abstract: We study how the Lamb shift of a static atom is modified when a nearby planar body rotates rigidly about its normal while the atom is held at a fixed distance $a$. We derive a general formula for the shift in terms of the angularly Doppler-shifted reflection coefficients of the surface, valid for any axially symmetric planar material. Expanding the result to second order in the angular velocity $Ω$, we identify two independent contributions associated with the orbital and spin components of the electromagnetic angular momentum. The orbital contribution, proportional to $(Ωρ)^2$, reproduces locally the Lamb shift induced by a surface translating at the tangential velocity $Ωρ$, whereas the spin contribution, proportional to $(aΩ)^2$, originates from the rotational Doppler shift of the photon helicity and survives even on the rotation axis. We first illustrate the formalism using a graphene sheet and then apply it to finite-thickness Drude and plasma conductors and to doped semiconductors. Rotation enhances the Casimir-Polder interaction for graphene and metallic surfaces, whereas it weakens it for doped semiconductors, depending on whether the carrier plasma frequency reaches the near-field scale $1/a$. Above a threshold angular velocity, the atomic level also acquires a finite linewidth, providing a spectroscopic signature of quantum friction.

</details>


### [137] [Symmetry conservation with Trotterization and Quantum Phase Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01560)
*Edith Leal-Sánchez,Fanny Vain,Jong-Kwon Ha,Ryan J. MacDonell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过厄米激发算子表达哈密顿量以保持阿贝尔对称性，并开发了“算子折纸”技术处理非阿贝尔对称性，通过正交投影和幺正旋转减少Trotter化误差，在小分子测试中发现自旋和电子数对称性守恒的算子池在二阶Trotter化下误差减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Trotter化在处理量子化学哈密顿量时，由于算子非对易性引入误差，且碎片通常不守恒哈密顿量的对称性，这会影响时间演化和量子相位估计等算法的准确性。

Method: 1) 将哈密顿量用厄米激发算子表示，这些算子在任意编码下映射为对易泡利字符串之和，从而守恒阿贝尔群对称性；2) 针对非阿贝尔群对称性，提出“算子折纸”（operator kirigami）技术，通过正交投影切割非对易算子之和，并利用幺正旋转折叠项。

Result: 小分子和基组测试表明，电子数和自旋对称性守恒的算子池会导致较大误差，但随着分子增大误差减小，且二阶Trotter化可抵消该误差。

Conclusion: 展示了通过利用电子结构理论中守恒对称性的工具，可以在经典计算机上测试量子计算算法的潜力。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms for quantum chemistry and other many-body fermionic systems work by expressing the Hamiltonian in a basis of qubits and fragmenting the Hamiltonian into a sum of products of Pauli operators whose exponentials are easily encoded on a quantum device. Applying the product of exponentials, known as Trotterization, leads to an error associated with the non-commutativity of operators. This error can lead to breaking the symmetries of the Hamiltonian because the fragments are not symmetry conserving in general. Nonetheless, many algorithms for time evolution rely on Trotterization, including time evolution and quantum phase estimation. We show that we can express the Hamiltonian in terms of Hermitian excitation operators which map to sums of commuting Pauli strings for any encoding and conserve symmetries corresponding to Abelian groups of symmetry operators. Symmetries corresponding to non-Abelian groups, on the other hand, are not fully conserved by Trotterized Hermitian excitation operators, so we developed ``operator kirigami'' to cut the sum of non-commuting operators by orthogonal projection and to fold terms together using unitary rotations. We tested pools of operators for small molecules and basis sets, and found that electron number and spin symmetry conserving pools led to greater errors that decreased for larger molecules and were negated with second-order Trotterization. Our work shows the potential for testing quantum computing algorithms on classical computers by adapting tools used in electronic structure theory with conserved symmetries.

</details>


### [138] [An Information-Theoretic Principle for Optimal Quantum Encoding: Tight Frames and Equiangular Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01564)
*Farhad Farokhi,Shuixin Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度证明，量子计算推理过程的精度受限于经典数据经量子编码后的最大量子泄漏，且该泄漏量可作为编码策略的通用任务无关质量度量。最优通用编码策略由纯态实现：当量子比特充足时，基编码最优；系统维度小时，相位编码最优，其中任意紧框架（平均态为最大混合态）均最优，而等角紧框架（如qubit trine、正则单纯形、SIC-POVM）是唯一对称的最优编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子辅助统计推断中经典数据的最优编码策略提供信息论层面的理论指导，建立编码质量的通用度量标准。

Method: 通过信息论推导证明最大量子泄漏作为编码质量的通用上界；分析纯态、基编码、相位编码、紧框架和等角紧框架在不同条件下的最优性。

Result: 最大量子泄漏是编码策略的通用质量度量；纯态实现最优编码；基编码在量子比特充足时最优；相位编码在维度小时最优；紧框架（平均态为最大混合态）均最优；等角紧框架是唯一对称最优编码。

Conclusion: 量子编码的最大量子泄漏可作为统计推断中编码策略的通用任务无关度量，不同资源条件下存在对应的最优编码结构。

Abstract: Optimal encoding of classical data for quantum-assisted statistical inference is investigated from an information-theoretic perspective. We prove that the accuracy of any quantum-computing inference procedure is upper bounded by the maximal quantum leakage from the classical data through its quantum encoding, establishing leakage as a universal, task-agnostic quality measure for encoders. This demonstrates that the maximal quantum leakage is a universal measure of the quality of the encoding strategy for statistical inference as it only depends on the quantum encoding of the data and not the inference task itself. The optimal universal encoding strategy, i.e., an encoding strategy that maximizes the maximal quantum leakage, is proved to be attained by pure states. When there are enough qubits, basis encoding is proved to be universally optimal. However, when the dimension of the system is small, phase encoding is optimal. For the latter, any tight frame, any ensemble whose average state is the maximally mixed state, is in fact optimal. Within tight frames, equiangular tight frames (ETFs) are distinguished as the uniquely symmetric optimal encodings, i.e., they saturate the Welch lower bound on pairwise overlaps and possess a self-referential optimal measurement. Prominent special cases are the qubit trine, the regular simplex, and symmetric informationally complete positive operator-valued measures (SIC-POVMs), for which the ETF structure and explicit codeword constructions are provided. Numerical examples are presented to validate the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [139] [A Validation Framework for Quantum Simulation of Spin Dynamics against Inelastic Neutron Scattering and Classical Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01568)
*Gilles Buchs,Elaine Wong,Anshumitra Baul,Kathleen E. Hamilton,Arnab Banerjee,Stephan Eidenbenz,Gábor B. Halász,Keerthi Kumaran,Thomas Maier,Thomas Naughton,Elijah Pelofske,Vincent Russo,Allen Scheie,Yigit Subasi,D. Alan Tennant,Akram Touil,Travis S. Humble,Andrew T. Sornborger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子模拟动力学自旋响应的交叉管道验证框架，通过非弹性中子散射实验和经典多体模拟作为锚点，结合正反向观测映射、协方差/重采样不确定性传播、鲁棒性测试和度量层级，实现了对量子模拟在管道、求解器和模型三个层面的分层验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟动力学自旋响应的定量验证面临重大挑战：实验、经典模拟和量子模拟产生的原生可观测量不同，难以直接比较。随着量子模拟协议从理论进入硬件级基准测试阶段，亟需能够在经典难以处理的体制中也能进行有效验证的框架。

Method: 开发了交叉管道验证框架，包含：(1) 显式的正向和逆向观测映射；(2) 基于协方差或重采样的不确定性传播；(3) 结构化失真的鲁棒性测试；(4) 互补度量族的分层体系。引入执行器感知的反馈逻辑以改进一致性，同时保留失配的物理来源。

Result: 该框架能够区分随机不确定性和鲁棒性引起的失真，并将两者显式地贯穿整个比较链，在管道、求解器和模型三个层面提供度量的不确定性和失真信息以支持分层验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子模拟的定量验证提供了系统化方法，未来可扩展到上游不确定性和失真建模、自适应反馈、非对称验证、容错工作流以及社区可重复验证基础设施等方面。

Abstract: Quantitative validation of quantum simulations of dynamical spin response remains challenging because experiment, classical simulation, and quantum simulation do not produce the same native observables. This problem has become increasingly important as quantum simulation protocols for dynamical response have progressed from theory to hardware-level benchmarking against neutron-scattering data, while the longer term goal is validation in regimes that may eventually become classically intractable, including in future fault-tolerant implementations. Here, we develop a cross-pipeline validation framework for quantum simulation, using inelastic neutron scattering and classical many-body simulation as complementary experimental and computational anchors, based on explicit forward and inverse observable maps, covariance- or resampling-based uncertainty propagation, robustness tests for structured distortion, and a hierarchy of complementary metric families. The framework distinguishes stochastic uncertainty from robustness-induced distortion, carries both explicitly through the comparison chain, and uses the resulting metric-level uncertainty and distortion information to support layered validation at the pipeline, solver, and model levels. We also introduce actuator-aware feedback logic aimed at improving agreement without obscuring the physical origin of any remaining mismatch. We close by outlining future extensions of this methodology, including upstream uncertainty and distortion modeling, adaptive feedback, asymmetric validation beyond full classical benchmarking, fault-tolerant workflows, and community infrastructure for reproducible validation.

</details>


### [140] [Structured Factorization Approaches for Quantum State Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01608)
*Zhen Qin,Joseph M. Lukens,Brian T. Kirby,Zhihui Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的结构化分解框架，通过将密度矩阵参数化为 $FF^\dagger$ 并约束因子 $F$ 属于结构化模型类，实现可扩展的量子态层析。该框架涵盖多种先验结构（低秩、张量网络、神经网络），并开发了投影梯度下降与基于幂法的快速无步长优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析（QST）的复杂度随系统规模指数增长，需要利用先验结构（如低秩性、张量网络、神经网络表示）来实现可扩展性，但缺乏统一的理论与算法框架。

Method: 提出结构化分解框架，密度矩阵参数化为 $FF^\dagger$，因子 $F$ 约束于结构化模型类（Cholesky分解、低秩矩阵、矩阵乘积算子、MLP/Transformer神经密度算子等）。在此框架上，将QST表述为因子空间上的优化问题，提出投影梯度下降法以及基于幂法的无步长快速收敛算法（推广了Covers方法）。

Result: 对广泛的结构化量子态类，建立了最小二乘估计样本复杂度的统一统计分析。提出的投影梯度下降法可适配多种结构参数化与重构目标；幂法算法实现无步长快速收敛。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了多种QST先验结构，保证了物理合法性，并在样本复杂度与算法效率上均具有理论保证，为大规模量子态重构提供了通用范型。

Abstract: Since the complexity of quantum state tomography (QST) scales exponentially with system size, exploiting priors such as low-rankness, tensor-network structures, and neural-network representations is essential for scalable QST in terms of sample complexity and parameter complexity. In this paper, we introduce a unified framework, termed structured factorization, that builds on BurerMonteiro-type factorization by parametrizing the density matrix as $FF^\dagger$, where the factor $F$ is constrained to belong to a structured model class. This factorization guarantees physical validity by construction while allowing a broad range of structural priors to be incorporated directly through the choice of the factor space, ranging from the generic Cholesky decomposition to low-rank matrices, matrix product operators, and neural density operators based on multilayer perceptron and transformer architectures. Building on this structured factorization framework, we formulate QST as an optimization problem over the factor space from measurement data. We first develop a unified statistical analysis of the sample complexity of least-squares estimation for a broad class of structured quantum states. We then propose a projected gradient descent method that operates directly on the factor space and accommodates a wide range of structural parametrizations and reconstruction objectives. To further exploit the geometry of the maximum-likelihood estimation formulation and the constraints on the factors, we derive a power method that yields a step-size-free algorithm with fast convergence, recovering Covers method as a special case when the factor is unconstrained.

</details>


### [141] [Noise Suppression via Pulsed All-Optical Magnetometry with Nitrogen-Vacancy Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01615)
*Xiechen Zheng,Jeyson Támara-Isaza,John W. Blanchard,Ronald L. Walsworth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种脉冲式全光学（AO）微波-free磁强测量协议，通过在单个光脉冲内进行两次光致发光（PL）测量来抑制共模噪声，在近零磁场下实现了相比传统连续波AO技术高达10倍的低频噪声底改善，并研究了PL读出示时序和暗时间τ对AO-PL对比度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续波全光学NV色心磁强测量易受光强噪声等系统波动影响，限制了其性能。为解决这一问题，需要发展一种能有效抑制共模噪声的新型AO测量协议。

Method: 提出脉冲式全光学协议：在一个光学脉冲内进行两次PL测量（参考测量和信号测量），通过差分方式抑制共模噪声。实验在近零磁场下进行，利用NV-NV交叉弛豫产生的AO-PL对比度特征来验证协议性能。同时系统研究了PL读出示时序和光学脉冲间暗时间τ对对比度的影响。

Result: 脉冲式AO协议在近零磁场下成功解析了NV-NV交叉弛豫引起的AO-PL对比度特征，低频噪声底相比传统连续波AO技术改善了最高达10倍。最优暗时间τ随NV浓度变化而变化。

Conclusion: 脉冲式全光学协议能有效抑制共模噪声，显著提升NV色心磁强计在近零场下的信噪比。研究结果为针对不同应用场景优化NV-金刚石样品以实现高效全光学操作提供了重要指导。

Abstract: All-optical (AO) microwave-free magnetometry using nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond simplifies experimental design and broadens sample compatibility. While continuous-wave (cw) detection of AO photoluminescence (PL) changes is commonly employed, its performance is susceptible to systematic fluctuations such as optical intensity noise. To address these challenges, we introduce a pulsed AO protocol that employs two PL measurements within an optical pulse to suppress common-mode noise. At near-zero magnetic field, we experimentally demonstrate that the pulsed AO protocol resolves AO-PL contrast features arising from NV-NV cross-relaxation, achieving up to 10$\times$ improvement in the low-frequency noise floor compared to conventional cw AO techniques. We further investigate the dependence of AO-PL contrast on PL readout timing and the dark time duration $τ$ between optical pulses, with the optimal $τ$ varying based on NV concentrations. These findings provide insights into optimizing NV-diamond samples for effective AO operation across diverse applications.

</details>


### [142] [Shallow Unitary Circuits for Kramers-Wannier Dualities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01624)
*Yanting Cheng,Shang Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了对数深度的空间非局域幺正电路，实现了1D和2D中的Z₂及Zₙ Kramers-Wannier对偶性，将短程纠缠态高效映射为长程纠缠态，为量子平台上研究相变和拓扑对偶提供了纯相干路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子KW对偶是连接SRE态与LRE态的基本变换，但该对偶的实现速度极限（尤其在非局域连接下）尚不明确，因此需要探索高效实现该对偶的幺正电路。

Method: 显式构造了空间非局域、对数深度的幺正电路，分别实现1D和2D中的Z₂ KW对偶，并推广至任意Zₙ情形。电路实现的是完整的对偶映射，而非特定目标态的制备算法。

Result: 所构造的对数深度电路能够精确实现Z₂和Zₙ KW对偶，在对称（电荷中性）扇区内，将任意非不动点SRE态精确变换为其对应的LRE对偶态。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了高效、纯相干的路径，可用于在当代量子平台上探索相变和拓扑对偶。

Abstract: The quantum Kramers-Wannier (KW) duality is a fundamental transformation mapping short-range entangled (SRE) states to long-range entangled (LRE) states. While spatially local unitary circuits require linear-in-system-size depth to implement this duality, the ultimate speed limit for purely unitary circuits equipped with nonlocal connectivity remains an open question. Here, we explicitly construct logarithmic depth, spatially nonlocal unitary circuits that realize the exact $\mathbb{Z}_2$ KW dualities in both one and two spatial dimensions. We further generalize the construction to arbitrary $\mathbb{Z}_n$ KW dualities. Unlike algorithms tailored to prepare specific target states, our circuits implement complete duality maps. Within the symmetric (charge-neutral) sector, these dualities exactly transform arbitrary non-fixed-point SRE states into their corresponding LRE duals. Consequently, our results establish an efficient, purely coherent pathway for exploring phase transitions and topological dualities on modern quantum platforms.

</details>


### [143] [Chiral interaction enhanced magnon bundle emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01635)
*Zhicai Chen,Deyi Kong,Chengdeng Gou,Xiangming Hu,Fei Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在手性级联腔系统中放置量子比特和磁子，利用单向相互作用延长目标激发态寿命、抑制磁子再激发，从而提升双磁子束流的平均纯度和数量，实现高质量多磁子源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强磁子束流发射质量，解决传统方案中磁子再激发导致纯度和数量不足的问题。

Method: 将量子比特和磁子分别放入级联腔中，引入手性（单向）相互作用方案。

Result: 手性相互作用延长了目标激发态寿命，抑制了磁子再激发，提升了双磁子束流的平均纯度和数量。

Conclusion: 手性相互作用不仅能提供方向性控制，还能改善多磁子源的质量，有望应用于量子信息处理。

Abstract: In this paper, we suggest a chiral interaction scheme to enhance magnon bundle emission by placing a qubit and a magnon into a cascaded-cavity setup, respectively. It is found that the unidirectional interaction prolongs the lifetime of the target excited state, thereby suppressing the magnon re-excitation and promoting both the average purity and number of two-magnon bundles. Consequently, the chiral interaction not only offers directional control but also improves the quality of the multi-magnon source, which may find potential applications in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [144] [Bayesian Monotone Metrics for Multiparameter Quantum Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01685)
*Jianchao Zhang,Koichi Yamagata,Jun Suzuki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将Petz单调度量推广到先验平均态上，建立了贝叶斯单调度量框架，为多参数贝叶斯量子估计提供了统一的几何表述，并导出了一系列可计算的贝叶斯风险下界，这些下界严格优于相应的量子van Trees界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前多参数贝叶斯量子估计缺乏统一的几何表述形式，现有的贝叶斯风险下界在计算和紧致性方面存在局限。

Method: 通过在先验平均态上评估Petz单调度量，将整个统计意义上有效的（CPTP）量子度量族扩展到贝叶斯框架；引入量子后验均值算子和量子贝叶斯对偶Fisher信息矩阵等概念；证明对所有算子单调函数进行优化可坍缩为单参数子族。

Result: 建立了系统化的可计算贝叶斯风险下界族；证明了每个单调度量对应的下界普遍优于量子van Trees界；优化后的下界在代表性例子中严格优于贝叶斯SLD和RLD界。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯单调度量可作为贝叶斯量子估计的统一信息几何视角，为多参数设置提供系统且可计算的性能极限。

Abstract: Bayesian quantum estimation offers a finite-data framework for quantum sensing and metrology, yet a unified geometric formulation for multiparameter Bayes risk has been lacking. We introduce Bayesian monotone metrics by evaluating Petz monotone metrics on the prior-averaged state, providing a Bayesian extension of the full class of statistically meaningful (CPTP) quantum metrics. This framework yields Bayesian quantities, including quantum posterior-mean operators and a quantum Bayesian dual Fisher-information matrix, and it leads to a systematic family of computable lower bounds on the Bayes risk. The resulting bounds naturally incorporate multiparameter measurement incompatibility and, for every monotone metric in the family, we prove a universal dominance over the corresponding quantum van Trees (Bayesian Cramér--Rao) bound. Moreover, we show that optimizing over all operator monotone functions collapses to a one-parameter subfamily, turning the tightest bound into a tractable optimization with a clear geometric interpretation. In representative examples, the optimized bounds are strictly tighter than the Bayesian SLD and RLD bounds. Our results establish Bayesian monotone metrics as a unifying information-geometric perspective on Bayesian quantum estimation, enabling systematic and computable performance limits in multiparameter settings.

</details>


### [145] [Quantum Noncommutativity Uniquely Determines Relative Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01712)
*Gilad Gour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子相对熵是由单次量子态区分游戏中可加性度量唯一决定的，而经典理论允许多种散度度量，这种唯一性是量子非对易性导致的纯量子现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量子相对熵是量子信息理论中的核心概念，但存在无数满足数据处理不等式的替代散度度量，这引发了量子相对熵是否具有独特地位的基础性问题。作者试图寻找一个更尖锐的操作原理来唯一确定量子相对熵。

Method: 通过二元猜测游戏框架，考虑观察者使用最优测量区分量子态对。在可加性假设下，证明任何尊重最优测量所揭示的胜率的散度度量必须与Umegaki相对熵一致。利用量子非对易性消除经典情境中存在的自由度。

Result: 量子相对熵是唯一满足可加性且与单次量子态最优区分结果兼容的散度度量。该结果是精确的，不需要热力学极限或超可加性假设。经典理论则允许包括Rényi散度在内的连续族有效度量。

Conclusion: 量子相对熵不仅是渐近意义下的量，而是单次量子区分的唯一可加性区分度量，确立了其在量子信息理论中的基础地位，揭示了非对易性对度量空间的约束作用。

Abstract: Quantum relative entropy is a core concept in physics, governing the limits of communication, thermodynamic irreversibility and quantum resource conversion. However, the requirement that physical processes cannot increase state distinguishability, the data-processing inequality, permits an infinite family of alternative divergence measures. Here we show that quantum relative entropy is uniquely selected by a sharper operational principle. We evaluate distinguishability through binary guessing games, in which an observer discriminates between pairs of quantum states using the optimal measurement. We prove that any additive measure that respects the odds revealed by these optimal measurements must coincide with the Umegaki relative entropy. This rigidity is a purely quantum phenomenon. Whereas classical theory permits a continuous family of valid divergence measures, including Rényi divergences, quantum noncommutativity. collapses this mathematical freedom. The result is exact, requiring neither a thermodynamic limit of infinitely many copies nor super-additivity assumptions for correlated states. It establishes quantum relative entropy not merely as an asymptotic quantity, but as the unique additive distinguishability measure compatible with single-shot quantum discrimination.

</details>


### [146] [Performance of a two-mode coherent superposed channel in continuous-variable quantum teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01786)
*Deepak,Arpita Chatterjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入双模叠加算符 A=tab+ra^† b^† 作用于双模相干态，构造双模相干叠加态 |ψ⟩，通过Wigner分布和Wigner对数负性研究其非经典性和量子非高斯性，并将该态作为纠缠资源应用于Braunstein-Kimble连续变量隐形传态协议，计算了相干态和压缩态输入下的理想传态保真度，识别出超越经典阈值的参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索双模相干叠加态的非经典性与非高斯性，并研究其在连续变量量子信息处理中的应用潜力，特别是如何通过调控非经典性和非高斯性来提升量子隐形传态效率。

Method: (1) 引入双模叠加算符 A=tab+ra^† b^† 作用于双模相干态构造叠加态；(2) 利用Wigner分布和Wigner对数负性(Wigner logarithmic negativity)分析非经典性与量子非高斯性；(3) 将该态作为纠缠资源应用于Braunstein-Kimble CV隐形传态协议，计算相干态和压缩态输入的理想保真度。

Result: 识别出特定参数区域：增强的非高斯特征或增加的非经典性使传态保真度超越经典阈值。

Conclusion: 工程化双模量子态在连续变量量子信息处理中具有重要的操作意义，非高斯性和非经典性的调控可有效提升量子隐形传态性能。

Abstract: Glauber's coherent state is denoted by $\ketα$ and its two-mode extension is represented by $\ket{α,β}$. In this work, we introduce a two-mode superposition operator $A=tab+ra^\dagger b^\dagger$, whose action on the two-mode coherent state produces the two-mode coherent superposed quantum state $\ketψ=(tab+ra^\dagger b^\dagger)\ket{α,β}$. We investigate the nonclassicality and quantum non-Gaussianity of this state by means of the Wigner distribution and Wigner logarithmic negativity. Once its intrinsic nonclassical and non-Gaussian structure is established, the state is employed as the entangled resource in the Braunstein-Kimble continuous-variable (CV) teleportation protocol. We compute the ideal teleportation fidelity for coherent and squeezed inputs and analyze how the strengths of nonclassicality and non-Gaussianity influence the teleportation efficiency. Our results identify specific parameter regimes where enhanced non-Gaussian features or increased nonclassicality enable fidelities beyond the classical threshold, thereby revealing the operational significance of engineered two-mode quantum states in CV quantum information processing.

</details>


### [147] [Structure-Aware Compilation for Scalable Neutral-Atom Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01787)
*Dekuan Dong,Fengyu Zou,Hengzhun Chen,Guorui Zhu,Yingzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维中性原子阵列上结构化量子门系列的编译问题，通过利用代数结构（单比特门）和图论模型（C-Z门）优化寻址与传输调度，实验表明寻址层数最多减少至50%，原子传输操作减少约50%，QAOA电路传输成本降低超30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实中性原子硬件约束下，减少量子门编译中的寻址开销和传输开销，解决硬件级调度瓶颈。

Method: 单比特门：利用矩阵层级的代数结构实现秩一分解，减少寻址层数；C-Z门：构建图论模型形式化传输调度问题，设计高效编译算法。

Result: 单比特门系列寻址层数较朴素行列实现减少最多50%；C-Z门原子传输操作减少约50%；QAOA电路（MaxCut）传输成本平均降低超30%。

Conclusion: 中性原子硬件的物理约束可转化为代数和图论结构，将调度瓶颈转化为可处理分解与着色问题。

Abstract: We study the compilation of structured quantum gate families on two-dimensional neutral-atom arrays, aiming to reduce addressing and transport overhead under realistic hardware constraints. For single-qubit gates, we exploit the algebraic structures of gate families at the matrix level, enabling efficient rank-one decompositions over appropriate algebraic structures and thereby reducing the number of addressing layers. For controlled-Z (C-Z) gates, we formulate the transport scheduling problem using graph-theoretic models, leading to efficient compilation algorithms under realistic transport constraints. We provide provable performance guarantees for the proposed methods and validate them through extensive numerical experiments. Across representative single-qubit gate families, our methods reduce the number of addressing layers by up to a factor of two compared with naïve row- or column-wise implementations. For C-Z gates, our scheduling strategy reduces the required number of atom transport operations by approximately 50\%. When applied to QAOA circuits for MaxCut, the proposed framework reduces transport cost by more than 30\% on average. These results show that the physical constraints of neutral-atom hardware can be converted into algebraic and graph-theoretic structure, turning a hardware-level scheduling bottleneck into tractable decomposition and coloring problems.

</details>


### [148] [Interferometric characterization of the relative phase between two X-ray free-electron laser pulses using long-lived Mössbauer resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01796)
*Lukas Wolff,Jörg Evers*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Mössbauer共振的Ramsey型干涉方法，用于测量高重复频率X射线自由电子激光器（XFEL）后续变换极限脉冲之间的相对相位，从而为相干光谱学实验提供相位基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于相干性的光谱学方法中，向更高光子能量推进时，生成具有良好表征的相对延迟和相位的脉冲序列仍是一项挑战。本文旨在解决高重复频率XFEL脉冲间相对相位的测量问题。

Method: 采用Ramsey型干涉测量，通过在XFEL光束路径中（主实验上游或下游）引入长寿命Mössbauer共振，使其能够桥接XFEL脉冲之间的时间间隔，从而测量后续变换极限脉冲之间的相对相位φ。

Result: 成功实现了对高重复频率XFEL脉冲间相对相位的测量，所测得的相位可作为附加输入用于主实验的数据分析。

Conclusion: 该方法为高光子能量区域的相干光谱学提供了一种可行的相位测量方案，通过长寿命Mössbauer共振桥接脉冲间隔，有效解决了高重复频率XFEL脉冲相位表征的难题。

Abstract: Coherence-based spectroscopy methods are powerful tools to explore structure and dynamics of matter. However, towards higher photon energies, the generation of sequences of pulses with well-characterized relative delays and phases remains a challenge. Here, we introduce a method to measure the relative phase $\varphi$ between subsequent transform-limited pulses from high-repetition-rate x-ray free-electron lasers (XFELs). It is based on a Ramsey-type interference measurement, enabled by introducing long-lived Mössbauer resonances into the XFEL beam path up- or downstream a primary experiment, which allow one to bridge the temporal gap between the XFEL pulses. The measured phase can be used as additional input for the analysis of the primary experiment.

</details>


### [149] [Extracting Work from Discrete Quantum Polytropic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01811)
*Vishal Anand,Swarup Kumar Giri,Avijit Misra,Subhadip Mitra,Samyadeb Bhattacharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为非马尔可夫、含时量子热机建立了有限浴中可提取功的上界，并分析了系统-浴关联、浴非平衡态和残余相互作用能各自的热力学代价。通过量子多方腔光力学循环的验证表明：最大效率需准静态操作以利用非马尔可夫系统-浴共振，而最大功率则强制有限时间运行，且Trotter误差会以物理噪声形式抑制量子记忆效应，导致系统退化为马尔可夫Otto极限。结果表明，量子记忆资源利用与有限功率分属不同的运行体制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为含时非马尔可夫量子热机建立可提取功的严格上界，并量化系统-浴关联、浴非平衡态和残余相互作用能各自引入的热力学惩罚，从而阐明量子记忆效应在有限时间操作中的根本性限制。

Method: 解析推导了有限浴中非马尔可夫量子热机可提取功的上界；通过量子多方腔光力学循环进行操作性评估，分析准静态与有限时间两种运行模式下的效率与功率表现，并考察Trotter误差对量子记忆效应的影响。

Result: 最大效率需要准静态操作以利用非马尔可夫共振；最大功率强制有限时间运行，但Trotter误差作为物理噪声会不可逆地抑制量子记忆效应，使系统退化为马尔可夫Otto极限；有限浴相互作用的切换带来永久性热力学代价。

Conclusion: 量子记忆资源的有效利用与有限功率运行属于互斥的操作体制，实现了高效率与高功率无法兼得的基本权衡。

Abstract: We establish an upper bound on extractable work for time-dependent, non-Markovian quantum heat engines operating with finite baths. This bound analytically isolates the distinct thermodynamic penalties arising from system-bath correlations, bath non-equilibrium, and residual interaction energy. Evaluating this framework operationally via a quantum polytropic cavity-optomechanical cycle, we demonstrate that maximal efficiency requires quasi-static operation to successfully harvest coherent, non-Markovian system-bath resonances. Conversely, optimising for maximum power enforces a strict finite-time regime. Under realistic hardware constraints, this acceleration necessitates larger discrete operational steps, where we expect Trotterisation errors to manifest as physical noise. Such noise would irreversibly suppress delicate quantum memory effects, forcing a collapse to the memoryless Markovian Otto limit. Coupled with the permanent energetic tax of switching finite-bath interactions, our results indicate that the exploitation of quantum memory resources and finite-power operation belong to different operational regimes.

</details>


### [150] [Memory Device for Photons by exploiting Brillouin Interactions in Nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01816)
*Hashem Zoubi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用受激布里渊散射在纳米光纤中实现单光子级光存储的可能性。首先介绍了通过相干缓冲将光信号相干转移到声波的标准方法，但声波寿命限制了其应用。然后提出了一种无增益损耗的构型，利用两个反向传播泵浦场的光子-声子布里渊相互作用，在纳米线中实现显著慢光的时间延迟，从而实现光子存储。同时讨论了热声子散射影响可忽略的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单光子存储是量子信息处理和量子通信的关键组件，本研究旨在探索利用受激布里渊散射在纳米光纤中实现单光子级光存储的可行性，并克服标准方法中声波寿命带来的局限性。

Method: 首先采用标准方法，利用相干缓冲在纳米尺度波导中将光信号相干转移到声波，再通过逆过程提取。然后提出新构型，利用两个反向传播泵浦场的光子-声子布里渊相互作用，实现无增益损耗的慢光传输，通过时间延迟在纳米线中实现光子存储。

Result: 标准方法受限于声波寿命，不适用于单光子信号。新构型能够在无增益损耗条件下实现单光子级显著慢光，并通过时间延迟实现光子存储，且可满足热声子散射影响可忽略的条件。

Conclusion: 利用受激布里渊散射在纳米光纤中实现单光子存储是可能的，所提出的新构型克服了声波寿命限制，为实现高效单光子存储提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Memory devices for single photons are notable components for quantum information processing and quantum communications. The present study investigates the possibility of achieving storage of light at the level of single photons inside nanofibers by exploiting stimulated Brillouin scattering. We present first the standard approach using a coherent buffer in a nanoscale waveguide by transferring the optical signal coherently to an acoustic wave, and that can be extracted by the reverse process. The life time of the acoustic wave put limitation on the applicability of such approach for single photon signals. We introduce a configuration for achieving a slow signal at the level of single photons without gain or loss. The process utilizes photon-phonon Brillouin interactions involving two counter propagating pump fields. The photon storage is achieved through time delay of significantly slow signal inside nanowires. We address the condition for getting negligible influence due to the scattering off thermal phonons.

</details>


### [151] [Low-ancilla block encodings via Hamiltonian simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01843)
*Yuxin Zhang,Changpeng Shao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义量子信号处理的单辅助量子比特块编码构造方法，通过哈密顿量演化实现近似块编码。对于厄米分解的算子，利用高阶Trotterization可实现电路深度$\widetilde O(L(α/ε)^{o(1)})$且仅需1个辅助比特；利用多乘积公式可实现电路深度$\widetilde O(L)$但需$O(\log\log(1/ε))$个辅助比特。这些构造在保持（近）最优电路深度的同时显著减少了辅助量子比特数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法中块编码是核心原语，但标准构造通常需要对数级别的辅助量子比特开销和复杂的受控操作，且近期下界表明在精确构造中此类开销在广泛电路模型下不可避免。本文旨在绕过这一障碍，在近似设置下实现辅助量子比特开销的降低。

Method: 通过广义量子信号处理，将哈密顿量演化转化为底层哈密顿量的块编码，仅需一个辅助量子比特。针对厄米分解$A=\sum_{j=1}^L α_j H_j$，提出了两种实例化方式：(1) 利用高阶Trotterization实现哈密顿量演化；(2) 利用多乘积公式实现哈密顿量演化。

Result: 使用高阶Trotterization可获得$\varepsilon$-近似的块编码，仅需1个辅助量子比特，电路深度为$\widetilde O\big(L(α/\varepsilon)^{o(1)}\big)$。使用多乘积公式可获得电路深度$\widetilde O(L)$，但需要$O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$个辅助量子比特。

Conclusion: 本文的构造为标准的LCU（线性组合幺正）框架提供了替代方案，核心优势在于在保持（近）最优电路深度的同时大幅减少辅助量子比特数量，在近似设置下突破了精确构造中的辅助量子比特开销下界。

Abstract: Block encodings are a central primitive in quantum algorithms, but standard constructions typically require logarithmic ancilla overhead and complicated controlled operations. Recent lower bounds further show that such ancilla overhead is unavoidable for exact constructions in broad circuit models. We show that this barrier can be bypassed in the approximate setting. Specifically, we present a simple single-ancilla construction that converts Hamiltonian evolution into a block encoding of the underlying Hamiltonian, via generalized quantum signal processing. For operators given by Hermitian decompositions $A=\sum_{j=1}^L α_j H_j$, we instantiate this block-encoding construction in two ways, which differ in how the required Hamiltonian evolution is implemented. Using higher-order Trotterization, we obtain an $\varepsilon$-approximate block encoding of $A$ with only one ancilla qubit and circuit depth $\widetilde O\big(L(α/\varepsilon)^{o(1)}\big),$ where $α=\sum_j α_j$. Using multiproduct formulas, we obtain circuit depth $\widetilde O(L)$, at the cost of $O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$ ancilla qubits. Our constructions provide alternatives to the standard LCU framework, with a focus on reducing the number of ancilla qubits while maintaining (near-)optimal circuit depth.

</details>


### [152] [LUCI on IBM Hardware: Error Suppression with Almost Half Syndrome Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01887)
*Younghun Kim,Spiro Gicev,Martin Sevior,Muhammad Usman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM量子硬件上实验验证了LUCI框架的无重置方案，通过非对称缩放X或Z距离，展示了在综合征密度减半的情况下，动态编码仍能有效压制逻辑错误，挑战了传统静态容错架构的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠错协议依赖特定综合征电路，易受硬件缺陷影响且连接约束严格；LUCI框架虽理论上能通过子程序电路维持时空距离，但先前工作仅针对缺陷规避，未在物理硬件上证明在降低时间距离条件下仍能抑制错误。

Method: 提出LUCI框架下的无重置方案，在IBM量子硬件上通过非对称缩放X或Z距离进行基准测试，与标准表面码对比，测量逻辑Pauli错误的抑制比。

Result: LUCI框架下逻辑X错误抑制比为1.75(10)，逻辑Z错误抑制比为1.93(12)；标准方法分别为1.58(13)和2.44(7)。尽管综合征密度减半，LUCI仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 动态编码即使在无物理缺陷的情况下，通过规避高噪声组件也能优于静态方法，验证了LUCI框架在物理硬件上的可行性与有效性，为混合硬件兼容的量子纠错码设计铺平了道路。

Abstract: Long-lived logical qubits are essential for fault-tolerant quantum computation. However, the practical performance of traditional error correction protocols relies on performing specific syndrome circuits, causing vulnerability to hardware defects and imposing rigid connectivity constraints. Recent theoretical findings have proposed that flexible subroutine circuits within the LUCI framework can maintain space-time distance in the presence of isolated or broken components, albeit at the expense of temporal distance. However, these approaches have solely targeted defect avoidance and have not yet been demonstrated to suppress errors with reduced temporal distances on physical hardware. In this work, we propose a reset-free scenario for the LUCI framework and experimentally benchmark it on IBM quantum hardware. By asymmetrically scaling the $X$ or $Z$ distance, we compare our reset-free approach against the standard surface code and successfully demonstrate error suppression ratios for targeted logical Pauli errors. Remarkably, despite a nearly halved syndrome density in time, which requires two subroutine rounds for full syndrome extraction, the LUCI framework remains competitive with the rotated surface code implementation. In the LUCI framework, we observe error suppression of $1.75(10)$ for logical $X$ errors and $1.93(12)$ for logical $Z$ errors, whereas the standard approach yields $ 1.58(13)$ and $2.44(7)$, respectively. These results demonstrate that dynamic codes outperform standard methods by avoiding highly noisy components, even without physical defects, while preserving logical boundaries. Our findings challenge the conventional dependency on static fault-tolerant architectures by verifying the feasibility and efficacy of the LUCI framework on physical hardware and pave the way for hybrid, hardware-compatible code designs in quantum computing.

</details>


### [153] [Growth of Schrödinger cats in particle-number measurement schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01911)
*S. B. Korolev,A. A. Silin,A. A. Poshevkina,T. Yu. Golubeva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于多模高斯态光子数分辨测量的压缩薛定谔猫态生成方案，推导了二模和三模方案中生成态的解析表达式及其与压缩薛定谔猫态的保真度公式，分析了生成态振幅随探测粒子数的标度关系，并导出了可实现保真度的上界及多模方案提升生成态质量的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何通过光子数分辨测量从多模高斯态中高效生成压缩薛定谔猫态，并探索多模方案相较于传统方案在保真度和振幅标度方面的优势。

Method: 基于多模高斯态和光子数分辨测量的理论框架，推导了二模和三模方案中生成态的解析表达式及保真度公式，分析了振幅与探测粒子数的标度关系，并导出了保真度上界及多模方案优化条件。

Result: 获得了二模和三模方案生成态的解析形式及其与压缩薛定谔猫态保真度的显式表达式，明确了生成态振幅随探测粒子数的标度行为，并确定了多模方案提升保真度的条件及保真度的理论上限。

Conclusion: 多模高斯态结合光子数分辨测量可有效生成压缩薛定谔猫态，且多模方案在特定条件下能提升生成态的保真度，为量子信息处理中非经典态的制备提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the generation of squeezed Schrödinger cat states in schemes based on photon-number-resolving measurements on multimode Gaussian states. We derive analytical expressions for the states generated in two- and three-mode schemes, as well as formulas for their fidelity with squeezed Schrödinger cat states. We analyze how the amplitude of the generated states scales with the number of detected particles. Furthermore, we derive an upper bound on the achievable generation fidelity and identify the conditions under which multimode schemes can enhance the quality of the generated states.

</details>


### [154] [Hacking measurement-device-independent quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01989)
*Konstantin Zaitsev,Polina Acheva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了测量设备无关量子密钥分发（MDI-QKD）在标准安全模型下的根本性漏洞：当测量节点（Charlie）被敌手主动控制时，攻击者可在仅引入5.6%量子误码率的情况下恢复高达70%的筛选密钥，表明当前"测量设备可不受信任"的假设在现实场景中是不充分的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDI-QKD旨在消除单光子探测器的侧信道漏洞，其核心假设是测量节点即使完全不受信任（可被敌手操控）也不影响协议安全性。本文试图检验这一假设在现实敌手模型下的充分性。

Method: 作者构造了一种主动攻击方案：敌手完全控制测量节点Charlie，利用测量设备的不完美特性或协议实现中的自由度，在保持较低量子误码率（QBER）的条件下，从测量结果中提取与密钥相关的信息。

Result: 攻击可在仅引入5.6% QBER的情况下，恢复高达70%的筛选密钥。该攻击不依赖于MDI-QKD的特定实现细节，而是针对其标准安全模型本身。

Conclusion: 现有的MDI-QKD安全模型对测量节点"不可信但不可控"的假设在现实敌手面前不够充分。需要对测量设备无关假设施加额外约束，或采用包含更强敌手能力的精细化安全分析。

Abstract: The security of practical quantum key distribution (QKD) systems is fundamentally constrained by vulnerabilities of single-photon detectors. Measurement-device-independent quantum key distribution (MDI-QKD) was proposed to remove this limitation by allowing all measurements to be performed by a completely untrusted party, under the assumption that the measurement node can be treated as adversarial but does not compromise the security guarantees of the protocol.
  Here we show that this assumption is insufficient under realistic adversarial control of the measurement device. We present an attack in which an adversary exploits active control of the measurement node (Charlie) to obtain significant information about the secret key. The attack enables recovery of up to 70\% of the sifted key while introducing only 5.6\% quantum bit error rate. Unlike previously reported attacks targeting specific implementations of MDI-QKD, our results demonstrate a limitation of the standard security model underlying the protocol.
  These findings indicate that additional constraints on the measurement-device independence assumption, or refined security analyses incorporating stronger adversarial capabilities, are required to ensure the security of MDI-QKD in realistic scenarios.

</details>


### [155] [False vacuum decay in a two-dimensional quantum spin system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01994)
*Luka Pavešić,Ian G. Moss,Simone Montangero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过树张量网络模拟，研究了二维量子伊辛模型中的假真空衰变现象，提取了衰变率、有效界面张力和临界气泡尺寸，并与半经典场论计算进行了对比，发现两者高度一致，为2+1维相互作用量子自旋系统中临界气泡图像的有效性提供了数值证据。


<details>
