  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假真空衰变理论广泛用于描述多种物理现象，但量子版本的成核理论缺乏实验和数值验证，尤其是在二维及以上空间中气泡成核的几何特性尚未得到充分检验。

Method: 采用树张量网络（Tree Tensor Network）模拟方法，对二维量子伊辛模型中的假真空衰变过程进行数值计算，提取衰变率、有效界面张力和临界气泡尺寸等关键物理量，并与半经典场论计算结果进行对比。

Result: 数值模拟结果与半经典场论计算高度一致，验证了临界气泡图像在2+1维相互作用量子自旋系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子假真空衰变理论在更高维度的适用性提供了重要的数值支持，填补了该领域缺乏数值验证的空白。

Abstract: False vacuum decay describes the relaxation of a metastable state through the nucleation and growth of bubbles of the stable phase. Despite describing a broad variety of phenomena across different fields, the quantum version of the nucleation theory has little experimental or numerical support. Testing its predictions is particularly important in two or more spatial dimensions, where bubble nucleation acquires its true geometrical nature. Here, we study false vacuum decay in the quantum Ising model in two dimensions. Through tree tensor network simulations we extract the decay rate, the effective interface tension and the critical bubble size. We compare them to new semi-classical field theory calculations, and find excellent agreement. These results provide numerical evidence that the critical-bubble picture survives in an interacting quantum spin system in 2+1 dimensions.

</details>


### [156] [Partially-Blind Single-Qubit Classification over a Prototype Hybrid Quantum Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01998)
*Matteo Pasini,Tzula Benjamin Propp,Janice van Dam,Garazi Muguruza Lasa,Alexandre Wanick,Hugues de Riedmatten,Gustavo C. do Amaral*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种部分盲量子分类（PB-SQC）框架，在量子网络中使用单量子比特分类器对数据进行安全分类，保护数据和结果隐私，并通过仿真使用真实信用卡欺诈数据集验证了其有效性，同时展示了双量子比特分类器可实现计算验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NISQ时代，需要资源高效的原理验证实验，并能扩展到具有量子计算优势的大规模系统。同时，盲量子计算可提供信息论安全性，但尚未将数据分类任务与隐私保护相结合。本文旨在开发一个能在量子网络上实现远程客户端安全分类的框架，使服务器知晓执行分类任务，但数据和结果保持隐藏。

Method: 1. 提出部分盲单量子比特分类器（PB-SQC）框架，结合单量子比特分类器与盲量子计算思想；2. 在异构量子网络链路上集成多路复用固态量子存储器的服务器与客户端，利用纠缠交换共享纠缠；3. 使用真实信用卡交易欺诈数据集进行仿真，采用实际硬件参数；4. 扩展到双量子比特分类器（TQC）以实现计算验证。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该框架在真实信用卡欺诈数据集上的分类性能接近等效的经典深度置信网络。此外，双量子比特分类器能够提供计算的可验证性。

Conclusion: 该研究为短期内可实现、具备实用价值的量子网络应用铺平了道路，展示了将隐私保护的量子分类集成到量子网络中的可行性。

Abstract: In the NISQ era, there is a need for resource-efficient proof-of-principle experiments that can be built up to genuine utility. Single-qubit classifiers (SQCs) are small-scale hybrid quantum-classical machines capable of performing a basic machine learning task: classifying data. In principle, these can be scaled up to many-qubit quantum classifiers capable of quantum computational advantage. Another type of quantum advantage is enabled by blind quantum computation (BQC), wherein a client may run delegated quantum computations on an untrusted server with information-theoretic security. In this paper, we develop a framework and propose a prototype experiment for a SQC where it is known to the server that a classification is being performed, but the data and outcome stay hidden, i.e., it performs partially-blind SQC (PB-SQC). This can be integrated into a quantum network to deliver quantum-secured classifications to remote clients; we study this for a heterogeneous quantum network link in which entanglement is shared between a server and a client equipped with a multiplexed solid-state quantum memory using entanglement swapping. The framework we develop for PB-SQC on this setup is tested in a simulation with realistic hardware parameters on a real-world credit card transaction fraud database with classification outcomes approaching those of its equivalent classical deep-belief network. In addition, we show how a two-qubit classifier (TQC) instead of a SQC enables verification of the computation. These results pave the way towards a short- to mid-term quantum network offering use-case-ready quantum applications.

</details>


### [157] [Compressive Spectrum Sensing via Spectral Multiplexing in Rydberg Atomic Receiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02001)
*Jun-Rong Chen,Yi-Ming Yin,Le-Bin Chen,Kai Wang,Bang Liu,Li-Hua Zhang,Hao Tian,Ming-Min Zhao,Bin-Bin Wei,Dong-Sheng Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于调频本振的压缩频谱复用框架，在波导耦合里德堡原子接收器中实现，通过将宽带微波频谱压缩到窄带原子响应内，实现了超过1000倍的频谱压缩比（640 MHz→126 kHz），并通过最大比合并获得约10 dB的信噪比增益，无需辅助场或宽带电子设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 里德堡原子接收器灵敏度极高，但受限于极窄的瞬时带宽（~kHz量级），难以直接应用于宽带场景。现有扩宽带宽的方法需引入辅助电磁场或严格参数调谐，增加了系统复杂度。

Method: 采用调频本振在波导耦合里德堡原子接收器中创建多个并行传感通道，这些通道共同构成物理压缩感知矩阵，将输入信号生成多个窄带中频副本，从而将宽带微波频谱投影到窄带原子响应上。

Result: 实现了640 MHz带宽压缩到126 kHz原子本征带宽内，压缩比超1000倍；通过输出副本的固有测量冗余，利用最大比合并实现约10 dB的所需比特能量与噪声功率密度比增益。

Conclusion: 该方法无需辅助场或宽带电子设备，为芯片级量子接收器、低延迟感知及下一代无线通信提供了一条简单可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Rydberg-atomic receivers exhibit exceptional sensitivity yet are fundamentally constrained by the narrow instantaneous bandwidth, limiting their practical deployment in broadband scenarios. Prior approaches typically expand the bandwidth by physically broadening the atomic response, which usually requires auxiliary electromagnetic fields or stringent parameter tuning, thereby increasing overall system complexity. Here, we propose a compressive spectral multiplexing framework implemented in a waveguide-coupled Rydberg atomic receiver using a frequency-modulated local oscillator (FMLO). The FMLO creates multiple parallel sensing channels that collectively constitute a physical compressive sensing matrix, generating multiple narrowband intermediate-frequency replicas of the input signal. Thus, a broadband microwave spectrum is projected onto a set of narrowband atomic responses. It is demonstrated that spectral information spanning a bandwidth of over 640 MHz can be effectively compressed into the intrinsic atomic bandwidth of 126 kHz, achieving a spectrum compression ratio exceeding 1000. Furthermore, these output replicas offer intrinsic measurement redundancy and facilitate signal-to-noise ratio enhancement. An approximate 10 dB gain is achieved in the required bit-energy-to-noise-power-density ratio for multi-channel communication via maximal-ratio combining. This approach requires no auxiliary fields or broadband electronics, providing a simple and scalable pathway for chip-scale quantum receivers, latency-critical sensing, and next-generation wireless communications.

</details>


### [158] [Quantum sensing of aging transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02004)
*Huining Zhang,Yunbo Zhang,Xiaoguang Wang,X. X. Yi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用单个量子比特探针来定位振荡器网络中的老化转变点，通过探针激发态布居数在临界点附近的显著增强，实现高精度估计。即使在经典振荡器体系中，这种临界增强现象仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老化转变点是量子网络从稳定活跃状态突然退化为失活相态的临界阈值，准确检测该点对于预测网络崩溃、评估稳定性及鲁棒性控制至关重要。

Method: 采用单个量子比特探针与少量振荡器节点相干耦合，通过监测探针激发态布居数对失活比例p的敏感度变化，利用临界点附近Fisher信息增强来实现高精度估计。

Result: 当失活比例p趋近老化转变点时，探针激发态布居数对p的敏感性显著增强，Fisher信息出现临界增强，使得转变点估计精度大幅提升。这种增强在经典振荡器体系中依然保持。

Conclusion: 该工作为振荡器网络中的老化转变检测提供了可行路径，并给出了量子多体系统中临界现象的计量学视角。

Abstract: The aging transition is a critical phenomenon in which collective dynamics deteriorate as the fraction of inactive quantum nodes exceeds a threshold, referred to as the aging transition point. Such transitions are relevant to a broad range of biological and physiological systems, and may play an important role in quantum information processing, particularly in the stability assessment and robustness control of quantum networks. Detecting the aging transition point is therefore crucial for predicting network breakdown, since it marks the critical threshold at which a quantum network abruptly loses its stable active state and enters a degraded inactive phase. Here we propose a quantum sensing strategy to locate this transition point using a single qubit probe coherently coupled to a small subset of oscillator nodes. As the inactive fraction p approaches the aging transition point, the excited-state population of the probe becomes highly sensitive to variations in p, leading to a pronounced enhancement of the Fisher information. This critical enhancement enables high-precision estimation of the transition point. Remarkably, this enhancement survives even in the classical regime for the oscillators, where the Fisher information increases dramatically as p approaches the transition region. Our results establish a feasible route to sensing aging transitions in oscillator networks and provide a metrological perspective on critical phenomena in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [159] [Local distinguishability of six bipartite orthogonal product states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02006)
*Guang-Bao Xu,Hua-Kun Wang,Yu-Guang Yang,Dong-Huan Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意二分量子系统中六个正交乘积态（OPSs）的局域可区分性问题。通过使用两两正交关系数的向量，将六二分OPSs集合分为八类，共78种不同情况，其中除5种外均可通过局域操作和经典通信（LOCC）完美区分，并详细讨论了这5种情况的局域可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交量子态集合的局域可区分性在协议设计中至关重要，有助于减少量子态传输并降低操作成本，因此有必要对其进行深入研究。

Method: 利用两两正交关系数的向量分类方法，将任意二分量子系统中的六正交乘积态集合划分为八类，进而细分为78种不同情况。

Result: 78种情况中，除5种外均能通过LOCC完美区分；对这5种特殊情况进行了详细讨论。

Conclusion: 本文明确刻画了六个二分正交乘积态集合的局域可区分性特征。

Abstract: It is necessary to investigate the local distinguishability of orthogonal quantum state sets, as their adoption in protocol design helps diminish quantum state transmission and cut operational costs. In this paper, we explore the local distinguishability of six orthogonal product states (OPSs) on any bipartite quantum system. We classify different sets of six bipartite OPSs into eight categories by using the vectors of the numbers of pairwise orthogonality relations, where any two states are orthogonal on only one subsystem within each set. We find that these eight categories contain a total of 78 distinct cases, all but five of which are perfectly distinguishable via local operations and classical communication (LOCC). Furthermore, we discuss the local distinguishability of those five distinct cases in detail. Our work explicitly characterizes the local distinguishability of six bipartite OPSs.

</details>


### [160] [Mid-infrared pure-state quantum light source based on lithium niobate waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02016)
*Huang Yuhang,Wang Dongzhou,Ke Shaolin,Jin Ruibo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于铌酸锂薄膜的理论方案，通过1556.9 nm泵浦产生中心波长为3113.8 nm的纠缠光子对，实现了纯度0.999、亮度6.18×10^6 cps/mW的中红外量子光源，亮度比体PPLN晶体源高三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中红外量子纠缠光源主要依赖体PPLN晶体，限制了亮度和集成度，亟需更高性能的可集成片上光源方案。

Method: 基于铌酸锂薄膜，优化波导结构和周期性极化设计，实现II型相位匹配和群速度匹配，并结合域排列算法定制极化方向，实现精确相位匹配。

Result: 实现了纯度高达0.999、亮度为6.18×10^6 cps/mW的中红外量子光源，亮度比体PPLN晶体源高三个数量级。

Conclusion: 本研究为实现中红外波段高亮度、高纯度片上量子光源提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Mid-infrared quantum light sources hold broad application prospects in fields such as gas sensing and infrared thermal imaging. However, currently used mid-infrared quantum entangled light sources primarily rely on bulk periodically poled lithium niobate (PPLN) crystals, which limits brightness and integration. This paper proposes a theoretical scheme based on lithium niobate thin films, in which 1556.9 nm pumping is used to generate entangled photon pairs with a central wavelength of 3113.8 nm. By optimizing the waveguide structure and periodic polarization design, type-II phase matching and group velocity matching are achieved. This enables transverse electric (TE)-polarized pump input to be down converted to generate photon pairs with TE and transverse magnetic (TM) polarizations. Furthermore, by combining a domain arrangement algorithm used for the customized design of polarization direction in PPLN waveguides, precise phase matching is achieved, resulting in a quantum light source with a purity as high as 0.999 and a brightness of 6.18$\times 10^6$ cps/mW, which is three orders of magnitude higher than that of the bulk PPLN crystal source. This study provides a promising solution for realizing high-brightness, high-purity on-chip quantum light sources in the mid-infrared band.

</details>


### [161] [Idling error suppression through gate scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02031)
*Hoiki Madison Liu,Kazunori Maruyama,Hirotaka Oshima,Shintaro Sato*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过量子电路调度抑制空闲错误的新方法，无需引入额外门操作，仅通过调整门执行时序即可提升计算精度，并通过数值模拟和硬件实验验证了效果，同时提供了理论分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度量子计算需要有效抑制量子比特在等待期间的空闲错误，而传统动态解耦等方法需要插入额外控制门，带来额外开销。本文旨在探索一种不引入额外门操作的替代方案。

Method: 通过调整具有调度灵活性的量子门执行时序来重新安排量子电路，在不增加任何额外门操作的情况下抑制空闲错误。同时从理论上推导了空闲噪声下的密度矩阵演化。

Result: 数值模拟和硬件实验均表明，通过适当调整门执行时序，可以显著影响并改善整体计算精度。

Conclusion: 量子电路调度可作为动态解耦等传统方法的有效替代方案来抑制空闲错误，为提升量子计算精度提供了新的路径。

Abstract: Achieving high-precision quantum computation requires effective suppression of idling errors that occur when qubits remain inactive during waiting periods within a quantum circuit. Conventional mitigation techniques, such as dynamical decoupling, suppress decoherence by periodically refreshing quantum states through the insertion of additional control gates. In this paper, we propose an alternative approach that suppresses idling errors through quantum circuit scheduling without introducing any additional gate operations. By appropriately adjusting the execution timing of quantum gates with scheduling flexibility, we demonstrate through both numerical simulations and hardware experiments that the overall computational accuracy can be significantly influenced and, in many cases, improved. In addition, we analytically derive the density-matrix evolution under idling noise and provide a theoretical framework that explains the observed behavior.

</details>


### [162] [Undamped Modes in an N-Qubit Heisenberg Chain with Collective Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02054)
*Chun Hei Leung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了自旋-1/2海森堡链在集体自旋跳跃算符与环境耦合中的无阻尼行为，证明对于任意链长N≥3均存在无阻尼模，这些模对系统参数变化具有鲁棒性，并利用Bethe ansatz解表征了其数量和振荡频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开放可积量子系统中长寿命相干动力学的存在性，理解集体耗散环境下无阻尼模的普遍性质及其对参数变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 基于Bethe ansatz基矢进行分析，研究自旋-1/2海森堡链在集体自旋跳跃算符环境耦合下的无阻尼行为，通过解析方法表征无阻尼模的数量和振荡频率。

Result: 证明无阻尼模存在于任意链长N≥3的海森堡链中，且这些模对系统参数（包括集体耗散的具体形式和外部场）的变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 开放可积量子系统中存在长寿命的相干动力学，无阻尼模的发现为理解量子系统与环境的相互作用提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We investigate the undamped behaviors in a spin-1/2 Heisenberg chain coupled with an environment via collective spin jump operators. Using the Bethe ansatz basis, we show that undamped modes exist for any chain length N >= 3. These modes remain robust against variations in the system parameters, including the specific form of the collective dissipation, and the external field. Exploiting the Bethe ansatz solution, we further characterize the number of undamped modes and their oscillation frequencies, uncovering long-lived coherent dynamics in open integrable quantum systems.

</details>


### [163] [Quantum Convolutional Autoencoders for Reconstruction-Based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02135)
*Donovan Slabbert,Francesco Petruccione*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子卷积神经网络(QCNN)改编为量子自编码器(QAE)框架，用于基于重构的异常检测。研究了两种量子卷积自编码器架构（层级式与瓶颈式），并在真实系外行星异常检测数据集上进行了基准测试，结果表明量子瓶颈结构可提升异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCNN在量子机器学习中因其高效参数化和层级化量子信息表示而日益流行，异常检测是重要的机器学习任务。本文旨在将QCNN适配到QAE框架中，用于半监督重构式异常检测，以探索量子模型在该任务上的潜力。

Method: 将QCNN架构改编为量子自编码器，对特征提取和降维后的时间序列数据进行半监督训练（仅用正常样本）。设计两种架构：层级式（信息在电路中分布式保留）和瓶颈式（使用额外解码量子比特进行显式压缩与重构）。变化量子潜在空间大小以研究其对重构精度和异常检测性能的影响。

Result: 潜在空间大小与模型容量之间存在权衡；显式的潜在空间压缩（量子瓶颈结构）相比在电路中保留信息的架构，可提升异常检测性能。两种量子自编码器与变分量子电路及经典基线在真实系外行星数据集上进行了对比。

Conclusion: 量子瓶颈结构有助于提升异常检测性能，潜在空间大小与模型容量需权衡。该工作为量子机器学习在异常检测领域的应用提供了新的架构方向。

Abstract: Quantum convolutional neural networks (QCNNs) have become increasingly popular in quantum machine learning (QML) due to their efficient parameterization and hierarchical representation of quantum information. Anomaly detection is an important machine learning task with applications across a wide range of domains, including scientific data analysis. In this work, we adapt a QCNN architecture into a quantum autoencoder (QAE) framework for reconstruction-based anomaly detection. The models are trained in a semi-supervised manner on normal samples to reconstruct feature-extracted and dimensionally reduced time-series data, with reconstruction error used as an anomaly score. We investigate two quantum convolutional autoencoder architectures that differ in their treatment of latent information: a hierarchical architecture in which information remains distributed across the circuit and a bottleneck-based architecture in which information is explicitly compressed and reconstructed using additional decoder qubits. The size of the quantum latent space is varied to study its influence on reconstruction accuracy and anomaly detection performance. The approaches are benchmarked against both a variational quantum circuit and a comparable classical baseline using a real-world exoplanet anomaly-detection dataset. Results indicate a trade-off between latent-space size and model capacity, while also suggesting that explicit latent-space compression through a quantum bottleneck can improve anomaly detection performance relative to architectures that retain information throughout the circuit.

</details>


### [164] [Extending the computational reach of Quantum Annealing using Reverse Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02146)
*Lucas Joshua Menger,Thomas Lippert,Manpreet Singh Jattana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验研究，发现反向退火相比标准前向退火在组合优化问题中能持续提升解质量和效率，其优势随问题复杂度增加而增强，且优于单纯延长前向退火时间。研究识别了反向退火参数的最优区间，指出其对大规模高复杂度问题最具价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量子退火是一种有前景的组合优化启发式方法，但当前硬件上噪声和小能隙导致性能随问题规模增大而下降。反向退火被提出作为改进策略，但其何时能够提供系统性优势尚不明确。

Method: 在D-Wave Advantage系统上进行系统性实验研究，在Max-Cut、数字划分和稀疏聚类问题上基准测试反向退火，改变反向距离、暂停持续时间和退火时间等参数。

Result: 结合前向和反向退火能持续提升解质量和效率；反向退火优势随问题复杂度增加而增强；反向退火比单纯延长前向退火时间带来更大效率提升；存在与冻结点和能级交叉位置相关的窄最优参数区间。

Conclusion: 反向退火对大规模高复杂度优化问题最具价值，随着量子退火硬件向更现实应用扩展，其重要性将日益增加。

Abstract: Quantum annealing is a promising heuristic for combinatorial optimization, but on current hardware its performance degrades for larger and more complex problems due to noise and small energy gaps. Reverse annealing has been proposed as a refinement strategy, yet it remains unclear when it provides systematic advantages over standard forward annealing or simply increasing annealing time. We find that combining forward and reverse annealing consistently improves solution quality and efficiency across multiple problem classes. The benefits of reverse annealing increase with problem complexity and are strongest in regimes where forward annealing is increasingly limited. Moreover, reverse annealing yields larger efficiency gains than simply extending forward annealing times. We establish these results through a systematic experimental study on a D-Wave Advantage system, benchmarking reverse annealing across Max-Cut, Number Partitioning, and sparse clustering problems while varying reverse distance, pause duration, and annealing time. We identify a narrow optimal regime for reverse annealing parameters linked to the location of freeze-out points and energy-level crossings in the annealing schedule. These findings demonstrate that reverse annealing is most valuable for large, high-complexity optimization problems and is likely to gain importance as quantum annealing hardware scales toward more realistic applications.

</details>


### [165] [Thermodynamics of Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02157)
*Lixiang Ding,Xingze Qiu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过非平衡热力学框架，建立了量子储层计算中临界区预测性能与微观能耗之间的严格谱共振机制，揭示了预测容量与信息耗散之间的基本热力学权衡，并证明动态量子相干性可在不增加机械功的情况下提升预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子储层计算在处理复杂时序数据方面具有潜力，但其基本计算极限和能耗极限尚未明确，需要建立预测性能与热力学成本之间的理论关联。

Method: 将Holevo容量映射到Bogoliubov-Kubo-Mori几何流形上，解析证明量子临界区的计算峰值源于能隙闭合导致的共振频率匹配；引入量子信息耗散量化非预测性历史数据残留，推导广义Landauer界；通过相干性分解分析动态量子相干性的作用。

Result: 发现临界共振在解锁最优预测容量的同时，必然最大化信息耗散和环境擦除所需的不可逆功；动态量子相干性可增强预测容量而不增加额外机械功。

Conclusion: 建立了量子学习器件的终极能耗极限，为设计高能效量子神经形态硬件提供了理论原则。

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing provides a framework for processing complex temporal data, yet its fundamental computational and energetic limits remain unresolved. Here, we establish a non-equilibrium thermodynamic framework that links the macroscopic predictive performance of driven open quantum systems to their microscopic energetic costs. By mapping the Holevo capacities onto the Bogoliubov-Kubo-Mori geometric manifold, we analytically prove that the computational peak within the quantum critical region originates from a strict spectral resonance: the closing of the energy gap forces the reservoir's transition frequencies to align with the chaotic drive. To evaluate the associated thermodynamic costs, we introduce quantum informational dissipation to quantify the non-predictive historical data structurally retained by the reservoir, deriving a generalized Landauer bound for continuous temporal processing. This reveals a fundamental thermodynamic trade-off: the critical resonance that unlocks optimal predictive capacity inherently maximizes informational dissipation and the irreversible work required for environmental erasure. Furthermore, coherence decomposition demonstrates that dynamic quantum coherences strictly amplify predictive capacity without demanding additional mechanical work. These findings establish the ultimate energetic limits of quantum learning devices, providing theoretical principles for designing energy-efficient quantum neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [166] [A Structure Theorem for Phase-Space Representations of Continuous-Variable Quantum Error-Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02164)
*Enrico Bozzetto,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将广义概率论中准概率表示的结构定理与玻色子量子纠错码联系起来，为连续变量纠错码提供了通用的相空间表示，并以GKP码、猫码和二项式码为例展示了通过该方法获得的相空间表示。这种表示使我们能够从一般层面和针对每种具体编码定义错误在相空间中可能采取的数学结构，并以单光子丢失错误为例进行了抽象和具体的展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将广义概率论中准概率表示的结构定理与玻色子量子纠错码建立联系，为连续变量量子纠错码提供统一的相空间表示框架，并揭示错误在这些表示中的数学结构。

Method: 通过连接广义概率论中准概率表示的结构定理与玻色子量子纠错码，提出了连续变量纠错码的通用相空间表示方法，并以GKP码、猫码和二项式码为具体案例进行验证。

Result: 成功为连续变量量子纠错码建立了通用的相空间表示，并在GKP码、猫码和二项式码中得到了具体的相空间表示形式，同时从抽象和具体层面（以单光子丢失错误为例）刻画了错误在相空间中的数学结构。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了准概率表示与玻色子量子纠错码之间的理论联系，为连续变量纠错码的相空间分析提供了统一框架，有助于更深入地理解错误在相空间中的结构特性。

Abstract: In this paper we connect the structure theorem for quasiprobability representation of generalised probabilistic theories to bosonic quantum error correction codes, giving both a general phase-space representation for continuous-variable error-correcting codes, and showing as specific examples the phase-space representations obtained through this method for Gottesman-Knill-Preskill codes, cat codes, and binomial codes. This representation allows us to define both generally and for each of these codes the mathematical structure in phase space that errors can take, which we show both abstractly and for the specific example of single photon loss errors.

</details>


### [167] [Computable measures of fermionic non-Gaussianity from the covariance matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02242)
*Poetri Sonya Tarabunga,Bernhard Jobst,Raúl Morral-Yepes,Marc Langer,Barbara Kraus,Frank Pollmann,Sheng-Hsuan Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种费米子非高斯性的凸资源理论，引入两类可计算度量：占据数熵（基于协方差矩阵的Williamson正规形，其中Tsallis-α熵家族成员被证明在高斯协议下单调，可作为凸资源单调量并下界非高斯门数量）和自然轨道参与熵（基于自然轨道基下态振幅的Rényi-α熵，上界经典模拟成本）。通过稳定子态、平移不变态、随机SWAP掺杂匹配门电路和键调制XXZ模型进行了分析，统一了量子信息、凝聚态物理和量子化学中的相关概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子非高斯性（fermionic magic）是理解费米子量子系统计算复杂度的关键资源，但现有量化方式受限，缺乏可计算且具有操作意义的度量方法。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于协方差矩阵的Williamson正规形，定义两类熵度量家族：(1) 占据数熵——占据数的Tsallis-α熵；(2) 自然轨道参与熵——自然轨道基下态振幅的Rényi-α熵。通过分析高斯协议下的单调性、稳定子态和平移不变态中的简化结构，以及SWAP掺杂匹配门电路和XXZ模型进行验证。

Result: 证明了占据数熵家族中的一个成员在高斯协议下具有单调性，可作为可计算的凸资源单调量，从而下界非高斯门数量；自然轨道参与熵上界了正交高斯基下的经典模拟成本。两类度量在稳定子态和平移不变态中简化并揭示额外结构。

Conclusion: 建立了可计算费米子非高斯性的资源理论框架，统一了量子信息、凝聚态物理和量子化学中的相关概念，为研究量子多体系统复杂性提供了新方向，并为评估费米子态经典可模拟性提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Fermionic non-Gaussianity, or fermionic magic, is a key resource underlying the computational complexity of fermionic quantum systems, yet tractable and operationally meaningful ways to quantify it remain limited. We address this challenge by developing a convex resource theory of fermionic non-Gaussianity and introducing two families of computable measures for pure fermionic states, both derived from the Williamson normal form of the covariance matrix. The first family, occupation number entropies, is defined as the Tsallis-$α$ entropy of the occupation numbers. We prove that one member of this family is monotonic under Gaussian protocols, establishing it as a computable convex resource monotone. It consequently lower bounds the number of non-Gaussian gates needed for state preparation. The second family, natural-orbital participation entropies, is given by the Rényi-$α$ entropy of the squared amplitudes of the state in the natural-orbital basis, defined by the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix. These measures quantify state compressibility in this basis and thus upper bound the classical simulation cost in an orthonormal Gaussian basis. We analyze both families for stabilizer and translation-invariant states, where they simplify and reveal additional structure. We further study representative examples, including random SWAP-doped matchgate circuits and the bond-modulated XXZ model, highlighting the role of non-Gaussianity in many-body phenomena. Our work establishes a resource-theoretic framework for computable fermionic non-Gaussianity that unifies notions arising across quantum information, condensed-matter physics, and quantum chemistry, opening new directions for studying the complexity of quantum many-body systems and providing practical tools to assess the classical simulability of fermionic states relevant for quantum advantage.

</details>


### [168] [A transition-metal qubit in diamond with all-optical control and millisecond quantum memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02258)
*I. M. Morris,T. Alberth,L. Crooks,T. Lühmann,D. J. Twitchen,S. Pezzagna,J. Meijer,S. S. Nicley,J. N. Becker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究展示了利用金刚石中的镍空位(NiV⁻)缺陷实现全光学控制的固态自旋量子比特，在1.65 K下相干时间超过1毫秒，结合了自旋-轨道保护的地态相干性、全光学控制和近红外发射，克服了传统金刚石色心在可扩展量子网络中的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需要同时具备高效光学访问、相干控制和长寿命量子存储的量子比特，但现有金刚石色心在这三者之间存在固有权衡，缺乏一个可扩展平台能同时实现所有特性。

Method: 利用单个NiV⁻缺陷，在1.65 K（兼容紧凑型闭循环低温系统）下实现全光学控制的自旋量子比特；采用Raman Rabi振荡和Ramsey干涉测量，并通过全光学动态去耦技术（CPMG序列）将相干时间从T₂* = 371 ns延长至T₂^(CPMG-4) = 1.27 ms。

Result: 实现了相干时间超过1毫秒的全光学控制金刚石自旋量子比特；T₂* = 371 ns，经CPMG-4动态去耦后T₂ = 1.27 ms。

Conclusion: NiV⁻缺陷作为一种可部署的金刚石自旋-光子接口，提供了一条超越现有平台的独立路径，结合了自旋-轨道保护的地态相干性、全光学控制和近红外发射，适用于可扩展量子网络。

Abstract: Quantum networks require qubits that combine efficient optical access, coherent control, and long-lived quantum memory, but realizing all three in one scalable platform remains a central bottleneck. Diamond color centers are leading candidates, yet widely studied defects retain tradeoffs among these capabilities. Here, we show that transition-metal defects in diamond provide a distinct route beyond these platforms by combining spin-orbit protected ground-state coherence, all-optical control, and near-infrared emission. Using a single nickel-vacancy (NiV$^-$), we demonstrate an all-optically controlled diamond spin qubit with coherence exceeding one millisecond at 1.65 K, compatible with compact closed-cycle cryogenics. We implement Raman Rabi oscillations and Ramsey interferometry and use all-optical dynamical decoupling to extend coherence from $T_2^*$ = 371 ns to $T_2^{CPMG-4}$ = 1.27 ms, establishing NiV$^-$ as a deployable diamond spin-photon interface.

</details>


### [169] [Bockstein braiding statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02280)
*Po-Shen Hsin,Yu-An Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现了空间维度d中p-和q-维激发的新型互易统计——Bockstein编织统计，适用于p+q=d-1的相邻情况（而非传统的p+q=d-2条件）。该统计由场论中的Bockstein操作β_N描述，对应响应项(2πi/N)∫A_{d-p}∪β_N B_{d-q}，并通过闭合幺正过程W_N(X,Y)测量。该统计阻碍两个Z_N激发的同时凝聚，排除具有相应混合反常的系统的完全对称有能隙相，并在对称性破缺时导致对称性分数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统编织统计要求p+q=d-2，但对于相邻维度p+q=d-1的情况，已知的互易统计尚未被系统研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，识别并表征这种相邻情况下的新型统计。

Method: 通过场论描述，引入背景场A_{d-p}和B_{d-q}耦合到两种激发类型，利用Bockstein操作β_N构造响应项。同时构造了局域产生算符X和Y（支撑区具有交错一维重叠）及闭合幺正过程W_N(X,Y)来测量该统计。

Result: 识别出Bockstein编织统计，其场论描述为(2πi/N)∫A_{d-p}∪β_N B_{d-q}；构造了从一维粒子-粒子统计到三维粒子-膜统计的完整谱系；证明了非平凡Bockstein编织统计阻碍同时凝聚、排除全对称有能隙相并导致对称性分数化。

Conclusion: Bockstein编织统计是p+q=d-1情形下Z_N激发的新型互易统计，对理解高维拓扑序、反常和对称性分数化具有基础性意义。

Abstract: Braiding statistics, from the Aharonov-Bohm phase to anyons in fractional quantum Hall systems, play a central role in quantum physics. For $p$- and $q$-dimensional excitations in $d$ spatial dimensions, ordinary braiding requires $p+q=d-2$. In a field-theoretic description of $\mathbb Z_N$ excitations, ordinary braiding is described by the linking response $(2πi/N)\int A_{d-p}\cup B_{d-q}$, where $A_{d-p}$ and $B_{d-q}$ are background fields coupled to the two excitation types. In this work, we identify new mutual statistics in the adjacent case $p+q=d-1$. For two invertible excitations obeying $\mathbb Z_N$ fusion, one can choose local creation operators $X$ and $Y$ whose supports have a staggered one-dimensional overlap. The closed unitary process $W_N(X,Y)=(Y^{-1}X^{-1})^N(YX)^N$ measures the resulting mutual statistic. Its field-theory description is $(2πi/N)\int A_{d-p}\cupβ_N B_{d-q}$, where $β_N$ is the Bockstein operation; we therefore call the invariant Bockstein braiding statistics. The construction yields particle-particle statistics in one dimension, particle-loop statistics in two dimensions, and loop-loop or particle-membrane statistics in three dimensions. Nontrivial Bockstein braiding statistics obstructs simultaneous condensation of the two $\mathbb Z_N$ excitations. It also rules out a fully symmetric gapped phase for systems with the corresponding mixed anomaly and implies symmetry fractionalization when one of the $\mathbb Z_N$ symmetries is broken.

</details>


### [170] [Neural-Network Inverse Design of SRF Cavities and Transmons for Bosonic Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02289)
*Joseph Yaker,Jovan Markovic,Alessandro Reineri,Doga Murat Kurkcuoglu,Silvia Zorzetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种深度神经网络方法，分别用于超导射频腔体几何结构的逆设计（目标腔体观测参数）和transmon量子比特的逆设计（目标耦合率、频率和退简谐性），实现从目标性能到器件几何的直接映射，替代传统迭代仿真，设计精度分别达~5%（腔体）和~2%（transmon）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维超导射频腔与transmon量子比特耦合的量子信息处理架构中，器件几何结构与电磁性能、耦合强度之间存在复杂的多对一逆映射关系。随着系统规模扩大，传统迭代仿真方法计算成本过高，亟需高效的逆设计方法。

Method: 构建两种深度神经网络模型：(1) SRF腔体逆设计网络，输入目标腔体观测参数，输出腔体几何结构；(2) transmon量子比特逆设计网络，输入目标耦合率、量子比特频率和退简谐性(g, ν_q, α)，输出transmon设计参数。

Result: 候选设计与目标的匹配精度：腔体约5%，transmon约2%，经端到端重仿真验证。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络方法能够高效地将目标器件性能直接映射到候选几何设计，大幅降低设计成本，为可扩展的量子信息处理系统逆设计提供了快速替代方案。

Abstract: Three-dimensional superconducting radio-frequency (SRF) cavities provide exceptionally long-lived electromagnetic modes and, when coupled to nonlinear elements such as transmon qubits, become promising architectures for bosonic quantum information processing. The inverse design of such systems, i.e., recovering device geometries that produce specified electromagnetic and coupling targets, is generally a one-to-many problem. The qubit-cavity coupling strength depends sensitively on both the transmon geometry and its position within the cavity's electromagnetic field. As these systems scale up and their design parameter spaces grow, the cost of conventional iterative simulation becomes prohibitive. We present two deep neural network (DNN) approaches that address this inverse-design problem at complementary levels of the design stack. The first proposes SRF cavity geometries that produce target cavity observables. The second proposes transmon qubit designs that produce target qubit-cavity parameters -- the coupling rate, qubit frequency, and anharmonicity $(g, ν_q, α)$. The recovered candidate designs match the targets to within $\sim$5\% (cavity) and $\sim$2\% (transmon), confirmed by end-to-end re-simulation. Both approaches map desired device behavior directly to candidate designs, a fast alternative to the iterative simulation studies usually required.

</details>


### [171] [Time-Reversal and Reversible Dynamics in Cavity QED for Quantum Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02320)
*Simone Colombo,Edwin Pedrozo-Peñafiel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了腔量子电动力学（腔QED）中基于时间反演协议（如SATIN、 scrambling增强计量、相互作用基读出）实现量子增强计量的方法，强调可逆多体动力学在解码量子信息中的关键作用，指出解码能力将与纠缠生成能力同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子增强计量虽能通过纠缠超越标准量子极限，但高度纠缠态中的编码信息往往难以通过实际测量提取，这一核心挑战催生了利用多体相互作用进行解码的研究方向。

Method: 基于时间反演协议，包括相互作用基读出、SATIN（时间反演相互作用信号放大）、scrambling增强计量等方案，利用腔QED平台的可调谐相互作用和可控可逆性实现信号解码与放大。

Result: 腔QED平台成功实现了Loschmidt回波、SATIN信号放大、scrambling增强计量等实验演示，展示了可逆多体动力学在量子增强传感中的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 解码量子信息的能力将与生成纠缠的能力同样重要，可逆多体动力学应被确立为量子增强传感的核心资源。

Abstract: Quantum-enhanced metrology relies on entanglement to achieve sensitivities beyond the standard quantum limit. While remarkable progress has been made in generating highly entangled many-body states, extracting their metrological advantage remains a central challenge because the encoded information is often inaccessible to realistic measurements. A key development of the past decade has been the realization that many-body interactions can play a dual role: they can be used not only to generate entanglement, but also to decode it. This idea underlies interaction-based readout and time-reversal protocols, in which controlled non-linear dynamics transform weakly encoded signals into experimentally accessible observables. Cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED) provides a particularly powerful setting for these approaches because it combines collective enhancement, tunable interactions, and controllable reversibility within a single platform. In this review, we discuss the emergence of time-reversal protocols in cavity QED, from their conceptual roots in Loschmidt echoes to modern implementations of signal amplification through a time-reversed interaction (SATIN), scrambling-enhanced metrology, and more general interaction-based readout schemes. We examine the physical mechanisms that enable reversible many-body dynamics, review key experimental demonstrations, and discuss future directions involving complex entangled states, nonlinear decoding, and emerging quantum platforms. Together, these developments suggest that the ability to decode quantum information may become as important as the ability to generate it, establishing reversible many-body dynamics as a central resource for quantum-enhanced sensing.

</details>


### [172] [Temporal nonlocality of a qudit resides in the input state, not the channel, and certifies temporal teleportation up to a fundamental limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02331)
*Karol Bartkiewicz,Patrycja Tulewicz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究时间维度上的非局域性——单量子系统两次测量间的关联强度。提出"时间非局域鲁棒性"(TNR)完全由初始态携带，最大混合态时TNR为零。TNR可设备无关地保真时间隐形传态，但存在"过度认证"问题：可逆幺正演化能产生最大时序贝尔信号，实际传输却低于经典基线。通过SDP层级统一刻画时间纠缠、导引和非局域鲁棒性，并给出完全的认证校正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索单量子系统两次测量间关联的非经典性来源，揭示时间维度"非局域性"的本质——它源于初始态的混合度而非通道的相干性，并解决由此导致的设备无关认证中的过度认证问题。

Method: 定义时间非局域鲁棒性(TNR)度量；利用半定规划(SDP)统一层级框架刻画时间纠缠鲁棒性(TER)、时间导引鲁棒性(TSR)和时间非局域鲁棒性(TNR)；建立严格的下界层级和基于无信号时间条件(NSIT)的上界层级；数值验证d=2到5。

Result: TNR=0当且仅当输入态为最大混合态；TNR可设备无关地保真时间隐形传态，d=3时达到7/9；可逆幺正演化可实现最大TNR但传输低于经典基线，存在过度认证；给出普适上界TNR≤(d-1)/d及精确的通道解析值；证明无法通过选择探针实现通道普适的认证。

Conclusion: 时间非局域性的资源是输入态混合度的反作用，而非通道相干性。TNR既是强大的设备无关认证工具（保真时间隐形传态），也存在陷阱（过度认证），但可通过本文的校正方案完全解决。统一的SDP层级框架完整刻画了时间维度的量子关联层次结构。

Abstract: Correlations between two moments in time can be too strong for any classical explanation -- and, remarkably, this can happen for a single quantum system measured twice, with no second particle involved. We show that when one qudit is sent through a noisy channel, the strength of this "nonlocality in time" -- the temporal nonlocality robustness $\mathrm{TNR}$ -- is carried entirely by the starting state: it vanishes precisely when the input is maximally mixed (completely random), $\mathrm{TNR}(ρ_A,\mathcal{E})=0\Leftrightarrowρ_A=\mathbb{1}/d$, for the standard noise families. The resource is not any coherence in the channel but the back-action of the input's mixedness, and it survives even complete decoherence. This is at once a power and a trap. As a power, $\mathrm{TNR}$ device-independently lower-bounds the fidelity of temporal teleportation -- sending an unknown state forward in time -- reaching $7/9$ at $d=3$, without trusting the measuring devices. As a trap, because the certified quantity is decoupled from the channel's actual coherence transmission, it can certify more than the channel delivers: an injective (reversible) unitary attains the maximal temporal-Bell signal yet teleports below the classical baseline. We resolve this over-certification completely -- a universal cap $\mathrm{TNR}\le(d-1)/d$ with an exact channel-resolved value, honest certification for the depolarizing channel and for any sufficiently mixed probe, and a proof that no choice of probes makes it channel-universal. Underpinning the results is a unified semidefinite-programming hierarchy of the temporal entanglement, steering and nonlocality robustnesses ($\mathrm{TER}$, $\mathrm{TSR}$, $\mathrm{TNR}$), with a strict lower hierarchy and an upper one conditional on no-signaling in time ($\mathrm{NSIT}$). All structure is verified numerically for $d=2$ through $5$.

</details>


### [173] [Kardar-Parisi-Zhang dynamics in an open integrable system: beyond the spontaneous-symmetry-breaking ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02341)
*Guo-Qiang Wang,Chang-Ling Zou,Guang-Can Guo,and Xu-Bo Zou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现开放可积B3模型中的电荷输运遵循Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ)动力学而非自发对称破缺(SSB)预期的扩散行为，表明SSB唯象假设在描述开放系统流体动力学时存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证基于自发对称破缺(SSB)的唯象假设在描述开放量子系统电荷输运时的适用边界，探究当该假设失效时是否涌现非平凡动力学。

Method: 将B3模型等价为两个相互作用的非对称XXZ自旋链，分析SSB唯象假设只能捕捉链间相互作用的影响，而链内非对称XXZ结构在适当初始条件下主导动力学行为。

Result: B3模型表现出KPZ标度行为而非SSB预期的扩散行为，且即使跳跃率为负值时KPZ标度依然存在。

Conclusion: 开放系统中电荷输运理论需要超越基于SSB的唯象假设，考虑更丰富的动力学机制如KPZ标度行为。

Abstract: The universality of dynamical scaling laws constitutes a cornerstone in the theoretical understanding of quantum many-body systems, particularly in non-equilibrium settings. Recent advancements have proposed a phenomenological ansatz based on spontaneous symmetry breaking (SSB) to unify the description of charge transport in open quantum systems. However, it remains unclear under which conditions it fails to capture the emergent hydrodynamics and if it does break down, whether nontrivial dynamics emerge. In this work we show that Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) dynamics in an open integrable model (the B3 model), rather than diffusion from SSB, emerges. We find that the B3 model is equivalent to two interacting asymmetric XXZ spin chains and the ansatz can only capture the influence of the inter-chain interactions. When the initial state is appropriate, the asymmetric XXZ structure dominates the dynamics, which gives KPZ scaling behavior even when the hopping rate becomes negative. Our work motivates theory of charge transport in open systems beyond the ansatz based on SSB.

</details>


### [174] [Recovery Algorithm for Correlated Errors in Permutation-Invariant Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02346)
*Omprakash Chandra,Yingkai Ouyang,Gopikrishnan Muraleedharan,Gavin Brennen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子纠错恢复(QER)方法，利用误差信道知识寻找最优恢复映射，用于置换不变(PI)码。针对集体和局部对称振幅阻尼噪声，提出了新的CAD码族，其中CAD9码的纠错性能比现有码提升一个数量级以上，CAD4码能完美纠正1个全局对称AD错误，且恢复电路仅需10个门，可通过线性几何相位门实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子纠错码（如稳定子码）在非Pauli噪声（如振幅阻尼噪声）下需要额外开销，且恢复方案依赖噪声参数独立假设，保真度受限。本文旨在利用误差信道的先验知识设计最优恢复映射，同时降低电路复杂度和寻址需求。

Method: 利用量子电路实现通用的相干QER映射，作用于系统与辅助量子比特，恢复存储在PI码中的量子信息。PI码具有可调参数以适应噪声模型，且恢复电路简单、寻址需求低。针对集体和局部对称关联振幅阻尼(AD)噪声建模QER，并提出新的CAD码族，给出了4量子比特和9量子比特的显式示例。

Result: CAD9码在9量子比特上纠错性能比现有码提升一个数量级以上；CAD4码能完美纠正1个全局对称AD错误，其恢复电路仅包含10个系统门及系统-辅助门，可由线性几何相位门实现。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了从优化恢复映射到实验可实现的低开销协议的直接路径，适用于非Pauli噪声环境下的量子信息恢复。

Abstract: Quantum Error Recovery (QER) uses knowledge of the error channel acting on a quantum system to find optimal recovery maps. The scheme restores the uncorrupted state with a fidelity exceeding that achieved by noise parameter independent quantum error correction. We use a generic coherent QER map implemented with a quantum circuit acting on the system together with ancillary qubits to recover quantum information stored in permutation invariant (PI) codes. PI codes admit tunable parameters to suit the noise model and benefit from simple recovery operation circuits with reduced addressability requirements, unlike stabilizer codes. We showcase the method by modeling QER in PI codes after collective and local symmetric correlated amplitude-damping (AD) noise, a non-Pauli noise process for which stabilizer codes often require additional overhead. We also propose a new PI code family called CAD codes with explicit examples on 4 and 9 qubits for global symmetric AD errors. We show that CAD9 (supported on 9 qubits) code beats many existing codes by more than one order of magnitude. For the CAD4 code, which perfectly corrects 1 global symmetric AD error, the compiled recovery circuit consists of 10 system and system-ancilla gates which can be realized from linear geometric phase gates. Our work provides a direct path from optimized recovery maps to experimentally implementable, low-overhead protocols.

</details>


### [175] [Stable Self-Modulating Quantum Fast-Weight Programmers with Bounded Memory Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02363)
*Kuo-Chung Peng,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chun-Hua Lin,Yifeng Peng,Junghoon Justin Park,Huan-Hsin Tseng,Hsin-Yi Lin,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Chen-Yu Liu,Shinjae Yoo,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出有界旧态调制规则（对循环记忆分支应用保号tanh门），解决自调制量子快速权重编程器中旧态乘子发散的问题，在量子动力学和电信预测任务上验证了该策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自调制量子快速权重编程器（Self-Modulating QFWP）使用输入依赖门控来更新快速权重，但其无界旧态乘子在长序列场景下会发散，导致训练不稳定。作者旨在提出一种稳定的有界旧态调制策略，既保留记忆调制的关键机制，又消除发散问题。

Method: 提出有界旧态调制规则：仅对循环记忆分支应用保号tanh门，将旧态乘子限制在(-1,1)范围内，同时保持加法更新和新更新调制不变。与标准QFWP、完整Self-Modulating QFWP、Only-New和Only-Old变体进行对比实验。

Result: 在CUDA-Q量子动力学预测任务上，旧态调制是相对标准QFWP最一致的改进来源；有界旧态门消除了长序列发散并提升了整体鲁棒性。在米兰SMS预测任务上，原始无界Self-Modulating QFWP在长输入窗口下表现最佳，行为接近Only-Old消融。

Conclusion: 累积记忆调制（即旧态门控）是Self-Modulating QFWP的核心改进机制，有界旧态门控是一种针对性的稳定化策略，能在保留性能提升的同时避免长序列发散问题。

Abstract: Quantum Fast-Weight Programmers (QFWPs) store temporal information in dynamically programmed variational-circuit parameters rather than in nonlinear recurrent hidden states, offering a practical route to quantum sequence modeling. Self-Modulating QFWP improves this framework by using input-dependent gates for both new fast-weight updates and the accumulated fast-weight state, but its unbounded old-state multiplier can diverge in long-sequence regimes. We propose a bounded old-state modulation rule that applies a sign-preserving tanh gate only to the recurrent memory branch while leaving the additive update and new-update modulation unchanged. We evaluate standard QFWP, full Self-Modulating QFWP, Only-New, and Only-Old variants on two CUDA-Q quantum-dynamics forecasting tasks and on Milan SMS telecommunication activity prediction. The quantum-dynamics results show that old-state modulation is the most consistent source of improvement over Standard QFWP, and that bounding the old-state gate removes long-sequence divergence while improving aggregate robustness. On Milan SMS forecasting, the original unbounded Self-Modulating QFWP converges across the tested grid and shows its clearest gains at longer input windows, with behavior close to the Only-Old ablation. These findings identify accumulated-memory modulation as the key mechanism of Self-Modulating QFWP and bounded old-state gating as a targeted stabilization strategy.

</details>


### [176] [Copying Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02408)
*Hans Maassen,Burkhard Kümmerer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子概率论框架下，即有限维C*-代数与单位完全正映射的范畴中，讨论并证明了量子信息的不可广播定理及其紧密相关的不可克隆定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者的目标是在更抽象的量子概率论（C*-代数范畴）框架下重新审视并证明不可广播定理和不可克隆定理，以揭示这些量子信息基本定理的数学本质和更广泛适用性。

Method: 在有限维C*-代数与单位完全正映射构成的范畴中，形式化地定义并推导不可广播定理和不可克隆定理的成立条件。

Result: 在量子概率论框架下，不可广播定理成立：一组量子态允许共同广播操作当且仅当其密度矩阵属于交换族。不可克隆定理作为其特例也被证明。

Conclusion: 不可广播和不可克隆定理在C*-代数范畴中仍然成立，这体现了这些定理在量子信息理论中的基础性和普适性。

Abstract: The no-broadcasting theorem in quantum information says that a set of states on a quantum system admits a common broadcasting (copying) operation if and only if their density matrices belong to a commuting family. We discuss and prove this theorem, as well as the closely related no-cloning theorem in the context of quantum probability theory, i.e. in the category of (finite dimensional) C-star-algebras with unital completely positive maps.

</details>


### [177] [Optimal stellar rank approximation of squeezed cat states with photon catalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02427)
*Julian K. Nauth,Nathan Walk,Ananga M. Datta,Kurt Busch,Jens Eisert,Oliver Benson,Roger A. Kögler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了光子催化在低阶Fock态与压缩态之间生成压缩相干态叠加的非高斯量子态协议，利用星秩形式化框架表征资源复杂度，在保真度、成功概率和实验损耗之间进行权衡，证明了某些参数区域下的协议最优性，并为近时光子实现提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯量子态和操作是玻色子平台实现量子计算优势与量子纠错的关键资源，然而光学系统中的非高斯态产生在实验上仍具挑战性，通常依赖概率性播报协议。本文旨在系统分析光子催化协议生成压缩相干态叠加的适用性，并量化资源复杂度、保真度与损耗之间的权衡关系。

Method: 采用星秩形式化（stellar rank formalism）表征输入资源（包括态和测量）及生成态的非高斯复杂度；比较催化输出态与目标态之间的保真度，与相同非高斯输入资源下任何协议所能达到的最大保真度进行对比；利用Fock基下的Hilbert空间截断方法模拟所有光学模式的损耗；将光子催化与高斯玻色采样启发的协议在成功概率和态质量上进行基准比较。

Result: 识别出催化协议在某些参数实例下可达到最优性；确定了以最小资源实现高保真近似目标态的参数区域；证明了确定性Fock态源相较于高斯玻色采样协议的优势；揭示了所生成态在实验损耗下的鲁棒性；量化了非高斯资源复杂度、可达保真度与损耗之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 光子催化协议在生成压缩相干态叠加方面具有实际可行性，并在特定参数下可达到理论最优；研究结果为近时光学量子信息处理提供了实用设计指南。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian quantum states and operations constitute essential resources for achieving quantum computational advantage and enabling quantum error correction in bosonic platforms. However, their generation in optical settings remains a challenging experimental task, often relying on probabilistic heralded protocols. Here, we present an in-depth analysis of the suitability of photon catalysis between low number Fock states and squeezed states for the generation of squeezed coherent state superpositions. We employ the stellar rank formalism to characterize the non-Gaussian complexity of input resources (including both states and measurements) and the generated states. This enables a systematic comparison of the fidelity between the catalyzed output and the target states to the maximum fidelity achievable by any protocol with the same non-Gaussian input resources. In this sense, we identify instances where the catalysis protocols considered here are provably optimal. We identify parameter regimes in which high-fidelity approximations of the target states can be achieved with minimal resources. Furthermore, we benchmark the performance of photon catalysis against Gaussian boson sampling-inspired protocols in terms of success probability and state quality, highlighting the advantages of deterministic Fock state sources. We also investigate the generation of related non-Gaussian resources including squeezed Fock states relevant for quantum error correction. To account for experimental imperfections, we model losses across all optical modes using a Hilbert space truncation approach in the Fock basis and analyze the robustness of the generated states under realistic conditions. Our results quantify the trade-offs between non-Gaussian resource complexity, achievable fidelity, and losses in photon catalysis protocols, providing practical guidelines for near-term photonic implementations.

</details>


### [178] [Optimal Stabilizer Testing and Learning with Limited Quantum Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02444)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Louis Schatzki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限量子记忆下的稳定子态测试与学习问题，发现记忆约束会消除传统上稳定子态测试（常数样本）与学习（Θ(n)样本）之间的复杂度分离。具体结论：(1) 在k量子比特记忆下，测试复杂度为Θ(n-k)；(2) 非自适应学习复杂度为Θ(n²/k)；(3) 纯度测试存在指数下界。核心结论是：即使保持0.99n量子比特的记忆，稳定子态测试也无法常数样本完成；当记忆为cn（0<c<1）时，测试与学习一样困难，均需要Θ(n)样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究相干量子记忆资源如何影响稳定子态测试与学习之间的样本复杂度分离现象，揭示记忆约束下测试与学习复杂度的变化规律。

Method: (1) 将稳定子态测试与隐藏移位问题建立新联系，给出上界；(2) 利用随机正交群的组合学方法对似然比进行平均情形下界分析，证明测试复杂度下界；(3) 通过非自适应框架分析学习复杂度；(4) 扩展技术证明纯度测试的指数下界。

Result: (1) 测试复杂度：Θ(n-k)；(2) 非自适应学习复杂度：Θ(n²/k)；(3) 纯度测试：指数下界；(4) 记忆充足（k=0.99n）时无常数样本测试器；记忆为cn时测试与学习等价，均需Θ(n)样本。

Conclusion: 相干量子记忆是维持稳定子态测试与学习之间复杂度分离的关键资源。有限记忆下，测试与学习之间的分离消失，二者趋于同等困难。

Abstract: We study stabilizer state testing and learning with limited coherent quantum memory. Here an algorithm sequentially receives copies of an unknown $n$-qubit state, but may keep only $k$ qubits of coherent quantum memory between measurements. With unrestricted memory, seminal work of Gross, Nezami and Walter showed how to test $n$-qubit stabilizer states using $6$ copies, which is dimension independent, unlike the learning complexity of $Θ(n)$. We show that this testing-vs-learning separation is lost under memory constraints. More concretely we show that
  (1) The sample complexity of testing stabilizer states in the $k$-qubit memory framework is $Θ(n-k)$. Our upper bound goes via a novel connection to the hidden shift problem and the lower bound is proven using a novel approach to average case bounds on likelihood ratios via combinatorics of the stochastic orthogonal group.
  (2) The sample complexity of learning stabilizer states with $k$ qubits of memory, in the non-adaptive framework, is $Θ(n^2/k)$.
  As a further application of our techniques, we prove an exponential lower bound for purity testing even when the memory may be left coherent throughout the protocol. Our main results identify coherent quantum memory as the resource enabling the usual separation between stabilizer testing and learning. In particular, even with $k=0.99n$ qubits of memory, there is no constant-copy stabilizer tester; furthermore for $k=cn$ qubits of memory (for $0< c < 1$), stabilizer testing is as hard as learning, with both requiring $Θ(n)$ copies.

</details>


### [179] [A Quantum-Walk Representation of Color-Ordered MHV Scattering Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02456)
*Anirudh Verma,C. M. Chandrashekar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于图论的框架，利用置换树上的量子游走来表示量子色动力学中颜色排序的MHV散射振幅，并通过量子傅里叶变换在硬币空间上组合编码贡献，为量子算法模拟量子场论散射过程提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子色动力学中颜色排序的MHV散射振幅提供一种图论框架，连接置换树、量子游走和开放量子系统，从而为量子算法模拟量子场论散射过程奠定基础。

Method: 采用置换树上的量子游走，每个根到叶路径对应外部胶子的不同颜色排序；根据Parke-Taylor振幅的旋量-积结构分配局部转移振幅；引入基于Kraus算子的量子信道描述扇区分辨贡献；使用加权收集算子在公共参考节点上相干组合终端扇区；通过硬币空间上的量子傅里叶变换组合编码贡献得到颜色分解振幅。

Result: 低点胶子振幅的数值结果表明，所提出的表示方法能够忠实捕捉Parke-Taylor特征结构，并与解析结果一致。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将置换树、量子游走和开放量子系统统一起来，为量子算法模拟量子场论中的散射过程提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce a graph-theoretic framework for representing color-ordered maximally helicity violating (MHV) scattering amplitudes in quantum chromodynamics using coined quantum walks on permutation trees. Each root-to-terminal path corresponds to a distinct color ordering of the external gluons, while local transition amplitudes are assigned according to the spinor-product structure of the Parke--Taylor amplitudes. The walk evolves in coherent superpositions over permutation sectors, giving a dynamical picture of the underlying combinatorics. A quantum-channel formulation based on Kraus operators is also introduced to describe sector-resolved contributions, while a weighted collection operator coherently combines the terminal sectors at a common reference node. A quantum Fourier transform on the coin space is then employed to combine the encoded contributions into the corresponding color-decomposed amplitude. Together, these constructions establish a unified graph-based framework connecting permutation trees, quantum walks, and open quantum systems providing a framework for quantum algorithms to simulate scattering processes in quantum field theory. As an example, numerical results for low-point gluon amplitudes demonstrate that the proposed representation faithfully captures the characteristic Parke--Taylor structure and is consistent with analytical results.

</details>


### [180] [Quantum mutual information as a robust probe of integrability in open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02462)
*Nirupam Sen,Keshav Das Agarwal,Aditi Sen De*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Haar平均总关联和(aSTC)及平均真正多体纠缠作为量子信息 scrambling 的鲁棒探针，在长程量子XYZ自旋模型中证明aSTC的长时间平均和瞬时涨落能忠实区分可积与混沌动力学，且在非马尔可夫环境下仍保持区分能力，甚至在弱马尔可夫区域比OTOC更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找比传统OTOC更鲁棒、系统尺寸无关且能区分可积与混沌动力学的量子信息scrambling探针，特别关注开放量子系统中Markovian和非Markovian环境的影响。

Method: 基于Haar平均总关联和(aSTC)与平均真正多体纠缠的信息论框架，在长程量子XYZ自旋模型（可积极限为最近邻横向XY模型）中分析，并引入热库耦合（Markovian和非Markovian）研究开放系统动力学。

Result: aSTC的长时间平均和瞬时涨落能忠实区分可积与混沌动力学且与系统尺寸无关；非Markovian环境下信息回流恢复scrambling动力学使aSTC保持区分能力；在弱Markovian振幅阻尼和非Markovian退相位噪声下，aSTC的涨落能区分可积与非可积性，而OTOC失效。

Conclusion: aSTC是一种比OTOC更鲁棒的量子scrambling探针，尤其在开放量子系统的弱马尔可夫区域具有独特优势，为量子混沌和scrambling研究提供了新的信息论工具。

Abstract: The dynamics of a quantum system encode signatures of whether the underlying Hamiltonian is integrable or chaotic, giving rise to the concept of quantum information scrambling through the properties of the resulting dynamical states or operators. We introduce an information-theoretic framework based on the Haar-averaged sum of total correlations (aSTC), together with average genuine multipartite entanglement generated dynamically from initially fully separable states, as robust probes of quantum information scrambling. Using the long-range quantum XYZ spin model in transverse and longitudinal magnetic fields, whose integrable limit is the nearest-neighbor transverse XY model, we demonstrate that the long-time average and, more importantly, the temporal fluctuations of the aSTC provide a faithful and system-size-independent signature of integrable and chaotic dynamics, similar to the conventional measure of scrambling, out-of-time-ordered correlator (OTOC). When the system is in contact with the thermal reservoir and system-bath coupling follows Markovianity, we find that the fluctuations of the aSTC and OTOC continue to distinguish integrable and chaotic dynamics only at intermediate times. However, we observe that in the non-Markovian domain, information backflow restores the scrambling dynamics, enabling the aSTC to retain its distinguishing power even at long times. Interestingly, we exhibit that, under Markovian amplitude damping and non-Markovian dephasing noise, the temporal fluctuations of the aSTC can discriminate between integrability and non-integrability in the weak Markovian regime, even when OTOC fails to do so.

</details>


### [181] [Topological Control of Quantum Chaos Diagnostics: OTOCs, Spectral Statistics, and Information Scrambling in Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02463)
*Reza Pirmoradian,Soheir Rouhani,M. Reza Tanhayi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过图论方法研究Ising自旋网络中的可积-混沌转变与信息置乱，证明长程耦合和异质度分布加速量子信息传播，并建立了网络拓扑与信息论、算符和谱诊断的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究网络拓扑结构（路径图、Erdős–Rényi、Watts–Strogatz）对量子多体系统中可积性破缺、信息置乱和热化动力学的影响，建立图论与量子混沌之间的桥梁。

Method: 将自旋建模为顶点，利用邻接矩阵描述不同拓扑上的相互作用；哈密顿量包含局域和非局域非局域化项；通过双体互信息和三体信息量化置乱；使用OTOC提取量子Lyapunov指数；采用Krylov复杂度分析算符增长；利用能级间距分布（Poisson→Wigner–Dyson）和谱形状因子（SFF）诊断谱统计特征。

Result: 非局域耦合增强和场异质性驱动可积性破缺；三体信息在强混沌区呈现大幅负值；OTOC早期指数增长，量子Lyapunov指数随混沌参数系统变化；Krylov复杂度在混沌相中快速增长；能级统计从Poisson转变为Wigner–Dyson；SFF呈现斜率-下降-平台-高原结构；Thouless时间缩短与信息置乱加速强相关。

Conclusion: 建立了统一的理论框架，将网络拓扑与信息置乱、算符动力学和谱统计相联系，为理解量子多体系统的热化与非平衡动力学提供了深刻见解。

Abstract: We investigate the integrability-to-chaos transition and information scrambling in Ising spin networks via a graph-theoretic formulation. Modeling spins as vertices and interactions via adjacency matrices across path, Erdős--Rényi, and Watts--Strogatz topologies, we demonstrate that long-range couplings and heterogeneous degree distributions drastically accelerate quantum information propagation. The Hamiltonian comprises local and normalized non-local interactions; tuning the non-local coupling and field heterogeneity drives integrability breaking. To quantify scrambling, we employ bipartite mutual and tripartite information. Increasing non-local interactions drives tripartite information to large negative values, signaling deep information scrambling. Out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) exhibit exponential early-time growth, yielding quantum Lyapunov exponents that scale systematically with parameters governing the chaotic regime. Complementing this, Krylov complexity reveals rapid operator growth in the chaotic phase, synchronizing with OTOC and mutual information dynamics. Spectrally, the transition manifests as a shift from Poissonian to Wigner--Dyson level spacing statistics. The spectral form factor (SFF) exhibits the characteristic slope-dip-ramp-plateau structure, enabling the extraction of Thouless and Heisenberg times. Crucially, a reduced Thouless time strongly correlates with accelerated informational and operator scrambling. Ultimately, this work establishes a unified framework bridging network topology with information-theoretic, operator, and spectral diagnostics, offering profound insights into thermalization and non-equilibrium dynamics in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [182] [Symmetries of Pauli Noise from Lindbladian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02481)
*Moein Malekakhlagh,Edward H. Chen,Luke C. G. Govia,Alireza Seif*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现量子电路噪声中的物理结构会引入近似对称性约束，使得Pa利保真度与其门共轭值之间存在对称关系。利用这种对称性，仅凭误差类型而非幅度即可固定规范，从而系统识别SPAM误差。研究表明相干误差不会引起一阶不对称，而特定非对角耗散误差会破坏对称性，常见单量子比特噪声源仅引起二阶不对称。实验在IBM Kingston上验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路噪声表征受限于规范自由度，某些参数（如SPAM误差的个体贡献）原则上无法从门集内的任何实验中学到。现有方法无法有效区分这些不可学习参数，导致误差表征和缓解困难。

Method: 利用Lindbladian微扰理论分析Clifford门（包括ZZ_{π/2}、CZ、CNOT、iSWAP、SWAP）的噪声结构，推导出Pauli保真度与其门共轭之间的对称关系，并给出对称性破缺的选择定则，从而通过固定规范来分离SPAM误差。

Result: 相干误差不会引起一阶不对称；仅特定非对角耗散误差可在第一阶破坏对称性；T1弛豫和T2φ纯退相等常见噪声源仅引起二阶不对称。数值和IBM Kingston实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用噪声物理结构引入的近似对称性可有效固定规范，实现SPAM误差的系统识别，简化量子误差表征与缓解流程。

Abstract: Characterizing noise in quantum circuits is fundamentally limited by gauge degrees of freedom; certain parameters, such as the individual contributions of state preparation and measurement (SPAM) errors, are in principle unlearnable from any experiment within the gate set. Here, we show that the physical structure of realistic noise processes imposes approximate symmetry constraints on the Pauli fidelities of gate noise channels. These symmetries relate the fidelity of a Pauli $P$ and its gate-conjugate $U_g P U_g ^{\dagger}$, and can be used to fix the gauge using only knowledge of the error type and not its magnitude. Using Lindbladian perturbation theory, we analyze a broad class of Clifford gates, including $ZZ_{π/2}$, CZ, CNOT, iSWAP, and SWAP, and demonstrate that coherent errors do not induce first-order asymmetry, while only a restricted set of predominantly off-diagonal dissipative errors can break the symmetry at first order, for which we derive simple selection rules. Notably, common single-qubit noise sources such as $T_1$-relaxation and $T_{2φ}$-pure-dephasing can only cause asymmetry at second order. Leveraging these symmetries to fix the gauge enables systematic identification of SPAM errors, simplifying error characterization and mitigation. We validate our results numerically and experimentally on IBM Kingston.

</details>


### [183] [Automated logical Clifford gadgets for heterogeneous architectures via chain maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02482)
*Asmae Benhemou,Noah Berthusen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化框架，利用链映射在任意CSS码对之间合成逻辑CNOT电路，能够搜索浅层稀疏的物理电路实现，并扩展到逻辑CZ门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同CSS码之间的逻辑CNOT操作选择非常有限，通常仅适用于结构相关的码族，缺乏通用的自动化方法来合成跨码逻辑CNOT电路。

Method: 提出基于链映射的自动化框架：给定两个CSS码和所需的二分逻辑CNOT网络，先构造实现所需逻辑作用的链映射的仿射空间，然后在该空间中搜索浅层且稀疏的物理电路候选方案。

Result: 在多种异构CSS码对上进行了基准测试，恢复了已知的横向构造，并发现了新的低深度解，包括距离保持和部分距离保持的例子（可通过额外flag测量提升至全码距离）。

Conclusion: 该框架可应用于码切换、魔法态注入、泡利积测量和级联码操作，为异构架构中的逻辑接口提供了有利的时空权衡，并可直接扩展到目标逻辑CZ门。

Abstract: Transversal CNOTs are ubiquitous for entangling logical qubits of identical CSS codes pairwise. For distinct codes, the options are much more limited, and are typically known only for structurally related code families. We introduce an automated framework for synthesising inter-code logical CNOT circuits between arbitrary CSS codes using chain maps. Given a prescribed bipartite logical CNOT network between these codes, our method constructs the affine space of chain maps realising the desired logical action, and then searches this space for shallow and sparse physical circuit candidates. We benchmark this method on a range of heterogeneous CSS code pairs, recovering known transversal constructions, and finding new low-depth solutions, including distance-preserving and partially distance-preserving examples, which we demonstrate can be promoted to the full code distance using additional flag measurements. We discuss applications to code switching, magic-state injection, Pauli product measurements, and operations on concatenated codes, where bespoke chain maps offer favourable spacetime tradeoffs for logical interfaces tailored to heterogeneous architectures. Finally, we show how our framework straightforwardly extends to targeted logical CZ gates.

</details>
